the segment won crediting (15 + 25) × 1.10 = 44 to RORY with one `xp` event and `seg_xp` back to 0; a segment lost crediting the events alone, 1); SCR-01 won three times paying 17 XP and +2 Prestige each (tier 1), the first toast "SCRAP: 17 XP, +2 Prestige", the third ending ", PI tick vs Kowalchuk" and the second not, three `reward_toast {size: scrap, won}` events; a scrap lost paying 6 and no Prestige; SCR-23 (tier 3) paying +3 at the cap; a spar fought to its end 44 XP and 3 Spirits with the toast "SPAR: 44 XP, 3 Spirits, PI tick vs Kowalchuk", won 66 XP, 5 Spirits, 10 HBP, the tell-highlight lab 22; six stablemate spars with PRIYA bonding 3 / 6 / 9 / 12 / 15 / 15 (the first toast carrying ", RORY+PRIYA +3", Gymmates raised at 10) and 18 after `new_day()`; three segments won then the verdict — 28 per segment and 110 at the close to each of RORY, PRIYA and MARCUS, JUNE on the bench 228 (50 % of the dual's 414, houseless), 8 Spirits, 50 HBP, RORY+PRIYA and PRIYA+MARCUS +8 each and RORY+MARCUS nothing, one dual toast beginning "DUAL: 414 XP, 8 Spirits, 50 HBP, RORY+PRIYA +8, PRIYA+MARCUS +8"; a dual lost paying 44 at the close, 5 Spirits, no HBP, the pairs +5; RORY & PRIYA at 25 → Corner Voices with RORY's PWR 45 → 45.9 and the threshold 25, at 50 → Adjacent Trust at +5 % and the threshold 20, RORY & MARCUS at 50 not adjacent (R1 / R3) → +2 % and 25, the six thresholds → levels 0–5, an ALLIED pair (Kowalchuk & Whitecloud) 6 for 5 where an unallied one takes 5; RORY carried from 4,780 past 4,800 → level 10 with one `level_up`; the ledger kept through `end_bout` and `new_dual`; two sims equal in hash with the same seeded bond and different from one without. `-- determinism` adds the reward ledger to the fully delegated dual: 380 XP paid across four fighters, 5 Spirits, two Kizuna pairs and the toast "DUAL: 233 XP, 5 Spirits, RORY+PRIYA +5, PRIYA+JUNE +5", identical between its two runs.
**Result.** GREEN on all 24 bosses (248/248 each, 5,952 assertions); determinism 22/22; geometry 5/5; margins GREEN; sim_test 14/14; 16,000-frame soaks on the profile of Ep1's SCRAP 1 three times over (two wins paying "SCRAP: 17 XP, +2 Prestige" each and a loss paying 6: 40 XP and 4 Prestige on the ledger), the Priya trial twice over (her card now marked `stablemate`: "SPAR: 33 XP, 5 Spirits, RORY+PRIYA +3, PI tick vs Cassidy Vane" — the lab's half, the win's Spirits and HBP, the bond at 6; since C24 her card is a generic of her own dialect and the tick names Priya), a seeded RORY + PRIYA bond at Adjacent Trust (three `kizuna_effect` events, RORY 76 XP after R1), and the sanctioned format through three segments with the ledger printed (RORY 52 XP, JUNE 8), zero errors. What the gates caught: the event payload key `kind` collided with the event's own key a fifth time (`reward_toast`; renamed `size`); the dual's toast was written to the feed after the verdict and overwrote the "DUAL OVER" line older gates read (it now precedes the verdict); a drop-XP test placed at 1.4 m whiffed the uppercut and measured nothing — a gate correcting a test.

## C23 — The save is the ledgers written down, and a fight in flight restores to the same hash — PROVEN
**Claim.** A SaveGame (NP_11 §9) is written from the sim's own ledgers and nothing else: the stable's cards as dressed with level, xp and bonds read from the reward ledger, the currencies, the Scout Book (the PI ledger as `pi_map`, the pages earned), the rematch remixes set, the ledger's day, the format and the boss; the overworld's fields (story flags, staff, the walls, the codex, the hybrid registry) are carried under the schema's names and written by nobody yet (the hybrid registry is the Forge's since C41). A save read and built into a new sim writes itself back byte-equal, its ledgers equal with their ints, its remixes live from the bell. A fight in flight rides beneath it as a DualState: the schema's fields readable, and beneath them every state variable of the sim, so a sim built on another boss, seed, roster and format and restored to it lands on the same state hash and continues a scripted log to the hashes of the unbroken run. The content tables are not state: a snapshot carries a stamp of the data it was made under and restores only under the same data; another schema, another build of the sim, other content or a blob that is not a state are refused, each naming why, and leave the sim untouched.
**Mechanism.** `sim/save.gd` (`TNBSave`, static): `to_save(sim)` builds the record from `dressed`, `rewards`, `pi`, `remix_for`, `fmt` and `_first_boss` — `fighter_record` reads level (`level_of`), xp (`xp_of`) and bonds (the pair keys of `rewards.kizuna` holding the name, with `kizuna_level`) per card; pages come from `scout_page` for every boss and remix the PI ledger marks beaten, in sorted order so the text is stable — and `write_text` serializes it with sorted keys and full precision; `read` refuses any schema but `SaveGame/NP_11-s9/v1.0` by name, `_intify` returns every whole float JSON hands back to the int it was, `card_of` takes the sim's card keys back out of a Fighter record; `build(content, rd, seed)` runs `setup` with the ledgers and the remix ledger (a new last parameter, applied before the first `_load_segment`) and the cards dressed, then `restore`s the DualState if one rides beneath. In `core.gd`, `state_vars()` is every script variable less `SNAP_SKIP` (the content tables and the RNG object); `snapshot()` reads them all with `get`, adds the RNG's seed and state, and encodes the lot with `var_to_bytes` (ints stay ints, floats keep every bit, Vector2s survive — `var_to_str` does not: it printed 0.1 + 0.2 as 0.3), base64 under the readable `dual_state()` fields with the schema, the content stamp (`hash(var_to_bytes(the tables))`), the variables' signature and the hash at the moment; `restore()` checks the schema, the stamp and the signature, decodes, `set`s every variable and the RNG, and reports whether the hash it lands on is the snapshot's. `new_day()` now counts `rewards.day` (the save's calendar). The greybox writes `user://tnb_save.json` on Shift+F5 and loads it on Shift+F6 or with `-- load=<path>` at the bell; `-- demo saveload` saves at tick 3,000 and loads back at 3,600.
**Why the blob and not the schema's fields.** The DualState NP_11 §9 names is the overworld's view of a dual — twelve fields. The sim that plays it has 181 state variables, and a resume that dropped any of them (the boss's queue, the tracker's tokens, the RNG's state, a cooldown) would land on a different hash and diverge from the fight the player left. So the schema's fields are written readable, for the overworld and the HUD, and the exact state rides beneath them; the content is stamped rather than carried because it is the data's, not the save's.
**Test.** `-- smoke` PAC-069 stage: 8 assertions — the record from a seeded sim (PI 2 vs Kowalchuk, Yuri beaten, RORY + PRIYA at 25, RORY at 4,808 XP, 5 / 4 / 20 currencies, Yuri's rematch set): every schema key, RORY level 10 at 4,808 with bonds `[PRIYA 25 Lv2]`, JUNE a reserve, the currencies with Beans 0, `pi_map` equal to the ledger with one page (Yuri's), the remix, format mini, boss 1, day 1, the sim's hash untouched by the writing; the round trip (4,864 bytes) read and built writing back byte-equal, the ledgers equal with their ints, the four cards dressed, Yuri's rematch live from the bell, no dual beneath a save written between fights, a ledger-less sim differing; the ledgers through three scraps won, a stablemate spar, R1 won on EP and a new day (PI 2, Yuri's page, 145 XP, the bond at 3, 6 Prestige, day 2 with the daily cap cleared); a snapshot at tick 3,000 of a scripted log (60 KB) restored into a sim built on another boss, seed, the 8-card roster and the sanctioned format landing on the same hash with the saved sim untouched, both run to 5,000 with every one of 20 hashes the unbroken run's and the RNG states equal; the readable fields (segment 1, clock 66.0 s, the score, tension, relay, lineups sealed / revealed, the corner budget, cooldowns, supers, zones, the adaptation window and flags, crowd heat, condition) equal between the sim they were read from and the sim restored; the refusals — a SaveGame at v0.9 named against v1.0, a DualState under other content, from another build, with a blob that is not a state, at v0.1 — each with its reason and the sim's hash kept; the content stamp unchanged by 5,000 ticks and equal for every sim loaded from the data; a fight in flight at tick 2,500 written to `user://` (60 KB), read back, built and restored to its hash, run 500 more ticks in step with the sim that wrote it, the seeded bond beneath it kept, a missing file refused. `-- determinism` adds the mini log broken at tick 12,000 by a save to text (188 KB: the SaveGame, the DualState with 12,000 ticks of events beneath it) and a load into a sim seeded otherwise: the 200 remaining hashes the unbroken run's, the final hash equal.
**Result.** GREEN on all 24 bosses (256/256 each, 6,144 assertions); determinism 23/23; geometry 5/5; margins GREEN; sim_test 14/14; 16,000-frame soaks: `-- demo boss=1 saveload` saved at tick 3,000 (27 KB on disk), ran on to 3,600, loaded back to tick 3,000 on the same hash and finished its frames; `-- demo load=user://tnb_save.json` resumed that file at the bell (R1 with 71.7 s on the clock) and a missing file was reported; zero errors. What the probe settled before the code was written: `var_to_str` rounds floats (0.3 for 0.1 + 0.2) and JSON returns every number as a float, so the blob is `var_to_bytes` and the ledgers are re-typed on the way in.

## C24 — A non-named opponent is assembled from pieces into the same shape the sim executes, and named strings never appear in one — PROVEN
**Claim.** The generic opponent assembler (NP_04 §9) builds a scrap's card, a spar's mockup or a dual's filler from a pattern-piece library tagged Style × House-dialect × tier — 63 pieces at this draft: 36 strings (three per Style per tier), 12 punish hooks (three per Style), 15 tell dialects (every roster House but the sealed one, the school's, the gym's for a no-name) — by the section's rules: 2–3 strings (two at tier 1, three above; an opener first; the Style's SPECIAL last at tier 3 and never lower), one PUNISH, one tell channel per dialect with the dialect's motifs, SELECT weights from the Style's preset; the interrupt block by tier; tells shifted into the tier's loudness band (NP_04 §3) and floored by class (§1); the difficulty knob as piece tier + tell tier + a stat block by tier. The result is the compiled pattern shape the sim already runs, executed whole (`pattern_coverage()` carries nothing), and deterministic from its spec, so a card is the same opponent in every sim. No piece shares a label or an action sequence with any of the 24 bosses' 79 strings. Ep1's four cards fight on strings of their own; a filler takes a named boss's slot in a dual, ticks its dialect, pays, and earns no page.
**Mechanism.** `data/pattern_pieces.v1.json` holds the rules (strings by tier, one punish, the SPECIAL's tier, the interrupt rule by tier, the three bands and the class floors, the stat blocks), the Style presets (weights for two and for three strings), the dialects (channel, House, element, motifs by class), the string pieces (id, Style, tier, role, label, class, a Standard-band tell, actions) and the hooks (Style, tier, trigger, a response with `{lead}` / `{second}` / `{third}`). `sim/assembler.gd` (`TNBAssembler`, static, preloads nothing): `assemble(pieces, spec)` seeds a `RandomNumberGenerator` from the spec, draws an opener from the Style's openers at or under the tier, fills from the rest of the pool, appends the SPECIAL at tier 3, dresses each string in a motif of the dialect's class list (no motif twice while one is fresh) on the dialect's channel at `tell_ms(rules, tier, class, base)` (the base shifted into the band, then the floor), writes the preset weights, the interrupt block by rule, one hook with `resolve()`, and a META of Style, element, House, slot, `generic`, tier, dialect, seed, pieces, label. In `core.gd`, `spec_from_card(card)` reads a card's Style, element, House, its dialect (named, else its House's, else its own name), tier, a seed from its name unless it names one, its stats or the tier's block; `generic_spec(name, style, dialect, tier, seed, label)` is a filler's; `register_generic` keeps the spec in `generics` and the pattern in `gpatterns` (state, never content: `_pat(name)` reads it before `patterns`, the content stamp never sees it); `set_generic_slot(i, spec)` is applied by `_build_enemy_lineup` in the named boss's place in `blineup` and `bdressed`; `_load_segment` assembles a generic card's pattern before `_declare_bases` and dresses the boss from its META and the spec's stats, raises `generic` and describes the pieces on the feed, and applies no remix over a generic; `dialect_of` answers a generic card's or slot's dialect; `_pi_beaten` books no page for one; `objective_available` and `special_breakable` judge a generic card on its assembled pattern; `pattern_coverage` counts `generic`, `tier`, `dialect` as executed META and `seed`, `pieces`, `label` as presentational. The state hash carries the generic's name and seed and the slots. Ep1's cards in `bouts.v1.json` carry `generic: true` and a tier.
**Why the pattern shape and not a second agent.** Every rule the sim already executes — the interrupt block, the punish grammar, the tell floors, the SELECT, PI, the rewards — reads a pattern; an assembled opponent that is a pattern gets all of it for free and is judged by the same coverage function as a named boss. The seed from the card's name is what makes a scrap opponent a character rather than a roll: the CHALLENGER at the bike racks has the same two strings every time you fight him.
**Test.** `-- smoke` PAC-070 stage: 8 assertions — the library (63 pieces; 3 strings per Style per tier; every action verb parsing; every hook's trigger keying a sensor; every dialect with a channel, three motifs and a SPECIAL motif; every roster House but the sealed one covered; no label or action sequence shared with the 24); the rules over 48 assemblies (4 Styles × 3 tiers × 4 dialects: the counts, the opener first, the SPECIAL last at tier 3 and never lower, no piece over the tier, one resolved PUNISH with all 48 responses known, every tell on the channel with a listed motif, the preset weights, the interrupt block by tier, `interrupt_coverage` agreeing, nothing carried under `pattern_coverage` — 336 executed META keys, 0 carried, 0 idle); the same spec byte-equal twice, eight seeds eight distinct assemblies, a spec without a seed taking its name's hash and assembling the same in another sim; the knob (tier 1's tells 600–670, tier 3's normals 400 with the SPECIAL at 700; `tell_ms` 450 → 600 / 450 / 400 by tier and 700 held for a SPECIAL under Subtle; every tell of the 48 over its class floor; stat blocks 40 and 70); a spar against DOCKHAND, a tier-2 Slugger card of the Kowalchuk dialect (the spar opening on his three assembled strings, 13 of them told in 20 s, his hook `player_whiffed_body_shot → immediate S2` answering its trigger, his opener breaking to a jab, three scraps won paying 51 XP and ticking Kowalchuk to PI 1, SCR-09 refused against him); Ep1 by card (MARCUS on two Slugger strings — Lumber, Woodpile — none of Yuri's, his chin from his card, Yuri's slot borrowed; JUNE on two Swarmer strings on the gym's channel; the CHALLENGER on the school's motifs; PRIYA's trial on three Out-Boxer strings at tier 2; the card the same pattern in another sim); a filler in R1 (Dock Filler, a tier-2 Out-Boxer of the Tremblay dialect: FROST, House Tremblay, the matchup card read, Yuri's slot borrowed; R1 won ticking Tremblay to PI 2, booking no page, paying 28 XP; R2 a named boss; two sims with the slot hashing alike, a seed apart differing; the content stamp unchanged); a dual with a filler snapshotted at tick 600 and restored into another sim on the same hash with the assembled pattern in it, run 300 more ticks in step. `-- determinism` adds the delegated dual with a tier-3 Slugger filler in R1: 113 tells of his own strings (three of them the SPECIAL), 2,704 sensors, Kowalchuk ticked to PI 2, every hash identical twice and different from the named run.
**Result.** GREEN on all 24 bosses (264/264 each, 6,336 assertions); determinism 24/24; geometry 5/5; margins GREEN; sim_test 14/14; 16,000-frame soaks on the profile: a dual with a Slugger filler in R1 (Kowalchuk ticked, no page, on to R3), three spars against a tier-3 Out-Boxer of the Tremblay dialect (PI 1 then 2), Ep1's PRIYA TRIAL three times on her own strings ("SPAR: 33 XP, 5 Spirits, RORY+PRIYA +3, PI tick vs Priya") and SCRAP 2 five times on MARCUS's two ("PI tick vs Marcus" on the third win), zero errors. What the gates caught: the first filler gate counted matchup cards from the sim's own setup as well as the dual it started — a gate correcting a test; nothing in the sim.

## C25 — The IDLE lines are each boss's neutral game: how he closes, the range he keeps, his guard and his clocks — PROVEN
**Claim.** Every IDLE line of the 24 patterns (NP_04 §2's IDLE primitive; 52 lines, 41 verbs) is a behaviour the sim performs between strings, or a flag only the presentation reads, named as such. How he closes: each walk at its own pace straight at where the player is (Moreau and Remy 0.45 m/s, Moira 0.7, Sam 0.8, Reyes 1.0, the Echo 1.2), against the shared approach's 1.2 that leads the player's movement by 0.35 s (the Choir keeps it); Alfie crowds to 1.5 m, Deshawn to 1.3, Osei and Surety wait at 2.0. How he circles: at range, one way (Yuri 0.5 m/s) or the other (Carter 1.2), Marta 0.8 at 2.1 m stepping back out to it, Vane, the Cartographer and Sora reversing on 2.5 / 3 / 4 s clocks, Baro after every string. His rhythm: Ada steps only in the first 200 ms of every beat of the metronome her phase lines tighten, Chiamaka on both of her tempos; Tam comes in 35° off the line and switches sides every 1.5 s. His stance: Vey mirrors the player's lateral movement at range; Vasile holds 1.7–2.1 m both ways; Cal takes the centre and comes no closer than 2.2 m; Moira approaches from the centre side and cuts the ring. His guard when he has not read the punch: the authored rate by head and body (mid 0.35 / 0.35 — the old flat rate — high 0.5 / 0.15, tight 0.45, rough 0.25, perfect 0.5, textbook 0.4, Tam's low bait 0.1 / 0.6, Osei's shell 0.6 / 0.1, Sam's 0.8 while he walks in), halved when he is wobbled unless composed (Cal, Surety). His clocks: Yuri taunts every 8 s and Carter every 10 s — the guard open 500 ms, his read off, his next string held 700 ms — Vane's `no_taunt` keeps the clock off, and Tam's tempo flips every 6 s (his gap between strings ×1.4 then ×0.6, his walk ×0.7 then ×1.3). Remy's `unmasked` names his slot at the corner of a blind dual where every other slot shows '?'. Three lines are flags: Jean-Guy's silent feet (the Choir's too, beside its approach), Marta's feet quiet while she arms, Reyes' corner bell (two with a Reyes dressed). A frozen boss and a generic keep the shared approach and the 0.35 guard.
**Mechanism.** `data/neutral.v1.json` holds the default (the pre-C25 approach and guard), the tell range (1.9 m) and one record per verb by category — move (kind, approach speed, lead, range, and the kind's numbers: orbit speed and direction or reversal clock, the step window and beat source, the angle and its switch, the mirror factor, the hold band and retreat speed), guard (head, body, wobble multiplier, the advance rate), clock, bias, audio, scene, reveal. `_neutral_profile(pattern)` folds a pattern's IDLE lines into one profile at `_load_segment` (after a remix is applied); `idle_verb` / `idle_arg` read `verb@Ns`. `_neutral_move(dist)` replaces the shared approach in `step`: with the neutral game frozen or no move verb authored it is the old statement to the float; in a tell, a hold or a fake he closes to the tell range at his profile's pace; in IDLE / RECOVER the profile's kind runs — `advance`, `pulse` (`_on_beat()` over `bmods.metronome_ms` or `bmods.tempos`), `orbit` (`orbit_dir()`: fixed, on a clock of his neutral steps, or on `bstrings_done`'s parity; a radial pull to range, outward only for the verbs that retreat), `angle` (`angle_side()`), `mirror` (the player's velocity across the line), `hold`, `ground` (the centre, or the centre-side point at his range from the player) — with Moira's `cut_ring` targeting the centre-side point at range; every move is scaled by the wobble, `_speed_mult("B")` and Tam's move tempo. `_neutral_clock()` runs on his own steps (`bneutral_t`): the taunt when due and he is between strings (`guard_open_t`, `btaunt_t` — `read_prob` returns 0.05 while it runs — `btimer` held), Tam's flip (`btempo_fast`); `_idle_tempo()` scales the default RECOVER draw without changing the RNG draw count. `_idle_guard(body)` replaces the flat 0.35 in the unread branch of the player's punch startup (one `randf` either way). `_footwork_tick()` keeps `bfootwork_audio` and raises `footwork_audio` on a change; `_load_segment` raises `corner_bell`; `boss_slot_label(idx)` answers the corner's slot (the `corner_open` event and the greybox's corner line read it). `pattern_coverage()` classes an IDLE line by the table: executed, presentational (the three flags) or carried. `neutral_on` (the tests' `freeze_boss` turns it off), `bneutral_t`, `btaunts`, `btaunt_t`, `btempo_fast`, `bfootwork_audio` are in the state hash; `NEUTRAL` is content (in `SNAP_SKIP` and the content stamp).
**Why the neutral game is a profile and not strings.** The IDLE lines are not attacks and have no tells; they are what he does while there is no string, which the sim already did one way for everybody. Folding them into one profile per segment keeps the RNG stream where it was (no draw is added or removed: the guard reuses the one roll, the tempo scales the one recover draw, every clock and reversal counts his own steps), so a frozen boss is the pre-C25 boss exactly and every older gate that froze him measures what it measured.
**Test.** `-- smoke` PAC-071 stage: 8 assertions — the table (41 verbs: 23 moves, 10 guards, 3 clocks, 1 bias, 2 audio flags, 1 scene, 1 reveal) against the 24 bosses' 52 lines, every verb used known and every table verb used, `pattern_coverage()` at 49 executed / 3 presentational / 0 carried with nothing carried anywhere, every boss authoring a move verb and a generic none; how he closes from 3.4 m, measured over 0.5 s (Moreau and Remy 0.45, Moira 0.7, Sam 0.8, Reyes 1.00, Osei and Surety 0.70, Alfie 1.30, Deshawn 1.50, the Echo and the Choir 1.20, each within 0.02) and where he stops (Alfie 1.49, Deshawn 1.30, Osei and Surety 2.00, the rest 1.89–1.90), Moreau with his neutral game frozen and a generic card both at the shared 1.20, the Choir leading a strafing player (0.285 m sideways in 0.5 s) where the Echo does not (0.132); how he circles (Yuri −0.50 m/s and Carter +1.20, Marta 0.80 at 2.1 m, Vane ±0.50 across 2.5 s, the Cartographer ±0.60 across 3 s, Sora ±0.70 across 4 s, Baro ±0.60 across a string; Marta from 1.6 m back out to 2.07, Yuri staying at 1.60); the rhythms tick for tick (Ada 48 moving ticks of 192 on her 800 ms beat and 48 of 156 at 650 ms, Chiamaka 48 of 180 on 600 / 900, none off the beat; Tam 34.3° off the line, then −34.3° after 1.5 s, at 0.77 m/s on his slow tempo); the stances (Vasile from 1.4 m back to 1.71, from 2.6 m in to 2.09, standing at 1.85; Vey mirroring a strafing player 1.00 to 1; Cal standing on the centre with the player at 2.15 m, coming to 2.23 with the player at 3.0 m, going back to the centre from off it; Moira ending 1.91 m from the player on the centre side, 0.11 m from the centre); the guards (every authored rate exact, Vey's none at 0.35, Yuri's halved to 0.175 wobbled while Cal's and Surety's hold 0.35, Sam 0.80 walking in at 2.2 m and 0.35 in range, Osei frozen 0.35, and live her shell turning 61 % of 211 unread head jabs and 10 % of 212 body jabs); the clocks (in 25 s Yuri 3 taunts and Carter 2, Vane none in 60 s, Ada none; Yuri's first at tick 960 with the guard open 60 ticks, his read 0.05 and a jab landing unblocked; a taunt in his recover holding the next string 83 ticks; Tam's tempo flipping at his steps 720 / 1,440 / 2,160 / 2,880 and his live gaps 34–52 ticks fast, 70–130 slow); the flags and the reveal (Jean-Guy's and the Choir's feet silent from the bell, Yuri's audible, Marta's audible idle, quiet in her tell and audible after; Reyes' bell alone and together with a Reyes card dressed, Yuri's none; Remy's slot named in a blind dual where the rest read '?', every slot named in a sanctioned dual, the corner before a blind R2 naming Remy and hiding Yuri). `-- determinism` adds the delegated dual vs Carter's lineup on their neutral games (7 taunts), every hash identical twice and different from the same dual frozen.
**Result.** GREEN on all 24 bosses (272/272 each, 6,528 assertions); determinism 25/25; geometry 5/5; margins GREEN; sim_test 14/14; 16,000-frame soaks on the profile against Carter (6 taunts, on to R3), Tam (7 tempo shifts), Marta (54 footwork flags) and Vasile, zero errors. What the gates caught: two older live gates changed with the fights. The profile's out-boxer against the live Yuri broke no tell in its 30 s at seed 7: Yuri's first taunt at tick 1,494 shifts the timeline, and his unchanged 35 % guard blocked the two tell-jabs that had broken the tell before, so the gate now runs two fights per archetype (seeds 7 and 8), requires reads, hits and the spacing order in each, and counts broken tells across both (1 / 0 / 3 / 2 and 5 / 6 / 6 / 3). The delegated dual against Vasile turned one clock kit, not two: his measured stance knocks RORY out of R1 at 63.5 s and two standing counts had stopped the clock short of the 60 s turn. The gate now proves one turn live and both turns on the same log with his neutral game frozen, each run hashed twice. Two of my own measurements were wrong the first time: the player's strafe input moves him toward −x, and a feed line follows each tempo event. Cal's and Moira's arrival tolerance was also tightened from 0.1–0.15 m to 0.03 m.

## C26 — The Talk: a right read lands on the fighter it was said to, a wrong one costs nothing, and bonds tell you which — PROVEN
**Claim.** The corner's fifth action (NP_10 §4) is Whitlock's talk with the next fighter: three options keyed to that fighter's personality — the plan, the fire, the arm around the shoulder, each in the fighter's own line — and the one the card names lands. A right read puts the fighter at +5 % on every stat for their own segment and nobody else's: the talk is bound to the fighter it was said to, so a swap after it leaves it unheard. A wrong read costs the action and nothing else — once that fighter is out, the sim is the one where nobody talked, to the hash ("never a penalty for trying to know your kids"). One talk per corner; a card with no personality cannot be read. Bond levels reveal which talk lands: the next fighter's best Kizuna with a dressed teammate — Gymmates rules one wrong option out, Corner Voices and above names the right one — with the teammate named as the one who tells you. The profile is NP_11 §9's `personality.talk_profile` on the card, carried by the save. The profile in manager mode never talks.
**Mechanism.** `data/talk.v1.json` holds the three approaches in order, the stat (+5 %), the reveal levels (1 rules out, 2 names) and the 24 lines (eight cards × three approaches); each Millbrook card in `core.gd` carries `"talk"` (RORY fire, PRIYA plan, MARCUS arm, JUNE fire, SAM arm, DEX arm, YURI fire, MARTA plan). `corner_action({kind: talk, option})` takes the budget like any action, refuses a card with no profile, a second talk in the corner (`corner.talked`) and an unknown option (none of those spends the action), raises `talk {who, option, landed, line}` and on a right read writes `pending_fx.talk = {who, pct}`; `_load_segment` applies it after the Kizuna effects when the fighter going out is `who` (`talk_effect`), else raises `talk_unheard`. `talk_menu()` (in `corner_options()`) lists the three lines, whether the card can be read, whether the corner has talked, and `talk_hint(name)`: the best `kizuna_level` between the fighter and any other dressed card. `_open_corner` raises `talk_hint` when there is one. `sim/save.gd` writes `personality: {talk_profile, bark_set}` into each Fighter record and puts `talk` back on the card. The corner dict and `pending_fx` are already state, so a talk hashes and snapshots with the corner; `TALK` is content (`SNAP_SKIP`, the content stamp).
**Test.** `-- smoke` PAC-072 stage: 7 assertions — the data (plan / fire / arm, +5 %) and the eight cards' profiles (fire 3, plan 2, arm 3) with three lines each, Priya's the plan; the right read (the corner offers PRIYA "Your projection holds. Third exchange, he leads right." / "Go take his head off." / "You don't have to be perfect out there."; the plan lands, one action spent, no Tension, the corner's talk done; in R2 she fights at PWR 36.75 and RFX 73.50 with a `talk_effect`; MARCUS in R3 at 70); the wrong read (the arm heard and missed, the action spent, PWR 35, no `talk_effect`, and after she is out the state hash equal to the dual where nobody talked) and, over 24 corners, each of the eight cards with exactly one read that lands, its own; the refusals naming why and spending nothing ("PRIYA has heard you already this corner", "no read on PRIYA yet - no talk profile on the card", "no such talk: 'shout'"), and a talk refused with no actions left, in a locked corner, in a fight and in a scrap; the talk bound to the fighter (the plan to Priya, JUNE swapped in, JUNE out at PWR 30 with `talk_unheard {PRIYA, JUNE}`; JUNE swapped in first, the corner talking to JUNE, the fire landing at 31.50); the reveal (no bond, no hint; RORY & PRIYA at 10 ruling out the fire, at 25 naming the plan; MARCUS & PRIYA at 50 beside RORY at 10, MARCUS speaking at Lv3; SAM & PRIYA at 90 with SAM not dressed, nothing; `talk_hint` raised at the corner's open); the save (the eight profiles as `personality.talk_profile`, back on the cards after a round trip; a corner snapshotted after the plan to Priya restored into another sim on the same hash, Priya going out at 36.75). `-- determinism` adds the delegated dual with talks in its corners (3 talks, 2 landed, 2 segments at +5 %), every hash identical twice and different from the same log without talks, in which nobody talks.
**Result.** GREEN on all 24 bosses (279/279 each, 6,696 assertions); determinism 26/26; geometry 5/5; margins GREEN; sim_test 14/14; 16,000-frame `-- demo talk` soaks on Yuri (a talk landed), Vasile (two missed) and the sanctioned format with RORY + PRIYA at Corner Voices (the hint, two landed), zero errors. What the gates caught: one of my own — the "no bond" hint was first read from a sim already past its corner, where there is no talk menu.

## C27 — The formation screen: your board is sealed at the bell, the named orders are presets read off the cards, and his projection is honest unless he runs a decoy — PROVEN
**Claim.** NP_10 §2–3 and NP_01 §7 as the sim's own data. Your board — the dressed squad in slots R1..Rk (k the format's segments, fewer if fewer are dressed), the rest in reserve, 3 staff — is taken at setup and sealed at the bell; sealed from the formation screen before the bell it is the same dual as a board handed over at setup, to the hash, and after the bell it is refused (the corner's swap is the reorder, NP_01 §7.4). The four named orders are presets over your starters with the ace and the anchor read off the cards: Standard (ace at the Hammer, R4; anchor-class at R5), Ambush (ace at R1), Wall-First (anchor-class at R1, ace at the Hammer), Relay Ladder (the best-bonded chain at R2-R3-R4 toward R5); a board of 3 or fewer closes on its ace. His projection gives each slot a confidence from the sources the sim has — how readable his coach's archetype is, a scout stand-in on his club's district, a Tactician on your board (+15 %), Pattern Intel on the dialect of the fighter in that slot (his page counting as PI 3) — capped, with low slots shown as silhouettes; the percentage is honest (a slot is projected right as often as it says). The projection is live on the formation screen and fixed at the bell: what you sealed against stays what it was (a PI rung earned in R1 does not rewrite it), each slot marked revealed with who is in it as its segment begins. A corner that runs decoys (NP_10 §3) poisons it at his `decoy_rate`: every name shifted a slot, every percentage as confident as the honest duals' ("the confidence % lies"), his real order untouched; a Tactician at Lv3 reads the decoy. A blind dual has no projection. Ep1's "Ascendant's probable order at 70% confidence" (NP_12A §5) is the projection of Millbrook's four against Ascendant B's corner. Every pairing on the board carries NP_06 §5's card — triangle, element, feud, the projected EP modifier — read by the same function the segment's matchup card runs, and one coach note.
**Mechanism.** `data/formation.v1.json` holds the four archetypes (label, text, `readable_pct`), the ace and anchor stat sums, the projection's numbers (the tactician's role and +15, PI +5 a level, cap 95, silhouettes under 35, the decoy's detect level 3 and one-slot shift) (since C28 the tactician's +15 and his Lv3 read are his passives in `staff.v1.json`, the level earned), the scout stand-in (PRIYA: Toronto, +30), the districts, the ring notes and the Ep1 board (MARCUS, PRIYA, RORY; JUNE). `coaches.v1.json` gains `decoy_rate` on the two corners with `decoys` (Vey's Academy 0.5, the Hollow Circuit 0.4). In `core.gd`: `setup(..., formation_d, board_d)` checks the board (`_board_check`: a named board, an archetype through `order_preset`, or explicit slots; every slot dressed and once; the count; the reserves the rest; up to 3 known staff with levels) and `_build_lineup` fights it in a dual (a scrap or spar keeps the first card); `seal_board(b)` restores the RNG to `dual_rng0` and sets the dual up again while `tick == dual_t0`, else refuses naming why; `order_preset`, `ace_of`, `anchor_of`; `staff_dressed()` feeds the corner's staff menu and actions (the equipped three when no board); `_reset_dual` records `sealed_p` / `sealed_b`, rolls `bdecoy`, raises `sealed` and says ORDERS SEALED; `_roll(tag, i)` hashes the seed, the boss, his corner, `seal_n` (counted by `new_dual`) and the slot — the fight's RNG is never drawn; `_projection_live()` computes the confidences, the honest guesses (the wrong slots trade names among themselves or with his bench) or the decoy's shift, and the Lv3 read; `_reset_dual` keeps it as `bprojection`, and `projection()` returns the live read until `bell_rung()` and the kept one after, with `revealed` / `actual` filled from the fight; `rings()` is the matchup card's arithmetic on six keys, which `_matchup()` now calls; `boss_rings()` dresses a boss's META with his opening kit; `pairing()` adds the EP / hissatsu / Tension percentages and `_ring_note()`; `overlay()` pairs your slots with his projected names; `formation_lines()` is the whiteboard as text. The DualState's `lineups.sealed` is the seal and `revealed` who went out; `sim/save.gd` writes the stable through `board_cards()` (board order, reserve flags). `FORMATION` is content (`SNAP_SKIP`, the content stamp); the seals are in the state hash. `main.gd`: `-- order=` / `-- board=` / `-- staff=`, `-- formation` (the whiteboard before the bell: 1-4 the named orders, 5 the card order, ENTER the bell), TAB, `-- demo formation`.
**Test.** `-- smoke` PAC-073 stage: 10 assertions — the data (the orders and their readability 40 / 30 / 35 / 25, PWR+SPD+RFX and CHN+BLK+STA, the tactician +15, the Ep1 board, `decoy_rate` 0.5 / 0.4 on exactly the two decoy corners); the presets (mini: ace PRIYA, anchor MARCUS, Standard RORY-MARCUS-PRIYA, Ambush PRIYA-RORY-MARCUS, Wall-First MARCUS-RORY-PRIYA; sanctioned: ace PRIYA, anchor SAM, Standard RORY-JUNE-MARCUS-PRIYA-SAM, Ambush PRIYA-RORY-JUNE-MARCUS-SAM, Wall-First SAM-RORY-JUNE-PRIYA-MARCUS, reserves DEX-YURI-MARTA; RORY at PWR 90 becoming the ace at R4; with RORY-SAM and SAM-MARCUS at 50 the ladder JUNE-RORY-SAM-MARCUS-PRIYA, two Adjacent Trust pairs beside each other against the card order's one); the seal (Ambush at setup going out PRIYA, RORY, JUNE, MARCUS, SAM across five segments; the same board sealed before the bell equal in hash and RNG state; a card-order board hashing as none); nine refusals naming why with nothing changed (after the bell; BOB not dressed; RORY twice; four slots; PHALANX; an unknown staff; four staff; reserves not the rest; no board 'ep9') and a bad board at setup leaving the card order with `board_refused`; after the seal (DEX swapped into R2 going out, the DualState sealed PRIYA-RORY-JUNE-MARCUS-SAM / revealed PRIYA-DEX, a scrap sending RORY and the dual after it the board again, the save's stable in board order with DEX-YURI-MARTA flagged and a load dressing the same five); Ep1 (vs the Ascendant B corner every slot 70 from Standard 40 + Priya 30, the order 70 %, the Ep1 board sealed from the data at the same 70 %, the feed "ORDERS SEALED - yours R1 RORY, R2 PRIYA, R3 MARCUS; theirs projected at 70%"); the sources (a journeyman's Standard 40; the Grain Kings' Ambush 30 and every slot a silhouette; Priya undressed 40; the Reveal on the board 85 and usable in the corner, and after R1 has raised his PI still 85 — the read sealed against — with R1 revealed as Vane and R2 not yet; PI 2 on Kowalchuk 70 / 80 / 70, the order 73 %; three pages and the tactician capped at 95; the blind dual all silhouettes at 0); the honest percentage (849 of 1,200 slots right at 70 %, 489 of 1,200 at 40 %, every wrong name one of his dressed and never shown twice, the fight's RNG undrawn, your seal not moving his projection); the decoy (Vey's corner: 203 of 400 duals, every name shifted a slot and wrong at the honest 70, the honest duals right 685 of 985; the same dual with his decoys removed equal to the hash; a Lv1 tactician at 85 % and fooled, a Lv3 turning every slot to a DECOY silhouette); the overlay (MARCUS vs Vane his style and his element, EP −10 %, hissatsu −10 %, "Cassidy Vane eats sluggers for breakfast"; PRIYA vs Yuri both ours, EP +10 %; RORY vs Sam the purist's; NP_06 §6's worked example 2 as a Swarmer/Frost of Tremblay vs Remy Moreau — his style, the feud, Tension +25 %, "Bad wind for us, but this segment will PRINT Tension"; the overlay equal to `pairing()` slot by slot, silhouettes unknown; at ten bells of two walked sanctioned duals — one against Reyes with DEX's feud, one against Vey in his opening kit — the overlay's rings equal to the segment's matchup card). `-- determinism` adds the delegated dual on a sealed Relay Ladder with the Reveal at Lv3 against Vey's corner (a decoy dual the tactician reads): out in the board's order, three Adjacent Trust segments, every hash identical twice and different from the same log on the card order, the projection identical in both runs. `tools/drive_formation.gd` loads the real scene with `-- formation` and presses the keys: the sim waits at tick 0 with the whiteboard on the HUD; 2 seals Ambush, 5 the card order, 4 the Relay Ladder (MARCUS, RORY, PRIYA), each at tick 0; ENTER rings the bell with MARCUS in R1; TAB puts YOUR BOARD [Relay Ladder] SEALED and THEIR PROJECTION on the HUD; 1 after the bell seals nothing.
**Result.** GREEN on all 24 bosses (289/289 each, 6,936 assertions); determinism 27/27; geometry 5/5; margins GREEN; sim_test 14/14; the key drive 7/7 on the real scene; 16,000-frame `-- demo formation` soaks — the Ep1 board against Ascendant B (the five picks, then the order at 70 % with all three slots right, still 70 % at the end), the Relay Ladder with the Reveal at Lv3 against Vey's corner (a decoy dual, read: five DECOY silhouettes; five Kizuna effects on the ladder) and Ambush in a blind dual (no projection) — zero errors. What the gates caught: my own — the tactician gate read the projection after a fought segment had raised PI on Vane's dialect (the projection is live; the gate now reads it at the bell); the first cap gate asked PI 3 of a dialect, which only a page gives (the projection now counts his page as PI 3, as `pi_own` does). And one the soak caught: the Ep1 soak's end-of-dual check disagreed with the projection printed at the bell, because the projection was recomputed live after R1 had raised his PI; it is now kept at the bell (`bprojection`, `bell_rung()`), and the sources gate checks it stays.

## C28 — The staff: three of a corps of ten, each passive live while dressed, levels earned by use, and a crowd the Hype can convert — PROVEN
**Claim.** NP_10 §6 as the sim's own data. The club keeps a corps of ten staff (NP_08 §1's cap) — cutmen, tacticians, hypes, conditioning coaches and the one rare Professor — and dresses three: the board's (C27), else the equipped three. Every dressed staffer's passive is live: the cutman restores +15 stamina to a fighter back on the stool with his condition carried (a fresh fighter has nothing to restore); the tactician gives the projection its +15 and the Scout Book a richer page; the hype makes the crowd's heat build 20 % faster; the conditioning coach adds 10 % to the team's stamina regen; the Professor doubles the Pattern Intel a dual segment earns. Two of a role both count; two carrying the same active pool its charges. A staffer's level is earned by use — every charge he spends is one use on the ledger, Lv2 at 3, Lv3 at 8 — and Lv3 unlocks his role's second passive: the cutman +10 composure too, the tactician reads a decoy seal (NP_10 §3; C27's read, now earned), the hype starts the dual at 10 Tension (NP_05 §1), the conditioning coach sends an overrun fighter out at 90 instead of 80, the Professor doubles scrap and spar PI as well. The crowd (NP_01 §1: "crowd heat feeds corner hype actions") is a meter of the building behind you in a dual: your own spectacle raises it — a DROP, a flash, a counter, an interrupt, getting up at eight, a hissatsu — his never does, and Raise the Roof converts 30 of it into its +8 Tension or is refused, charge and action kept, when the building is cold. The save writes the corps as NP_11 §9 Staff records and dresses the three back.
**Mechanism.** `data/staff.v1.json` holds the five roles (a passive and its text, the Lv3 passive and its text), the corps (id, placeholder handle, role, the active he carries from `tactics.v1.json`), the level thresholds, the crowd's sources and the conversion; `formation.v1.json` gives up its tactician numbers to it. In `core.gd`: `STAFF`, `staff_fx`, `crowd_heat`; `setup(..., formation_d, staff_d, board_d)`; `staff_dressed()` (the board's corps ids, else `_resolve_staff` of the equipped actives), `_corps_rec`, `_staff_active_rec`, `staff_active_of`, `staff_role`, `staff_name`, `_dressed_with_active`, `staff_uses` / `staff_level` (the reward ledger's `staff`), `staff_passives()` (numbers add, a floor or a start takes the best, flags hold, `by` names the carriers), `_staff_credit` (the pool's k-th charge is the use of the staffer whose charges it was; `staff_use` / `staff_level` events), `_crowd` (fed from `_ev` on your hits, drops, interrupts, `eight_up` and your casts), `staff_record`, `staff_line`, `seed_staff`; `_load_segment` reads `staff_fx` at each bell, the cutman's restore on a carried fighter before the overrun's clamp, the overrun at the Lv3 floor; the regen loop multiplies your stamina regen; `_reset_dual` pools each active's charges over its dressed carriers, zeroes the crowd and starts the Tension at a Lv3 hype's 10; `_pi_tick` takes a count (the Professor's extra); `corner_action`'s staff case takes an active or a dressed staffer, refuses Raise the Roof below its heat and spends it after; `corner_options` lists each active with its carriers and levels and the crowd; `scout_page` carries `tactician` notes; `_board_check` resolves staff to corps ids, dresses a staff-only board on the card order and refuses a declared level; the projection reads the tactician's `projection_pct` and `detect_decoys`. `sim/save.gd` writes `staff` from `staff_record` and `build` seals a board of the stable's starters with the saved three when they are not the default. `STAFF` is content (`SNAP_SKIP`, the content stamp); the crowd is in the hash, the staff ledger rides the reward ledger. `main.gd`: `-- staff=` seeds levels, the corner's staff lines, CROWD on the score bug, the level toast.
**Test.** `-- smoke` PAC-074 stage: 8 assertions — the data (5 roles each with both passives, 10 staffers in a cap of 10 — 2 cutmen, 2 tacticians, 2 hypes, 3 conditioning coaches, 1 Professor, the rare — each carrying his role's active; levels 3 / 8; the crowd's six sources and the 30; the default three `sportsmed`, `janitor`, `retired_boxer`); the board (OLD CUTMAN, the SPORTS-MED STUDENT and "the_reveal" resolving to the rival cornerman; Patch Job pooled at 4 charges by both cutmen; the named cutman then the pool crediting [2, 0] then [2, 2], the fifth refused, an undressed janitor refused; a staff-only board on the card order; refused "the Professor is dressed twice", "no staff 'nobody'", four staff, a declared level); levels by use (eight duals of the Reveal against Vey's corner: Lv2 at use 3, Lv3 at use 8 "reads a decoy seal", the ledger's 8 carried over new duals; in the first decoy dual after it, dual 9, the earned Lv3 reads it and the same board unearned is fooled); the cutman (RORY back for R4 of an Iron dual at the 40 / 20 he left with: 40 / 20 with no cutman, 55 / 20 with one, 70 / 20 with two, 55 / 30 at Lv3; PRIYA fresh at 100 with no restore); the conditioning coach (two seconds' rest from 50: 8.0 bare, 8.8 with one, 10.4 with three; an overrun at 80, at 90 with a Lv3); the crowd (drop / flash / counter / interrupt / up at eight / hissatsu 20 / 10 / 5 / 6 / 15 / 8 bare and 24 / 12 / 6 / 7.2 / 18 / 9.6 with the janitor, his spectacle 0; Raise the Roof at 20 heat refused with its charge and the action kept, at 36 +8 Tension and 6 left; the Lv3 janitor's dual at 10 Tension, his at 0); the Professor and the tactician (a dual segment vs Yuri counting 2 on Kowalchuk, 1 without him; a spar 1 at Lv1, 2 at Lv3; with a tactician Yuri's page notes his corner's Ambush, no decoys, R4; none without; two tacticians on a journeyman's board at 70); the save (10 Staff records, the dressed professor@0 Lv3 / old_cutman@1 Lv1 / rival_cornerman@2 Lv2, the Professor's two passives, his 8 uses and Chalk Talk; a load dressing the three back at Lv 3 / 1 / 2 on the stable's order; a default dual's save loading with no board). `-- determinism` adds the delegated dual with a staffed corner vs Yuri (the janitor at Lv3, the ring announcer, the Professor): 10 Tension at the bell, the building heated 14 times to 100, one Raise the Roof conversion and five staff calls refused, the Professor's segments counting 4 on Kowalchuk to the default corner's 2 (since C32 read per segment end — 2 over 1 against the default's 2 over 2 then, 1 over 1 since the relay's T3s reach the ring (C35) — because Yuri's Load-Bearing moves the fight itself), every hash identical twice and different from the default corner.
**Result.** GREEN on all 24 bosses (297/297 each, 7,128 assertions); determinism 28/28; geometry 5/5; margins GREEN; sim_test 14/14; the key drive 7/7 on the real scene; 16,000-frame soaks - the staffed corner vs Yuri (`-- demo boss=1 staff=janitor:3,ring_announcer,professor`: 10 Tension at the bell, the building at 100), the sanctioned board against Vey's corner with two cutmen and the rival cornerman at Lv3 (a decoy dual, read) and the Iron dual with the sports-med student at Lv3 (RORY back for R4 against Vane with his restore) - zero errors. Eight older gates moved with the corner, each still proving its claim: the corner budget's Raise the Roof now converts 30 heat set first; the Golden DROP's PRIYA, the Iron dual's RORY and the Gauntlet's RORY come back with the cutman's +15 on the carried stamina (55; 95 and 55; 65 and 45); C27's decoy gate and its sealed-board determinism gate seed the Lv3 tactician as 8 uses on the ledger instead of a declared level, and its data gate reads the +15 from the staff table; the Vasile determinism gate dresses a corner without a conditioning coach, because the default corner's regen changes RORY's R1 and the log's KO came before the 60 s kit turn. The default corner's live passives also move the other delegated logs' figures (C22's ledger now pays 463 XP, C26's talks land once, C25's Carter taunts 9 times): every gate asserts its claim, not those figures, and each holds.

## C29 — Recruitment: a club beaten flags its fighters, the approach states the whole condition, the grammar reads the sim's own ledgers, and nobody joins on his own — PROVEN
**Claim.** NP_08 §2 as the sim's own data. Every one of the 24 bosses, and Dex, carries a recruitment record: a condition in NP_08 §2.2's grammar — the roster docs' own line with its source — or the reason the docs close him (none, staff, post-game, unauthored; until C30 also Jean-Guy's Arc 1 refusal, which C30 corrects: NP_07A recruits him in Arc 2). Winning a dual flags the recruitable named fighters of the squad you beat as approachable (up to four, NP_08 §2.1's 2–4; a non-boss recruit surfaces with the boss whose club he belongs to); losing flags nobody. Approaching one surfaces the whole condition, every clause with what you have and what it needs ("honest: the game states requirements outright once discovered"). The grammar runs in full: BEAT_IN counts your wins against him by size (scrap, spar, dual segment), `xN` asks N of them, `USING STYLE_PROOF(style)` asks them won by a fighter of that style; STYLE_PROOF, PI_LEVEL (his page counting as 3), BOND (a stablemate's best Kizuna in the stable), STAT_PROOF (a stablemate with the stat) and WITNESS (duals won after he was approached) read the sim's ledgers; QUEST, REP, FEUD_RESOLVED, BLESSING and ITEM read the story flags the overworld sets. A met condition raises an event and nobody joins on his own ("rival bosses never auto-join"): recruiting him puts his card in the stable — now its own list, every card the club owns — where a board can dress him. The save writes the whole stable, the recruit ledger and the story flags.
**Mechanism.** `data/recruits.v1.json`: the grammar (each form's owner; the overworld forms' flag keys), the cap, the card tier, and the 25 records (card name, condition, source; `from_lineup` for the four Millbrook cards, `with` / `beat_as` for Dex, `hissatsu` for Vane's drafted move, `never` for the closed). In `core.gd`: `RECRUITS`, `stable`; `setup(..., staff_d, recruits_d, board_d)` starts the stable with the dressed; the reward ledger gains `beaten` (wins by opponent, size and style), `recruit` (state, witnessed, ready) and `story` (the flags); `_beat` from `_end_segment` (a segment won or his three drops, and the Golden DROP), `_end_bout` (a scrap or spar won, against the boss or the NPC card) and `_recruit_after_dual` (the dual, then the flagging and the witnessing); `parse_condition` (static: AND, `xN`, `USING`), `_clause_state`, `condition_state`, `recruit_state`, `recruit_view`, `recruits_open`, `approach` and `recruit` (by name or `*`), `recruit_card`, `_recruit_check` (READY once), `recruit_lines`, `seed_story`; `step()` takes `approach` / `recruit` between duals; `_board_check` takes `dress` (the squad named from the stable) and `_apply_board` dresses it; the hash carries the stable. `sim/save.gd` writes the stable beyond the dressed with `dressed: false`, `story_flags` from the ledger, reads them back as the bench and appends it to the loaded stable. `SNAP_SKIP` and the content stamp now carry `STAFF` and `RECRUITS`. `main.gd`: `-- flag=`, `-- demo recruit`, [L] / [P] at DUAL OVER, the RECRUITS lines and the toasts; `tools/drive_recruit.gd` presses the keys.
**Test.** `-- smoke` PAC-075 stage: 7 assertions — the data (25 records, 13 conditions and 12 closed since C30 — 12 and 13 before it, none of the 24 missing, every source cited, 11 forms: the sim's six and the overworld's five, the conditions using BEAT_IN / QUEST / FEUD_RESOLVED / BOND / PI_LEVEL / REP / WITNESS, the eight new cards on their bosses' rings at 70 with a library hissatsu); the flagging (a dual won on segments against `[yuri, jean_guy, sam, ada]` flagging all four since C30 — YURI, SAM, ADA and never Jean-Guy before it; a dual lost nobody; the sanctioned eight, SAM and YURI already in the stable, flagging JEAN-GUY, ADA and CARTER and never ANDERS (ADA and CARTER before C30); Marta's squad flagging MARTA, MOIRA, DEX with her and REMY, the cap of four; Ada approachable showing no condition); the approach (YURI's `BEAT_IN(dual[segment]) 1/1 met`; ADA's `BOND(Carter, 4) 0/4` and `QUEST(okafor_table) 0/1`; refused "moira_macisaac is not approachable - beat his club in a dual first", Anders's closed line (Jean-Guy's declined line before C30), "yuri_kowalchuk has been approached - his condition stands"; YURI READY and still not in the stable); the recruit (ADA refused naming both clauses; YURI joining with the toast "YURI JOINS THE STABLE (5 cards)", the dressed still the four, the stable five over a new dual; a board dressing RORY / PRIYA / MARCUS / YURI with YURI in R1 at PWR 80; "BOB is not in the stable", "a squad of 3 for a dual that dresses 4"); the forms (Marta's spar won by RORY counting 0 of 1 for the Out-Boxer clause, by PRIYA joining her with her quest and feud flags; DEX through a scrap against the Netmen and his quest, then MOIRA's BOND(Dex, 3) from 0 of 3 to joined at Adjacent Trust; CARTER at two scraps and REP 2 unmet, at three and REP 3 joined; TAM's PI 2 from the dual's own segment against him and a spar; VANE's WITNESS 0 of 1 until a second dual is won; PI_LEVEL(whitecloud, 2) 0 of 2; STAT_PROOF PWR>=60 met by MARCUS's 70 and >=90 not; STYLE_PROOF(out-boxer) met; an unknown form never met, its owner "?"); the save (6 Fighter records with YURI and SAM undressed, the ledger, the story flags; a load dressing the four, the stable six, YURI joined, ADA approached); the content tables (all 19 outside the snapshot — 20 since C30's `STRINGS` — a changed staff or recruit table changing the stamp). `-- determinism` adds the delegated log against Moira's squad (the dual the log wins on segments) with the approach and the recruit between duals and Remy's quest flag seeded (`quest:cold_storage` since C30, `quest:q_moreau` before): one flagging of four, four conditions surfaced (five since C30: REMY's string surfaces JEAN-GUY's), REMY joined; identical twice and different from the same log without the approach. `tools/drive_recruit.gd` loads the real scene against Yuri's squad, wins the dual on segments, and presses P (nobody is ready), L (the conditions on the HUD, READY shown), P (YURI and SAM, each beaten in his segment, join; ADA waits), L (nobody to approach): 5 / 5 (6 / 6 since C30, which checks the strings the join puts up; the flagged list now includes JEAN-GUY).
**Result.** GREEN on all 24 bosses (304/304 each, 7,296 assertions); determinism 29/29; geometry 5/5; margins GREEN; sim_test 14/14; the formation drive 7/7 and the recruit drive 5/5; a 60,000-frame `-- demo recruit` soak against Moira's squad (two duals ended in 49,721 ticks; the win flagged four, the approach surfaced four conditions, REMY joined on his seeded quest) and 16,000-frame recruit soaks against Yuri's squad and the sanctioned format, zero errors. A correction the content gate made: C28's mechanism said `STAFF` was content (`SNAP_SKIP`, the content stamp), but PAC-074's build left it out of both — the staff table rode inside every snapshot and changes to it did not change the stamp; this build puts it (and `RECRUITS`) in both, and the gate now checks every content table.

## C30 — The String Wall: a recruit turns up the cards he is strung to, a house's blessing opens its members and the houses bound to it, and the wall is the recruit ledger's view — PROVEN
**Claim.** NP_08 §2.3 as the sim's own data. The String Wall is six maps of the questlines' own people — 43 cards: 16 fighters with recruit records, 14 leads the docs name who have no card in the sim, 13 houses with NP_07A's blessing lines — and 42 strings of three kinds. A fighter recruited turns up the cards strung to him ("recruiting a node reveals adjacent nodes"): family both ways ("families chain": CARTER turns up ADA, NNEKA and CHIAMAKA; REMY turns up his cousin JEAN-GUY, and JEAN-GUY would turn up REMY), master to student one way ("masters unlock students": SAM turns up the junior he corners for and CHOIR; CHOIR signed turns up nobody), a member to his house (YURI turns up the Kowalchuk barn). A house's blessing — the overworld's `blessing:<house>` flag — turns up the house, its members and the houses bound to it on the feud graph by ALLIED, MARRIAGE or DEBT, never across a FEUD ("houses chain through blessings"); a house with nobody left to bless (Iron) opens with its crest, its last-of-line with it. A fighter the wall turns up is approached — his condition surfaces as an approach would, with the string he came by — and one the club had already approached or signed adds nothing; a lead is on the wall with its condition where the docs author one (Vera Iron's), and nothing in the sim signs it; a closed fighter is shown closed with his reason. The BOND conditions the roster docs authored are the first strings (Dex → Moira, Carter → Ada, Sam → the Choir). The wall is read off the ledgers — the recruit ledger's states and the cards turned up with their strings — and the save writes it (NP_11 §9's `string_wall{maps, leads}`).
**Mechanism.** `data/string_wall.v1.json`: the link kinds and what each reveals, the node kinds, the chain relations (ALLIED, MARRIAGE, DEBT), and 6 maps (id, title, source, nodes, links); a fighter node's id is its recruit record's; a lead carries its role, its note, its source and, where authored, its condition; a house its key, its authority, its blessing line (null for Iron) and its source; a string its ends, its kind, its reason and, for three, the BOND clause it keys. In `core.gd`: `STRINGS`; `setup(..., recruits_d, strings_d, board_d)`; the reward ledger's `string_wall` (each card turned up → the string it came by, its kind, the day); `sw_node`, `sw_links`, `sw_house_node`, `sw_state`, `sw_label`, `_sw_reveal`, `_sw_sync` (to a fixed point in the table's order, raising `wall_revealed` per string and `recruit_condition` with `via` per fighter surfaced), `string_wall` (the view: per map the face-up cards with their state and the strings between them, the feud graph's edges between face-up houses, the leads in the table's order), `string_wall_lines`, `_sw_not_a_recruit`; `_recruit_check` syncs the wall first, `recruit` syncs after a join and `recruit("*")` runs until nobody more is ready; setup syncs a ledger's recruits and blessings before the bell; `recruit_view` carries `via` and `recruit_lines` names it; `approach` / `recruit` refuse a lead or a house by name. `SNAP_SKIP` and the content stamp carry `STRINGS`. `sim/save.gd` writes `string_wall` from the view (the ledger holds it; a load recomputes it). `main.gd`: the STRING WALL lines at DUAL OVER, the toast (chained after the join's), the soak's wall; `tools/drive_recruit.gd` checks them. With it, a correction to C29's data: `recruits.v1.json`'s Jean-Guy is recruitable in Arc 2 on NP_07A Q-MOREAU's line (`QUEST(cold_storage) AND FEUD_RESOLVED(moreau, tremblay)`; his Arc 1 refusal kept as `arc1`), and Rémy's "through Q-MOREAU completion" reads Q-MOREAU's quest id, `QUEST(cold_storage)`.
**Test.** `-- smoke` PAC-076 stage: 5 assertions — the data (6 maps; 43 nodes, 16 fighters / 14 leads / 13 houses; 42 strings; no problem found: every fighter a recruit record, every lead and house sourced, every house on the feud graph with its blessing line in the grammar, every string's ends on the wall and its kind known, every fighter's house string to his own house; DEX → MOIRA, CARTER → ADA, SAM → CHOIR strung; a changed wall table changing the stamp; a fresh club's wall 0 of 43 up, no lines); family (ADA face down before; CARTER, signed after three scraps and REP 3, turns up "CARTER -> ADA, NNEKA OKAFOR, CHIAMAKA, HOUSE OKAFOR" — ADA approached via CARTER with `BOND(Carter, 4) 0/4` and `QUEST(okafor_table) 1/1`, one `recruit_condition` by the string, NNEKA a lead, CHIAMAKA never, House Okafor unblessed, House Petrov still face down; the recruit lines naming "via CARTER"; NNEKA's approach refused "NNEKA OKAFOR is a lead on the String Wall (staff: Hype) - no card in the sim; the overworld signs a lead"; ADA signed through the string at Duo with CARTER, adding no string (since C31 one: her line is House Okafor's, so her signing blesses the house and its string runs to House Petrov across E04); the other way, JEAN-GUY face down against Moira's squad, REMY joining turns him up approached via REMY at `1/1`, `0/1`, refused "jean_guy_moreau is not ready - FEUD_RESOLVED(moreau, tremblay) (0 of 1)", E05 closed → READY once → signed, House Moreau up — blessed since C31, E05 closed and Cold Storage being Moreau's line); master (SAM joining turns up "SAM -> THE JUNIOR, CHOIR, HOUSE TAGAQ", CHOIR approached via SAM with `BOND(Sam, 4) 0/4` and `QUEST(choir_name) 0/1`, the junior a lead, House Whitecloud face down; a ledger with CHOIR signed and SAM not: one card up, hers, no string, the junior and SAM face down); house (YURI joining turns up House Kowalchuk unblessed and not the cousins; `blessing:kowalchuk` turns up "the Kowalchuk blessing -> HOUSE KOWALCHUK, YURI, THE KOWALCHUK COUSINS, HOUSE WHITECLOUD", YURI approached via the blessing, the Whitecloud elder face down, House Reyes face down across E11's FEUD; `blessing:moreau` turns up "the Moreau blessing -> HOUSE MOREAU, JEAN-GUY, REMY, HOUSE IRON" and "HOUSE IRON -> VERA IRON", both Moreaus approached, Vera a lead with `ITEM(iron_gym_stencil_bag) 0/1` and `QUEST(estate_sale) 0/1`, House Iron "no blessing", House Tremblay face down across E05; the lines ending "HOUSES: HOUSE IRON = HOUSE MOREAU (DEBT)"); the view and the save (the Carter chain's three chairs 5 of 5 with 9 strings, the BOND one named, 4 leads via CARTER — 5 since C31, House Petrov via the Okafor blessing, and House Okafor shown blessed — the line "THREE CHAIRS AT THE TABLE 5/5: CARTER [in the stable], ADA [in the stable <- CARTER], NNEKA OKAFOR [lead <- CARTER], CHIAMAKA [closed <- CARTER], HOUSE OKAFOR [unblessed <- CARTER]"; the Moreau blessing's save — 6 maps, 5 cards up, 5 leads, equal to the view — loaded back turning no card, its wall equal and the save text written again byte-equal). `-- determinism` adds the String Wall on the delegated log against Moira's squad with Q-MOREAU's quest and E05 closed seeded: the strings "REMY -> JEAN-GUY, HOUSE MOREAU", joined in order REMY then JEAN-GUY (via REMY; no squad in the log fields him), identical twice and different from C29's log with E05 open, where JEAN-GUY waits approached. Since C31 E05 closed means Moreau's line is met before the bell, so the gate reads the string on C29's log (E05 open: "REMY -> JEAN-GUY, HOUSE MOREAU", JEAN-GUY waiting) and the house route on the E05-closed log ("the Moreau blessing -> HOUSE MOREAU, JEAN-GUY, REMY, HOUSE TREMBLAY, HOUSE IRON", "HOUSE IRON -> VERA IRON", both signed the tick the dual ends). `tools/drive_recruit.gd` adds the String Wall on the real scene after P: "STRING WALL (8 of 43 cards up)", CHOIR approached via SAM, the junior a lead, House Kowalchuk via YURI, the toast "STRING WALL  SAM -> THE JUNIOR, CHOIR, HOUSE TAGAQ": 6 / 6.
**Result.** GREEN on all 24 bosses (309/309 each, 7,416 assertions); determinism 30/30; geometry 5/5; margins GREEN; sim_test 14/14; the formation drive 7/7 and the recruit drive 6/6; a 60,000-frame `-- demo recruit` soak against Moira's squad with Q-MOREAU's quest and E05 closed (two duals in 49,738 ticks; the win flagged four, REMY joined and his string turned up JEAN-GUY, who joined in the same press: one `wall_revealed`, two joins), a 16,000-frame soak on the Moreau blessing (Cold Storage 5 of 7 up, VERA IRON a lead, the DEBT string to House Iron) and 16,000-frame recruit soaks against Yuri's squad and the sanctioned format (the eight's recruits face up in the stable, no strings from them), zero errors. The C29 gates the Jean-Guy correction moved are listed in REGISTER_COMMANDS_025 command 7; each still proves its claim.

## C31 — Blessings and the live graph: a house's line is read against the ledgers and blesses the club when met, a recruit arrives NATIVE or UNSANCTIONED, and the questlines' flips re-tune the house ring — PROVEN
**Claim.** NP_08 §3, NP_07 §5–6 and NP_07A §1–2 as the sim's own data. Every house carries NP_07A's blessing line — twelve of fourteen: Iron has nobody left to bless, the sealed house blesses itself — read against the ledgers with the house as its subject: BEAT_IN counts your wins against any fighter of that house; WITNESS counts duals won while the house's crest is up on the String Wall ("the patriarch attends a home dual"; Tagaq's only those won without your corner converting crowd heat); Petrov's STYLE_PROOF asks a win over an Ascendant fighter by Petrov moves alone; the rest read as C29 reads them. A met line — or the overworld's `blessing:<house>` flag — blesses the house once, with the day and how. A recruit of a blessed house arrives NATIVE with its natives unlocked (the library's moves of that house, the SECRET excepted: its gate event gives it); a recruit of an unblessed house arrives UNSANCTIONED (NP_07 §5): natives locked and no home bonus on his house's moves until the blessing lands and sanctions him. Recruiting around the family works where the docs author it (Efua, NP_07A Q-OSEI beat 1). The Reyes code is the one revocable blessing: La Campana thrown in a dual at a fighter above 40 stamina voids it for good. The feud graph flips on its questlines' flags — a FEUD on its own FEUD_RESOLVED flag, a DEBT on the quest that repays it, E02 only after both houses' final beats, E11 refused as authored, the provisional Petrov–Ascendant DEBT added — in the ledger, never in the table; the house ring's feud Tension, the ALLIED Kizuna bonus, FEUD_RESOLVED and the String Wall's chains read the graph as it stands. The save writes the house codex, the graph and each Fighter's house status and natives.
**Mechanism.** `data/houses.v1.json`: 14 houses (name, root, trait — shown, not executed — authority, blessing line and source; Kowalchuk's and Tagaq's witness rules, Petrov's `style_proof`, Reyes's `revocable`; the two statuses). `data/feud_graph.v1.json` gains each edge's `flip` (`on` / `to` / questline / when / source, or `held`, or `refused`), `mutation_rules` and the `provisional` E13, attached to the house table at load. `recruits.v1.json`'s Efua gains `around_the_family`. In `core.gd`: `HOUSES` (`load_houses`); `setup(..., strings_d, houses_d, board_d)`; the reward ledger's `houses` (blessed, day, how, witnessed, revoked) and `graph` (flips, refusals, additions); `_flag_set`, `house_blessed`, `house_state`, `house_clauses` and the house-subject readings in `_clause_state` (BEAT_IN, STYLE_PROOF, WITNESS; BLESSING by `house_blessed`; FEUD_RESOLVED by the edge for a pair on the graph); `_recruit_of_card`, `house_status`, `house_natives`, `natives_unlocked`; `_graph_sync`, `_bless_sync` and `_world_sync` (the graph, the blessings, the wall, to rest — in place of C30's wall sync in setup, `_recruit_check` and `recruit`); `_house_witness` from `_recruit_after_dual`; `_house_code_check` from `_start_cast`; `equip`; `house_codex`, `graph_view`, `house_lines`; the String Wall's house cards carrying `blessing_clauses`; `edge_relation`, `live_edges` and `_edge_between` under `_feud_between`, `_feud_edge_between`, `_allied` and the wall's chains and bonds; `_beat` recording the opponent's house and the house of the moves that won (`seg_cast_houses`); `crowd_spent_dual`; `P["house_status"]` at every segment's load, `hiss_mult` withholding the home bonus from an UNSANCTIONED caster; `recruit_view`'s `around`, the ready around the family in `_recruit_check`, `recruit` signing around it and carrying the status on `recruit_joined`. `SNAP_SKIP` and the content stamp carry `HOUSES`. `sim/save.gd`: the Fighter record's `house_status`, `natives` and `signature`; `house_codex` and `feud_graph_state` written. `main.gd`: the HOUSES lines at DUAL OVER, the blessing, void and graph toasts, the soak's houses; `tools/drive_recruit.gd` checks the lines.
**Test.** `-- smoke` PAC-077 stage: 8 assertions — the data (14 houses, 12 lines, each the String Wall's own and in the grammar; two natives a house off the library, none for Iron; the graph's flips 6 on flags, 5 held, 1 refused, E13 provisional; a fresh club unmutated and unblessed, 12 live edges; a changed house table changing the stamp); the patriarch attends (after a dual won against Yuri's squad Kowalchuk's line reads `1/1`, `0/1`; YURI joins UNSANCTIONED with no natives, the crest up unblessed; the next dual won → "kowalchuk met [YURI]", one `sanctioned`, YURI NATIVE with Grain Elevator and Full Load, the wall "the Kowalchuk blessing -> THE KOWALCHUK COUSINS, HOUSE WHITECLOUD", House Tagaq — SAM only flagged — witnessing nothing, the line "KOWALCHUK [blessed]  | YURI NATIVE", the String Wall's Kowalchuk card reading `BEAT_IN(dual[segment]) 2/1 ok`, `WITNESS(patriarch attends a home dual) 1/1 ok`); the quiet corner (SAM UNSANCTIONED at Adjacent Trust, Tagaq's line `3/3`, `0/1`; a dual won with 30 heat converted leaves it `0/1`, one without blesses: SAM NATIVE with Sorry Eh and Still Water); the open ledger (E13 added at `quest:open_ledger`; spars over Yuri by System Jab, over Vane with no cast, over Vane by System Jab and Grain Elevator leave STYLE_PROOF `0/1`; over Vane by System Jab alone `1/1` → "petrov met []", the blessing turning up the crest, the patriarch, the three students, ANDERS, BARO, House Okafor and House Osei); the Reyes code (blessed by the flag; La Campana at 100 stamina → revoked with `reyes_code`, the wall's crest "revoked"; at 30 → still blessed; broken before any blessing → the later flag lands nothing); around the family (Efua's line `0/1`, `0/1`, her drafted route `1/1`, READY around once; she joins UNSANCTIONED, her record's natives empty, Shell Game refused "EFUA is UNSANCTIONED - House Osei has not blessed the club, the natives stay locked", her Deep Roots at ×1.300; her quest and the internal feud → Osei blessed, EFUA sanctioned once, Shell Game and Through the Window equipped, her signature back, Harvest Moon refused as another house's, YURI's Harvest Moon refused as Kowalchuk's SECRET; her Deep Roots at ×1.430 = ×1.300 × 1.1); the live graph (Moreau vs Tremblay a feud until `feud_resolved:tremblay+moreau` flips E05, then none; FEUD_RESOLVED(moreau, tremblay) `1/1` "E05 on the feud graph: ALLIED"; E11 refused and still a feud; E02 FEUD on its flag alone, ALLIED once both final beats are set; an Okafor–Petrov pair's +8 → 10 once E04 is repaid; the Moreau blessing with E05 flipped reaching House Tremblay); the save (the codex Kowalchuk blessed by its line with YURI NATIVE, and Whitecloud; the graph's one flip E05 FEUD → ALLIED over 12 edges; YURI's record NATIVE with his natives, RORY's house status null; a load granting and flipping nothing, the text written again byte-equal). `-- determinism` adds a blessing on the delegated log against Moira's squad: a ledger from an earlier session (YURI signed, a segment won against him, his card on the bench) puts House Kowalchuk's crest up from the bell, the log's dual is the home dual he watches, and the line is met at the dual's end ("kowalchuk met [YURI]", YURI NATIVE, the wall "YURI -> HOUSE KOWALCHUK", "the Kowalchuk blessing -> THE KOWALCHUK COUSINS, HOUSE WHITECLOUD"); identical twice and different from the same log without YURI's signing, where the crest is down and nothing is granted. C30's gate on the same log now reads the string on the E05-open log and the house route on the E05-closed one. `tools/drive_recruit.gd` adds the HOUSES lines after the join (Kowalchuk's line with the segment won met and the witness to come, Tagaq's waiting on Sam's bond, YURI and SAM UNSANCTIONED): 7 / 7.
**Result.** GREEN on all 24 bosses (317/317 each, 7,608 assertions); determinism 31/31; geometry 5/5; margins GREEN; sim_test 14/14; the formation drive 7/7 and the recruit drive 7/7; a 60,000-frame `-- demo recruit` soak against Moira's squad with Q-MOREAU's quest and E05 closed (E05 flipped and the Moreau blessing granted before the bell; three duals ended in 49,735 ticks, the first won, JEAN-GUY and REMY joined NATIVE through the blessing), a 60,000-frame soak against Yuri's squad (two duals; the first won, YURI and SAM joined UNSANCTIONED; the second lost, so the patriarch's witness stays 0 of 1), a 16,000-frame soak on the live graph (E04 repaid, E13 added, E11 refused, House Reyes blessed by its flag) and a 16,000-frame soak on the sanctioned format, zero errors. Three C30 gates moved with the blessings (REGISTER_COMMANDS_026 command 8); each still proves its claim.

## C32 — House traits: the nine authored traits run on both corners, for the blood that carries them — PROVEN
**Claim.** NP_07 §3 as the sim's own data, and NP_07 §5's grammar on who carries a trait. Nine authored traits run: Never Twice (the second drop of a segment becomes a wobble, once a dual), Deep Roots (+50 % guard regen while trailing on drops), Long Winter (all stats +10 % in the R4 and R5 slots), First Frost (counter windows +100 ms in a segment's opening 15 s), Harvest (+1 Tension on every exchange win, anywhere), Load-Bearing (a composure pool 20 % deeper on the ropes and in the corner), Tide Discipline (stamina never below 10), Open Door (bonds grow ×1.5) and Debt of Honour (all stats +25 % against a house his is feuding with on the live graph). They run on both corners wherever a boss has an analogue: a boss's guard never regenerates, his counters are authored strings and he keeps no bonds, so Deep Roots, First Frost and Open Door are the player's side only. A fighter carries his house's trait when his lineage is blood — Chiamaka's root line and Itzel's origin line do; Anders (taken), Baró (a teaching root system) and HC-7 (extracted) do not; the sealed house's trait is offline — and when he is not a disowned heir; UNSANCTIONED keeps it (NP_07 §5 takes only the home bonus); the last of a dead lineage doubles it. The three §8 variants (Petrov's First Frost, Delacroix-Cardinal's Harvest, Moreau's Load-Bearing) are named and not run. Every multiplier stops at NP_06 §4's 1.5.
**Mechanism.** `houses.v1.json` gains `traits` (the nine with their magnitudes, their sides and each boss-side absence; the three variants `executed: false` with their base), each house's `trait_id`, Iron's `last_of_line`, `lineage` (the META qualifiers and whether each is blood) and `trait_rules`. In `core.gd`: `trait_of_house`, `card_trait`, `_tx`, `_tmult`, `_sfloor`, `_trait_stat_mult`, `_restat`, `_never_twice`; `trait_uses` (reset every dual) and `seg_tick0`; `_load_segment` arms `P["trait"]` and `B["trait"]` once both stand in the ring, re-draws their stats and pools for Long Winter and Debt of Honour, and raises `traits`; `_drop` (Never Twice), the player's guard regen (Deep Roots), `_window` (First Frost), both hit paths' Tension (Harvest), `_comp_damage` (Load-Bearing), every stamina drain (Tide Discipline), `_pay_kizuna` (Open Door: the larger of it and ALLIED, never both); setup now syncs the graph and the blessings before the first segment loads, so Debt of Honour reads the graph as it stands at the bell; `house_codex` adds `trait_live` / `trait_note`. `sim/save.gd`'s Fighter record carries `trait`. `main.gd`'s matchup card names both corners' traits; `tools/drive_recruit.gd` checks it.
**Test.** `-- smoke` PAC-078 stage: 8 assertions — the data and the carriers (9 authored and 3 variants; across the 24, Yuri Load-Bearing, Sam Never Twice, Ada, Carter and Chiamaka Harvest, Efua Deep Roots, Itzel Debt of Honour, Marta First Frost, Moira Tide Discipline, Tam Open Door and nobody else; the Millbrook eight's SAM, DEX, YURI and MARTA; a disowned Tagaq heir none; the Moreau variant, executed, running Load-Bearing); Never Twice (SAM's drops through three knockdowns 1, 1, 2 — the second a wobble, one NEVER TWICE, the score 2; Sam's the same on his side, and a new dual's second drop a wobble again); Deep Roots (EFUA's guard regen ×1.50 when trailing on drops, RORY's unchanged; EFUA recruited around her family UNSANCTIONED and carrying Deep Roots); the stats (a Whitecloud MARCUS PWR 70 in R1 and 77 in R4; Itzel's PWR 75 against YURI and DEX, 60 against RORY, 60 once E02 flips; a Reyes card 56.25 against Yuri; a Long Winter Iron doubled, 84 in R4; the card's own stats untouched); First Frost and Harvest (MARTA's slip window 60 ticks in the opening, 48 after, RORY's 48 throughout; ADA's clean hit +3.00 Tension, +2.00 with the trait stripped; Carter's the same); Load-Bearing and Tide Discipline (12 composure damage 10.0 on the ropes for Yuri and in the corner for YURI, 12.0 in the middle; a lean at 12 stamina leaves Moira and DEX 10.0, Vane 7.0); Open Door (TAM +12 for 8, a pair without him +8, TAM with an ALLIED partner +12 not 15, an ALLIED pair without him +10); in play (the bell's traits "Load-Bearing / Never Twice" for YURI against Sam; YURI's Fighter record "Load-Bearing", RORY's null; the codex's Kowalchuk trait live, Moreau's not). `-- determinism` adds the delegated log with YURI, SAM and MARTA against Carter's squad (Harvest on his side), identical twice and different from the same log on a house table with every trait taken out, where no segment names one. `tools/drive_recruit.gd` adds the matchup card's "TRAITS: yuri_kowalchuk Load-Bearing": 8 / 8. Two older gates moved: NP_01 §3's bare drop scoring strips the boss's trait first (Sam's Never Twice would wobble its second drop), and C28's Professor gate reads its PI per segment end.
**Result.** GREEN on all 24 bosses (325/325 each, 7,800 assertions); determinism 32/32; geometry 5/5; margins GREEN; sim_test 14/14; the formation drive 7/7 and the recruit drive 8/8; 16,000-frame soaks against Carter's squad and Itzel's in the sanctioned format, against Sam, and with YURI sealed into R1 against Itzel (Load-Bearing against Debt of Honour across E11; RORY led Marta 7-2 in R2 when the soak ended), and a 60,000-frame recruit soak against Yuri's squad, zero errors. Two older gates moved (REGISTER_COMMANDS_027 command 6); each still proves its claim.

## C33 — The Gym Wall: every stablemate's card hangs on the wall, its proximities suggest the formation's order, the bond lines fill and gild, and nothing on it moves a fight — PROVEN
**Claim.** NP_08 §7 as the sim's own data, and NP_10 §2's "the Gym Wall's card proximities pre-suggest bonded adjacencies". Every card in the stable hangs on a cell of the wall — the club's cards from the cell beside Whitlock's letter in the stable's order, a recruit beside the stablemate whose string brought him, the next free cell otherwise, the locker when the wall is full — and the player pins any card to any free cell. The wall's suggestion is the starters in the order that walks the wall the shortest way: the nearest cards in adjacent slots. The whiteboard offers it before the bell and a board may seal it; pins alone never change the order, and a board sealed otherwise stands whatever the wall says. A Kizuna line runs between every pair of pinned cards with any Kizuna, filled toward the next level and gilded at Sworn.
