Decorations unlock from play — a fight poster for a dual won, a ticket stub the first time a club is faced, a polaroid for every bond level crossed, a recruit's wraps — and hang anywhere on the wall with zero stats: nothing on the wall enters the fight's state, and only a board sealed from its suggestion changes a fight. The save writes NP_11 §9's `gym_wall {layout, decorations}` and a load hangs the same wall.
**Mechanism.** `data/gym_wall.v1.json`: the grid (8 × 4, the first card cell beside the letter), the letter (NP_12A scene 1), the four decorations with what unlocks each (the stub's club from the coach table, an unaffiliated squad its lead's), the band an unlock goes up along, the bounds, the suggestion (`GYM_WALL`, "The Gym Wall", key 6, an unpinned card 100 cells from everything) and `gild_level` 5. In `core.gd`: `GYMWALL` and `gym_wall` (cards on cells, the unpinned, the decorations; outside `hash_state`); `_gw_init` in setup before the world sync; `_gw_pin_new` (beside a named card at the nearest free cell, row-major ties, else the next free cell from the first; full → the locker and `wall_full`); `pin` and `arrange` (refusals named, `card_pinned` / `pin_refused` / `decoration_moved`); `_gw_deco` (`decoration_unlocked`) from `_gw_dual` in `_end_dual` (poster and stub), from `_pay_kizuna` on a level crossing (polaroid) and from `recruit` (his card pinned beside his string's stablemate, his wraps); `_perms`, `_gw_dist`, `_gw_preset` under `order_preset("GYM_WALL")` (every permutation of the board's starters, the shortest straight-line walk between cells, ties to the card order), `_board_check_squad` accepting the archetype; `gym_wall_suggestion`, `gym_wall_view` (the layout's grid, cards, unpinned and lines — level, name, fill, gilded — and the decorations), `gym_wall_restore`, `gym_wall_lines`; `formation_lines` labels a wall board "The Gym Wall" and, before the bell, prints THE GYM WALL SUGGESTS (6). `SNAP_SKIP` and the content stamp carry `GYMWALL`. `sim/save.gd` writes `gym_wall` and a load hangs it back (a save without one hangs its bench after the dressed). `main.gd`: `-- wall=NAME:col:row,...` (with `-- order=GYM_WALL` the board seals the suggestion), 6 on the whiteboard, the GYM WALL lines at DUAL OVER, the ON THE WALL toast, the soak's wall; `tools/drive_formation.gd` checks the suggestion and its seal.
**Test.** `-- smoke` PAC-079 stage: 7 assertions — the data and the default wall (8 × 4; the four on (1,0)–(4,0); the sanctioned eight along the top row and MARTA wrapped to (0,1); the letter alone at (0.5, 0.5); no lines; a changed wall table changing the content stamp); the player's hand (refused "BOB is not in the stable", "(8, 0) is off the wall (8 x 4 cells)", "PRIYA's card is on (2, 0)"; RORY to (0,3) and back; a decoration refused off the wall and one not hanging; the letter moved to (7.5, 5.5); two pins and three refusals as events); the suggestion (MARCUS pinned to (1,1) and PRIYA to (1,2): R1 RORY, R2 MARCUS, R3 PRIYA, a walk of 2.0, JUNE in reserve, on the whiteboard; the card order RORY-PRIYA-MARCUS until it is sealed; sealed, "YOUR BOARD [The Gym Wall]: R1 RORY  R2 MARCUS  R3 PRIYA"; Ambush sealed PRIYA-RORY-MARCUS standing after the same pins, and no suggestion from the bell; the sanctioned five RORY-JUNE-PRIYA-MARCUS-SAM along the top row, walk 4.0, and with SAM pinned under RORY MARCUS-PRIYA-JUNE-RORY-SAM, its mirror tied and the card order breaking the tie); the lines (RORY-PRIYA at 30 Corner Voices 20 %, PRIYA-MARCUS at 150 Sworn and gilded, MARCUS-JUNE at 5 half way to Gymmates, a pair with a card off the wall none; a wall of two cells hanging RORY and PRIYA, MARCUS and JUNE in the locker, two `wall_full`, the suggestion walking 101); decorations from play (a dual won against Yuri's squad: "MILLBROOK vs Prairie Grain Kings - Yuri Kowalchuk" and the stub "Prairie Grain Kings"; the next dual against them lost adding no poster and no stub; as many polaroids as the ledger's pairs hold levels, and "JUNE & RORY - Gymmates" on the crossing; a first dual lost against Sam's squad its stub "True North Club" alone; CARTER signed at (5,0), pinned to (5,2), ADA signed through his string at (5,1) beside him, their wraps "CARTER's wraps" and "ADA's wraps", Carter's squad's stub "Carter Okafor's squad"); zero stats (three cards moved, the letter rehung and three decorations added leave `hash_state` where it was, and 1,200 ticks against Yuri hash identically at every step with the stats and the ledger the same); the save (`gym_wall` with `layout` and `decorations` — six cards, three lines, five decorations — a load's view equal and the text written again byte-equal; a save without the wall hanging CARTER and ADA after the four). The content-table gate counts 22 tables. `-- determinism` adds the Gym Wall's board on the delegated log against Yuri's squad (MARCUS and PRIYA pinned, the suggestion sealed: R1 RORY, R2 MARCUS, R3 PRIYA): identical twice and different from the card order's run; the same order sealed by hand hashing the same, and the run with the wall rearranged and three decorations more hashing the same. `tools/drive_formation.gd` pins MARCUS and PRIYA through the sim before the bell (the greybox has no wall screen), finds THE GYM WALL SUGGESTS (6): R1 RORY  R2 MARCUS  R3 PRIYA on the whiteboard, seals it with 6 ("YOUR BOARD [The Gym Wall]") and, after the bell, finds the suggestion gone: 9 / 9.
**Result.** GREEN on all 24 bosses (332/332 each, 7,968 assertions); determinism 33/33; geometry 5/5; margins GREEN; sim_test 14/14; the formation drive 9/9 and the recruit drive 8/8; 16,000-frame soaks with the wall's suggestion sealed against Yuri's squad (R1 RORY, R2 MARCUS, R3 PRIYA) and in the sanctioned format against Carter's squad with SAM pinned under RORY (MARCUS, PRIYA, JUNE, RORY, SAM), and a save / load soak with two cards pinned by hand (the same hash back at tick 3,000, the pins where they hung), and a 60,000-frame recruit soak against Yuri's squad (the scene's seed is the clock: one run lost a dual — the club's stub, the first two lines — and another lost two — one stub still, and RORY and PRIYA's Gymmates polaroid), zero errors. No older gate moved.

## C34 — The Duo: a Kizuna Lv4 pair relays its named T3 by the bond, the partner's afterimage joining, and it hits as its base — PROVEN
**Claim.** NP_08 §5.2's Lv4 ("named duo T3 unlocked (afterimage variant)") and NP_05 §7 ("bonded pairs get named duo variants with unique VFX") as the sim's own data, with the GDD's §5.2 route to a T3 ("banked relay state or bonded adjacency"). When the fighter out and the one who fought the segment before him are a Duo pair or better, the pair's named T3 is live for his segment — by the bond, CHARGED or not (a CHARGED relay still halves the first cast). The pair's T3 is the Duo variant of a relay T3: the base that names the pair, else the relay T3 of the element both carry, else the ANY-element one; it carries the pair's name, its VFX and camera ids and the partner's afterimage, and hits as its base. LB + its family casts it while the bar can pay it; below that the card's own move answers the motion. Manager mode casts it before the card's own move. The feed, the matchup card and the whiteboard name it. Nothing else about a pair changes: no pair out of order, across a slot or in the first segment relays one, and bosses keep no bonds.
**Mechanism.** `tools/build_hissatsu_library.py` writes `duo_variant` on the two `relay:charged` T3s (afterimage `partner`, `name_rule` "{base} - {a} & {b}", `vfx_set`, `camera_script_id`, `effect_add` — the partner's afterimage state for Coast to Coast, none for Aurora Chain, which carries it — and `pairs`: Whiteout, Chinook Arch, Permafrost, Frost Heave and Snow Squall on Coast to Coast, Freeze-Up on Aurora Chain); North Star's stays null, the festival duos' `sync_500ms` untouched; `tools/hissatsu_audit.py` checks every variant's shape, pair keys and names. `economy.v1.json`'s Kizuna effects gain `"4": {"duo_t3": true}`. In `core.gd`: `duo` (the live relay; in `hash_state`, the snapshot and the DualState's `transform_log`); `duo_level`, `_duo_base`, `duo_move_for`, `_duo_check` at the end of `_load_segment` (after the CHARGED surge, so its price is the segment's), `duo_move`, `cast_move` under `_try_execute_cmd`, `duo_lines`; `castable` letting a Duo T3 past the CHARGED lock; `_start_cast`'s cast event carrying `duo`, `duo_of` and `vfx`, its feed naming the afterimage; the `duo` event; `formation_lines`' DUO RELAYS. `sim/profile.gd` takes a live, affordable Duo T3 before the card's own move. `main.gd`: the matchup card's DUO line, the DUO toast and the gilded afterimage on the cast, the soak's Duo casts; `tools/drive_duo.gd` drives it on the real scene.
**Test.** `-- smoke` PAC-080 stage: 5 assertions — the data (the variants: Coast to Coast 5 pairs, Aurora Chain 1, the festival duos `sync_500ms`, North Star none; Lv4 carries `duo_t3`; RORY → PRIYA "Whiteout / Relay: Coast to Coast / T3 HAYMAKER / vfx_duo_any / DMG+DISPLACE+STATE:afterimage_partner", RORY → JUNE "Freeze-Up / Relay: Aurora Chain / T3 ARC / vfx_duo_frost", RORY → SAM "Aurora Chain - RORY & SAM" (both FROST, unnamed), MARCUS → SAM "Coast to Coast - MARCUS & SAM" (nothing shared), a card with himself none); the relay (RORY and PRIYA at 89: R2 PRIYA carries nothing; at 90: nothing in R1, Whiteout in R2 with one `duo` event and "DUO :: RORY -> PRIYA (Lv4 Duo): WHITEOUT is live - LB + HAYMAKER, a T3 at 45 Tension, RORY's afterimage joining (Relay: Coast to Coast)"; at 150 Lv5 and Whiteout still; R3 MARCUS after PRIYA nothing; JUNE swapped into R2 after RORY at Duo: Freeze-Up; PRIYA sent first: Whiteout for RORY in R2, PRIYA the partner; RORY and MARCUS at 150 across PRIYA: nothing in R2 or R3); the cast (PRIYA at 60: Whiteout armed with no CHARGED relay, afterimage RORY, pair PRIYA|RORY, `vfx_duo_any`, 15 left, "PRIYA ARMS WHITEOUT (T3 HAYMAKER, 45 Tension) - DUO: RORY's afterimage joins (Relay: Coast to Coast)"; at 89 the same press a punch; RORY after PRIYA at 60 / 40 / 20: Whiteout, his own Aurora Borealis Breaker, a punch; CHARGED at 30: Whiteout, 7.5 left); the effect (Whiteout and a carried Relay: Coast to Coast cast by the same PRIYA in the same spot: 27.3273 composure and the same push to (0, −2.17) each, one displacement each; the afterimage state on Whiteout's only); delegated and on record (the profile's first cast in R2 Whiteout, at 89 Marginalia; the whiteboard "DUO RELAYS: R1 RORY -> R2 PRIYA (Whiteout)", after Ambush "R1 PRIYA -> R2 RORY (Whiteout)"; a DualState taken in R2 restoring Whiteout into a sim built on another boss and seed, on the same hash). `-- determinism` adds RORY and PRIYA at Duo on the delegated log against Yuri's squad: Whiteout live in R2 and cast by the profile with RORY's afterimage; identical twice and different from the same log at Adjacent Trust, where no Duo is live (PRIYA cast Marginalia there until C35; now, out CHARGED, the relay's plain Coast to Coast). `tools/drive_formation.gd` seeds the pair at Duo and finds the whiteboard's DUO RELAYS on the card order and the Relay Ladder, none on the Gym Wall's order: 12 / 12. `tools/drive_duo.gd` loads the real scene with the pair seeded: R1 RORY carries none; R2 PRIYA's matchup card reads "DUO: RORY -> PRIYA (Lv4) - WHITEOUT live: LB + HAYMAKER, 45 Tension (Relay: Coast to Coast)"; the relay's cast arms Whiteout, the scene spawns RORY's afterimage gilded and the HUD toasts "DUO  WHITEOUT  - PRIYA & RORY (Relay: Coast to Coast)"; it resolves on him; R3 MARCUS carries none: 5 / 5. `python3 tools/hissatsu_audit.py`: duo variants (2 relay T3s, 6 named pairs) GREEN, and RED on a string variant off a duo move, a pair key out of order or a name shared.
**Result.** GREEN on all 24 bosses (337/337 each, 8,088 assertions); determinism 34/34; geometry 5/5; margins GREEN; sim_test 14/14; the hissatsu audit GREEN; the formation drive 12/12, the Duo drive 5/5 and the recruit drive 8/8; 16,000-frame soaks with `-- demo duo` and RORY and PRIYA at Duo (the profile casting Whiteout, RORY's afterimage gilded), the same pair on the plain demo (one run sending PRIYA second with Whiteout live, another swapping JUNE in with none), RORY and JUNE at Duo in the sanctioned format, the Relay Ladder with two Duo pairs against Vey's corner (the whiteboard's two DUO RELAYS, one live in R3) and a save / load with the pair at Sworn (the same hash back at tick 3,000), zero errors. No older gate moved.

## C35 — The relay's own T3: whoever enters CHARGED has the relay's T3s opened for his segment, each by its own motion, North Star on a FULL RELAY — PROVEN
**Claim.** NP_05 §6 ("the next fighter out inherits … T3 moves unlocked for their segment") in play, whatever the fighter's card carries. The CHARGED entrance opens the library's relay T3s — Coast to Coast and Aurora Chain — and, on a FULL RELAY only, the chain's own North Star (NP_05 §10: "FULL RELAY exclusive"; a card that carries North Star casts it on a FULL RELAY only). Each answers its own family's motion (HAYMAKER, ARC, RISE: NP_05 §4) while the bar can pay it, the previous fighter's afterimage joining (§7); below its price the card's own move answers. A Duo's named T3 (C34) stands in for its base. A stolen CHARGED (Borrowed Blood's `steals_charged_state`) closes the relay; the Duo, the bond's, stays. Manager mode casts the Duo's first, else the open T3 with the most damage in it. The feed, the matchup card and the whiteboard name what the relay opens.
**Mechanism.** `tactics.v1.json`'s corner gains `relay_t3_opens` (`relay:charged`), `full_relay_t3_opens` (`full_relay_exclusive`) and the rule's text — the library's own source tags. In `core.gd`: `relay_moves` (the open T3s, a Duo's variant in its base's place), `cast_move` (the Duo's, then the relay's, then the card's own), `castable` (a FULL RELAY move off a FULL RELAY refused), `_t3_power` and `t3_pick` (the Duo's, else the open T3 with the largest DMG multiplier — North Star's base and the chain's links — times the same-element spike), `relay_line`, `_relay_check` at the end of `_load_segment` (the `relay_open` event, "RELAY :: …" on the feed), `formation_lines`' RELAY OPENS. `sim/profile.gd` takes `t3_pick`. `main.gd`: the matchup card's RELAY line; the demo no longer hands PRIYA's card North Star; `-- demo seg2` seeds the chain to a FULL RELAY so the relay opens it; the help lines. `tools/drive_duo.gd` ends R2 CHARGED and checks R3's RELAY line.
**Test.** `-- smoke` PAC-081 stage: 4 assertions — the data and the opening (the sources: `relay:charged` Coast to Coast and Aurora Chain, `full_relay_exclusive` North Star; R2 PRIYA out CHARGED, her card Marginalia, opens Coast to Coast and Aurora Chain — one `relay_open` event, families HAYMAKER / ARC, afterimage RORY, "RELAY :: PRIYA opens Relay: Coast to Coast (LB + HAYMAKER, 22), Relay: Aurora Chain (LB + ARC, 22) - RORY's afterimage joins"; not CHARGED, nothing; the sanctioned chain at two links the same two, at three — FULL RELAY, R4 MARCUS — North Star, Coast to Coast, Aurora Chain); the motion (CHARGED PRIYA at 60: HAYMAKER Coast to Coast with RORY's afterimage, ARC Aurora Chain, PISTON her own Marginalia, RISE a punch; not CHARGED, HAYMAKER a punch; RORY out CHARGED after PRIYA at 30 / 20 / 10: Coast to Coast, his own Aurora Borealis Breaker, a punch; R4 MARCUS's RISE on the FULL RELAY North Star, 84.000 composure at three links and 100.800 at four — ×1.200, his wall gauge set aside); the Duo and the steal (at Duo the relay opens Whiteout and Aurora Chain, HAYMAKER Whiteout, ARC Aurora Chain; stolen, the unlock off and nothing open, ARC a punch, HAYMAKER still Whiteout; stolen without a Duo, HAYMAKER a punch); manager mode and on record (the profile's picks: PRIYA Coast to Coast, RORY — FROST — Aurora Chain, R4 MARCUS North Star, at Duo Whiteout, not CHARGED none; delegated, PRIYA's first cast out CHARGED Coast to Coast; the whiteboard "RELAY OPENS (R2 PRIYA, chain 1): Relay: Coast to Coast (LB + HAYMAKER, 22), …"; a DualState on the FULL RELAY restoring its three into a sim built on another boss and seed, on the same hash). Two older gates moved: C10's North Star gate now needs the FULL RELAY as well as the unlock and measures at three links (1.6 + 0.8 × 3), and C12's afterimage gate casts the relay's own Coast to Coast from PRIYA's unmodified card. `-- determinism` adds the relay on the delegated log against Yuri's squad: R2 PRIYA and R3 JUNE out CHARGED with the relay open, JUNE casting Aurora Chain with PRIYA's afterimage; identical twice and different from the same log with the relay's T3s closed (the build before C35). `tools/drive_duo.gd` adds R3 MARCUS out CHARGED with "RELAY (chain 1): Relay: Coast to Coast (LB + HAYMAKER, 25), Relay: Aurora Chain (LB + ARC, 25)" on the matchup card: 6 / 6.
**Result.** GREEN on all 24 bosses (341/341 each, 8,184 assertions); determinism 35/35; geometry 5/5; margins GREEN; sim_test 14/14; the hissatsu audit GREEN; the formation drive 12/12, the Duo drive 6/6 and the recruit drive 8/8; two runs each (the scene's seed is the clock) of `-- demo seg2 boss=0` (the FULL RELAY seeded: North Star cast by the relay, two afterimages in one run's 2,997 ticks, one in the other's), 16,000-frame soaks against Yuri's squad (the relay opened to R2 in both, to R3 too in one) and in the sanctioned format against Carter's squad (opened once in each) and `-- demo duo` at Duo (Whiteout three times with the relay opened in R3, and once), zero errors. Two older gates moved and the logs' figures with them (REGISTER_COMMANDS_030 commands 6 and 7).

## C36 — In the Zone: the SURGE spends the whole bar on the first transformation tier, the world waits out its cinematic, and for 20 s the fight bends as NP_09 §3 says — PROVEN
**Claim.** NP_09 §1's shared rules and §3's In the Zone run on the sim. The SURGE motion spends the whole team bar — 100, the FULL RELAY's 75 — and empties it, always; it needs the Zone unlocked (fighter level 20 and the Provincials semifinal's story flag, NP_12 §4 beat 6) and is allowed once per fighter per dual, never in a scrap or spar. The cinematic is a camera hold the whole world waits out, so nothing interrupts it. Then 20 s of fight time: the world slows for the player (his timers at ×0.75), the first incoming punch of every 3 s is slipped automatically, stamina is frozen where it stood (no drain, no regen), every counter window is 200 ms wider and punches are 15 % faster. Then the Comedown: 10 s at 85 % stats, stamina moving again from where it was. The Zone and the Comedown end with the segment. Manager mode never SURGEs on its own; the player's SURGE passes through delegation. Spirit Manifestation, Inheritance and Awakening are named and not run (since C37 the Manifest runs, since C38 Inheritance, since C39 Awakening); enemy transforms stay authored per boss (since C40 they run where authored).
**Mechanism.** `data/transformations.v1.json`: `shared` (cost 100, the FULL RELAY's price from `tactics.v1.json`'s `full_relay_t4_cost`, `empties_bar`, the SURGE motion, `cinematic_ms` 2500, one per fighter per dual, the Comedown 10 s at 85 %) and `tiers` (the Zone executed — 20 s; `time_scale` 0.75, `auto_slip_every_s` 3, `stamina_frozen`, `counter_window_plus_ms` 200, `punch_speed_pct` 15; unlocked at level 20 and `story:provincials_semifinal`; the other three `executed: false`). In `core.gd`: `TRANS`; `setup(..., gym_d, trans_d, board_d)`; `tform` (the live Zone: tier, who, clock, start, frozen stamina, the auto-slip's window), `comedown` (clock, the stats kept), `transformed` (this dual's, reset each dual); `transform_tier`, `transform_cost`, `transform_why`, `transform` (the bar emptied, `freeze_t` for the camera hold, the `zone` event and feed), `_zone_tick` after the per-tick timers (the Zone's clock in fight time, the frozen stamina, the end into the Comedown with every stat ×0.85, the Comedown's end restoring them; `zone_end` and `comedown_end`), `_zone_fx`, `_zone_punch_mult` (startup and recovery ÷ 1.15), `zone_line`; the boss's step rate ×0.75 beside the flash window's ×0.6; `_auto_slips` slipping the first punch of each 3 s window (`evade` with `ZONE_SLIP`); `_window` +200 ms; `_sfloor` holding the frozen stamina against every drain; the `surge` intent; `seed_levels` (`-- level=`); `tform`, `comedown` and `transformed` in `hash_state` and the DualState's `transform_log`; `SNAP_SKIP` and the content stamp carry `TRANS`. `sim/profile.gd` passes `surge` through. `sim/save.gd` builds with the table. `main.gd`: H + Y and the pad's LB + both sticks; `-- level=`; `-- demo zone`; the Zone and Comedown toasts and HUD line; RORY lit in his element in the Zone, dimmed in the Comedown. `tools/drive_zone.gd` presses the keys on the real scene.
**Test.** `-- smoke` PAC-082 stage: 5 assertions — the data and the unlock (the shared rules and the Zone's five effects as the table has them, the other three tiers named and not run (two since C37, one since C38, none since C39; the gate moved with each), the table in the content stamp; RORY at level 19 "RORY is level 19 - the Zone opens at 20", at 20 without the flag "the Zone is taught at the Provincials semifinal (story:provincials_semifinal)", at 20 with it the Zone); the SURGE (at 99 "the bar is at 99 - a transformation takes the whole bar (100)"; at 100 the bar to 0 and a 300-tick camera hold in which his timers and the Zone's clock stand still, the Zone counted from its end, "IN THE ZONE :: RORY - the whole bar (100 of 100); 20s: the world at x0.75, the first punch every 3s slipped, stamina frozen at 100, counter windows +200ms, punches +15% speed"; the sanctioned R4 on a FULL RELAY 75 from 80, the bar to 0; again "RORY is already in the Zone", after it "RORY has transformed this dual (NP_09 s1: one per fighter per dual)", a new dual open again, a spar "no transformation in a spar"); the effects, each against the same sim without the Zone (his timers 300 of 400 ticks against 400; three R_HOOKs: slipped, landed, and 3 s on slipped again, against three landed; stamina 60.04 through four power hookercuts and two seconds' rest against 60 → 14.09 → 21.36; the slip and perfect-block windows +24 ticks; R_STRAIGHT's startup 23 against 26 and its busy 54 against 62); the end (in the Zone at 19.99 s, the Comedown at 20: PWR 45 → 38.25, "COMEDOWN 10.0s (85%)", stamina 100 → 88.49 on a power hookercut; 10 s on PWR 45; the events `zone_end`, `comedown_end`; a segment ended mid-Zone sending PRIYA out with neither); the manager and the record (delegated with the Zone unlocked and a full bar, no SURGE of his own in 1,200 ticks; the manager's SURGE passing through; "IN THE ZONE 15.8s | stamina frozen 100 | auto-slip ready"; a DualState mid-Zone restoring the same line on the same hash, the record {RORY: zone}; R2 PRIYA free to SURGE). The content-table gate counts 23 tables (24 since C41's hybrid table; the gate moved with it). `-- determinism` adds In the Zone on the scripted log against Yuri's squad: the bar topped up at tick 3,000 and RORY's SURGE — the Zone at 3,001, seven auto-slips, its end at 6,069 (the hitstops of the fight pause its clock) and the Comedown's at 7,702; identical twice and different from the same log with the bar topped up and no SURGE. `tools/drive_zone.gd` loads the real scene with RORY at level 20 and the flag: H + Y with an empty bar refused on the HUD; with the bar full the SURGE, its toast and the camera hold; after the hold the HUD's Zone line and RORY lit; 20 s on the Comedown's line and toast, RORY dimmed and unlit; 10 s more, no line: 6 / 6.
**Result.** GREEN on all 24 bosses (346/346 each, 8,304 assertions); determinism 36/36; geometry 5/5; margins GREEN; sim_test 14/14; the hissatsu audit GREEN; the Zone drive 6/6, the formation drive 12/12, the Duo drive 6/6 and the recruit drive 8/8; two runs each (the scene's seed is the clock) of 16,000-frame `-- demo zone` soaks with the fighters at level 20 and the flag against Yuri's squad (two Zones and two Comedowns in one, four Zones across two duals in the other), in the sanctioned format against Carter's squad (three SURGEs in each) and with a save / load (the same hash back at tick 3,000 in both), zero script errors. No older gate moved.

## C37 — Spirit Manifestation: the same SURGE casts the Manifest where the card has earned it, and for 30 s the fighter hits x1.4, echoes every clean punch, and pays his hissatsu from the manifest's own meter — PROVEN
**Claim.** NP_09 §4's Spirit Manifestation runs on the sim, on §1's shared rules. The SURGE casts the highest tier the fighter has unlocked — the Manifest over the Zone — for the whole bar, the same camera hold, and one transformation of any tier per fighter per dual. A named character unlocks his manifest by his own Arc 2 story flag (a manifest is earned in fiction); a generic stablemate — a card marked `generic` — by level 35 and a Zone mastery flag, taking his element's generic manifest; neither path opens the other's. Then 30 s of fight time: every composure hit he deals ×1.4 — his punches, clean or chipped through a guard, and his hissatsu's damage packets; his hissatsu paid from a private 50-point manifest meter instead of the team bar (each move's own price, CHARGED half and element edge as ever; SAVE THE BAR does not hold the meter; short of the price the motion throws its punch whatever the team bar holds; a cast broken in its window gives half back to the meter); the manifest's echo of every punch that lands clean, 0.3 s of fight time later, for 30 % of it — a drop or the segment's end cancels an echo in flight, his timed wall stops it, his gauge takes it by the punch's own source, and it earns and scores nothing; his DISPLACE packets at twice their metres. Then §1's Comedown, 10 s at 85 %; the meter's remainder leaves with the manifest. Manager mode never SURGEs on its own; manifested by the manager's SURGE, the card spends the meter. The named figures are the content lane's; the Manifest Clash is named and not run (it runs since C40, against an authored enemy Manifest).
**Mechanism.** `data/transformations.v1.json`'s `manifest` tier: `executed`, 30 s, `effects` (`damage_mult` 1.4, `manifest_meter` 50, `echo_delay_s` 0.3, `echo_pct` 30, `displace_mult` 2), the shared Comedown, `unlock` by the card (`named`: `story:manifest:{name}`; `generic`: level 35 and `mastery:zone:{name}`), `manifests` (the named figures' placeholder and an empty `figures` map for the content lane; one element-generic manifest per element, names placeholders, figures null), `clash` (`executed: false`, 5 s at ×0.5), `words` for the HUD and the feed (the Zone's too). In `core.gd`: `_tier_unlocked` (the unlock by the card) under `transform_tier`; `manifest_of`; `_tier_word`; `transform` giving the Manifest's `tform` its `manifest`, `meter`, `meter_max` and `echoes`, and its feed; `_manifest_dmg` in `_land_player_punch` and `_fx_dmg` (the player's side only); `_echo_queue` after a clean punch and before its composure hit, `_echo_land` from `_zone_tick` (before the tier's clock; a drop clears the echoes in `_drop`); `_resolve_cast`'s DISPLACE ×`displace_mult` for the player; `purse` and `can_pay` in place of every "the bar can pay it and it is not held" test (`_try_execute_cmd`, the WALL motion, `cast_move`, `t3_pick`); `_start_cast` paying from the meter and marking the cast's purse, `_interrupt_cast` refunding to it, the ARMS feed naming it; `transform_why`, `_zone_tick`'s end and `zone_line` in the tier's words, the MANIFEST line. The echoes and the meter live in `tform`, so `hash_state`, the snapshot and the DualState carry them unchanged. `sim/profile.gd` tests `can_pay` and spends the meter as an opening. `main.gd`: the toast naming the manifest and its meter, the figure behind the fighter pulsing on each `echo`, RORY lit brighter, the hissatsu line priced from the meter, `-- demo manifest`, the soak's transforms and echoes, the help lines. `tools/drive_manifest.gd` drives it on the real scene.
**Test.** `-- smoke` PAC-083 stage: 5 assertions — the data and the unlock (tier 3, 30 s, the five effects, the Comedown the shared one, the two unlock paths, the Clash at 5 s ×0.5 (not run; run since C40, the gate moved with it), two tiers still named and not run (one since C38, none since C39; the gate moved with each), the four generic manifests; RORY with the Zone only the Zone, with his Arc 2 flag the Manifest, with the flag alone at level 1 the Manifest, at 35 with a Zone mastery flag the Zone; a generic GALE card at 35 with his mastery flag the Manifest, at 34 the Zone, without the flag the Zone, with a named flag the Zone; "RORY's manifest" and "the Gale manifest"); the SURGE (the Manifest, the bar to 0, the meter 50, 3,600 ticks after a 300-tick hold, the record {RORY: manifest}, "SPIRIT MANIFESTATION :: RORY - the whole bar (100 of 100); 30s: RORY's manifest - all damage x1.40, hissatsu from its own 50-point meter, every clean punch echoed at 0.3s for +30%, displacement x2"; again "RORY is already manifested"; after it, the Zone unlocked beneath it, "RORY has transformed this dual (NP_09 s1: one per fighter per dual)"; a new dual open again); the effects, each against the same sim without the Manifest (R_STRAIGHT 12.6 against 9, no echo by 35 ticks and one at the 36th for 3.78, none without; a blocked straight no echo; one in flight, none after a drop; Aurora Borealis Breaker 58.5585 against 41.8275 and its ropes displacement 2.86 m against 1.43, no echo); the meter against the team bar (the price 31.5 off the meter with the team bar at 40 untouched, the control's off the bar; ## C38 — Inheritance: the SURGE takes on a source's house style — his trait, his natives from a private meter, his dialect — for 25 s, and the enemy misreads every punch — PROVEN
**Claim.** NP_09 §5's Inheritance runs on the sim, on §1's rules. The SURGE casts it when the fighter has a source: a story-flagged legacy (an authored gift), a stablemate of another house at Sworn (NP_08 §5.2's Lv5, the deepest bond first), or — a dual-house card — his own second house, which costs 75 ("it was always theirs"). Inheritance stands over the Manifest and the Zone, one transformation per fighter per dual. For 25 s of fight time the caster carries the source's house trait beside his own, a stat trait scaling his stats while he wears it; the source's natives answer their own motions — after any open T3, before the card's own move, a WALL native on the WALL motion — paid from a private 50-point meter while the card's own move and the relay still pay the team bar; and the source's dialect, so every read the enemy makes of his punches is a misread: no dodge, no guard. His own stats, style package and house rings stay his. Then §1's Comedown, and his own dialect scrambled 10 s: a feint he sells draws nothing. Manager mode never SURGEs on its own; inheriting by the manager's call, the card casts the strongest native the meter can pay. A legacy source and a dual-house card are data the content lane writes; none is authored yet.

## C39 — Awakening: the story gates the summit, and the SURGE stacks a chosen prior tier at 75 % with the rings' caps at ×1.75 and a drop refunding the bar — and a card chooses what its SURGE casts — PROVEN
**Claim.** NP_09 §6's Awakening runs on the sim, on §1's rules, and completes the ladder. It is gated per character by the story: his house's SECRET learned and his personal Arc 3 trial, and for the protagonist, RORY, the sealed house resolved as well, with a prior tier unlocked for it to stack. For 40 s of fight time it stacks a chosen prior tier's effects at 75 % strength — the Zone's, the Manifest's or Inheritance's, each effect by its kind: a multiplier's reach, an amount, a period's frequency, a state's fraction, a timing kept — the form the card's choice when he has that tier, else his highest prior tier. For him alone while Awakened the rings' caps rise from ×1.5 to ×1.75 (NP_06 §4's one exception), and every drop he scores puts 15 Tension back on the team bar. The +1 hybrid slot opens on nothing, since no hybrid technique exists (since C41 it holds one of his hybrids when he has one). Then §1's Comedown, the stacked tier's own riders staying with that tier. A card also chooses, between duals, what its SURGE casts: a lower tier he has unlocked, else his highest. The save carries both choices, and the matchup card names the SURGE.
**Mechanism.** `data/transformations.v1.json`'s `awakening` tier: `executed`, 40 s, `effects` (`stack_pct` 75, `ring_cap` 1.75, `drop_refund` 15, `hybrid_slot` 1), `stack_rules` (15 effects by kind: `mult`, `add`, `period`, `fraction`, `keep`), the standard Comedown, `words`, `unlock` (`awaken`; the flags `secret:{name}` and `story:trial:{name}`; the protagonist RORY's `story:sealed_house_resolved`; `needs_form`), `choice` (`surge_tier`, `awakening_form`, between duals). In `core.gd`: `_awaken_gate` and `awaken_form` under `_tier_unlocked`'s `awaken`; `transform_tier` honouring the card's `surge_tier`; `choose_surge(name, "tier" | "form", tier)` between duals, refusing a tier he lacks or a form that is not a prior tier; `surge_line`; `_stack_fx` scaling the chosen tier's effects, `transform` keeping them in `tform["fx"]` with the `form` and the feed's `_form_text`, `_zone_fx` reading them; `_frac` for a state's strength (stamina part-frozen in `_zone_tick`, the full freeze alone raising `_sfloor`; the misread's odds in `_misread_frac`, an extra roll only below 100 %; the inherited trait's `x` at 0.75, `_tx` returning a float); `_tmult(base, x, F)` and `hiss_mult` capped by `_cap_of` (the Awakened player's ×1.75, everyone else's ×1.5); `_drop`'s refund and its `refund` event; the end feed and `zone_line`'s AWAKENED head over the stacked tier's line. `sim/save.gd`: the two choices in `CARD_KEYS` and the Fighter record. `main.gd`: `-- surge=` and `-- form=`, `-- demo awaken`, the matchup card's SURGE line, the toasts, the ring floor lit in his element and the fighter at his brightest, the soak's forms, the help lines. `tools/drive_awaken.gd` drives it on the real scene.
**Test.** `-- smoke` PAC-085 stage: 5 assertions — the data and the gate (tier 5, 40 s, the four effects, 15 stacking rules, the standard Comedown, the gate's flags and the protagonist's, every tier run; RORY with the Zone only the Zone, with his SECRET and trial still the Zone, with the sealed house too Awakening stacking the Zone; PRIYA with her own two flags Awakening, with nothing to stack no tier; with the Manifest the form the Manifest, Sworn with SAM Inheritance, his choice the Zone the Zone, his choice a Manifest he lacks the Zone); the SURGE and the choice (Awakened with the Manifest: the bar to 0, 4,800 ticks after the 300-tick hold, the record {RORY: awakening}, the meter 37.5, damage ×1.3, echo 22.5 %, delay 0.3 s, displacement ×1.75, cap 1.75, refund 15; "AWAKENING :: RORY - the whole bar (100 of 100); 40s: Spirit Manifestation at 75% (RORY's manifest - damage x1.3, hissatsu from a 37.5-point meter, clean punches echoed +22.5%, displacement x1.75); the rings' caps x1.75; every drop scored +15 Tension back; the hybrid slot open on nothing"; again "RORY is already awakened"; the choice in the fight "between duals only"; between duals the Zone as his SURGE yes, Awakening as a form "an Awakening stacks a prior tier - the Zone, the Manifest or Inheritance", PRIYA's Manifest "PRIYA has not unlocked Spirit Manifestation", the Manifest as his form yes; "SURGE: In the Zone (100) - his choice"; the save's record and card carrying both; back to his highest "SURGE: Awakening (100) - Manifest-Awakening at 75%"; the next dual's SURGE the Zone); the effects, against full strength and without (a straight 11.7 against the Manifest's 12.6 and 9 plain, the echo 2.6325; his timers 325 against 400; four punches at him slipped, landed, 3 s on landed, 1 s more slipped, against four landed; four power hookercuts 11.53 stamina against 45.91; the slip window +18 ticks; Inheritance-Awakening's 24 straights 18 misreads and 2 dodges against 20 dodges; Never Twice at 0.75 until 39.99 s; at 40 s the trait gone, PWR ×0.85, no scramble, "THE AWAKENING ENDS - RORY comes down from the Inheritance-Awakening: 10s at 85%", "COMEDOWN 10.0s (85%)"; a drop in the Comedown 10 and no refund; ## C40 — The enemy's transformations: a boss transforms only where his pattern authors it, the player feels it coming and weathers it, and two manifests clash — PROVEN
**Claim.** NP_09 §1's enemy transforms run on the sim. They are authored per boss, never systemic.
- A line in the boss's pattern names its trigger (the phase vocabulary), its tier (the Zone or the Manifest, the tiers a boss can cast), its tell (at least the ARMORED floor, 1000 ms) and the line said as it starts. It runs once per boss per dual.
- He gathers between his actions: the tell runs on fight time behind his guard, and he neither attacks nor answers a hook. The player feels it coming.
- Then comes §1's shared camera hold, his bar is emptied, and his tier runs:
  - his Zone: his timers at 1/0.75 outside his tells (the tells keep C4's floors), his acting +15 %, the first player punch of every 3 s slipped, his stamina frozen;
  - his Manifest: his damage ×1.4, his clean punches echoed at 0.3 s for 30 %, his displacement ×2, and no meter.
- His Comedown, 10 s at 85 %, is the player's window. All of it ends with his segment.

§7's weathering is the player's counterplay:
- Every perfect block, slip or Clash exchange won shaves 1 s off the boss's transformation, and once one has, his damage is halved for the rest of it.
- If the player weathers it to its end without answering (no transformation of his own while it ran) and without being knocked down, the feat Stood in the Storm goes on the ledger.

§4's Manifest Clash runs when both fighters are manifested:
- The fight stops for 5 s at ×0.5: five alternating exchanges, the enemy's first.
- Each window is 360 ms either side of its cue, and a press outside it loses the exchange.
- Three exchanges win it: the winner's manifest ends the loser's, into the Comedown.

The roster's one authored line is Ioan Vasile's (NP_04B §24's "T4 usage at the 50% mark").

**Mechanism.**
- **The compiler** (`pbn_compiler.py`, identical in `tools/`):
  - The line `TRANSFORM @ <cond>: <tier> ["<manifest name>"] TELL(<motif>, <ms>ms) "<line>"` compiles into the pattern's `transform` block.
  - Its rails are read from the table: a tier a boss can cast, the tell floor, a manifest named, one line per boss. `--selftest` seeds ten violation classes; `--margins` lists the transformation tells.
  - `pbn/ioan_vasile.pbn` carries the line, compiled to `data/patterns/ioan_vasile.json` and the engine copy.
- **The table** (`data/transformations.v1.json`, both copies):
  - `boss`: the tiers, `tell_floor_ms` 1000, `per_boss_per_dual` 1, the grammar and the effects' readings;
  - `shared.weathering`: the actions, `shave_s` 1, `damage_mult` 0.5 and the feat;
  - the Manifest's `clash`, now executed: `exchanges` 5, `window_ms` 180, `to_win` 3, `delegated_hit_pct` 60.
- **The sim** (`core.gd`):
  - `btsurge`, `btform`, `bcomedown`, `btransformed` and `mclash` sit in the hash and the snapshot, reset at the dual and at the segment.
  - `_boss_transform_check` runs between his actions. While he gathers, `_fire_event`'s hooks, his desperation and his survival grab are held.
  - `_boss_transform`, `_btform_tick` (the gathering, the clock on fight time, his echoes, his frozen stamina, the Comedown, the Clash's start), `_boss_tform_end` and `_feat`.
  - His Zone acts in the boss step's rate, `_auto_slips` and `_sfloor`. His Manifest acts in `_land_boss_punch`, `_fx_dmg`, DISPLACE and `_becho_queue`.
  - `_weather` fires on the player's perfect block, slip and auto-slip. A transformation of the player's own marks his answered.
  - `_clash_start`, `_clash_step` and `_clash_end` run in a CLASH mode that holds the fight, the clock and both manifests.
  - Also: `bzone_line`, `_bword` (the enemy side's words, no pronoun), the DualState's `transform_log.away`, and `btransform_on` for the tests.
- **The scene** (`main.gd`):
  - the GATHERING / ENEMY ZONE / ENEMY MANIFEST / ENEMY COMEDOWN / Clash lines and their toasts;
  - the boss swelling as he gathers, lit in his element while transformed with his manifest figure behind him, and dimmed in his Comedown;
  - CLASH input through the ordinary keys;
  - the aids `-- btransform=` and `-- demo clash`, the help lines, and the soak's summary.
- **The drives:** `tools/drive_bzone.gd` and `tools/drive_clash.gd`.

**Test.** `-- smoke` PAC-086 stage: 5 assertions.
1. **The data and Vasile's line.**
   - The table: castable ["zone", "manifest"], the 1000 ms floor, one per boss per dual; the Clash executed at 5 exchanges, 180 ms, 3 to win, 60 %, 5 s ×0.5; the weathering's actions, 1 s, ×0.5, the feat.
   - Authored: ["ioan_vasile"] only, `{composure<50, zone, breath_held 1500 ms, the line}`.
   - At 51 % composure nothing. At 49 %: the tell 180 ticks, the state SURGE, his guard 180, one `boss_transform_tell` event carrying the line and motif, "GATHERING 1.5s | VEY: he has stopped breathing out loud - that is his Zone coming. Weather it."
   - 180 ticks of gathering under six jabs: his state held, no answer, the sensors still firing.
   - Then the 300-tick hold, his bar 60 → 0, his Zone 2,400 ticks, "ENEMY ZONE 20.0s | weathered 0 (-0.0s)".
   - 31 s on, all over. Under the trigger again nothing; the next dual he gathers again.
2. **His Zone against his line stripped.**
   - His timers idle, in a tell and acting: 533 / 400 / 613 of 400, against 400 each.
   - Four punches at him: slip, hit, 3 s on slip, hit, against four hits.
   - His stamina after −30: 0 against −29.97.
   - His Comedown: 1,200 ticks, PWR and SPD ×0.85, "ENEMY COMEDOWN 10.0s (85%) - your window", "THE ENEMY'S ZONE ENDS - ioan_vasile comes down: 10s at 85% - YOUR WINDOW (weathered 0 …", no feat unweathered.
   - 1,200 fight ticks on (hitstop aside), his stats back, one `boss_comedown_end`, no line.
3. **His Manifest**, authored on Moira MacIsaac at 80 % for the test.
   - "ENEMY MANIFEST 30.0s | weathered 0 (-0.0s) | the test manifest", no meter.
   - A clean hook 20.16 and its echo on the player 6.048, against 14.4 and none.
   - Her Undertow: displacement 4.5 m against 2.25 m, damage 65.52 against 46.8.
   - A drop clears the queued echo.
   - A line naming the Awakening never gathers.
4. **The Clash.**
   - 301 ticks after RORY's SURGE: "MANIFEST CLASH 1/5 | BLOCK or SLIP in 0.50s | won 0 lost 0", cues 60 / 180 / 300 / 420 / 540 of 600, defend first, the window 43 ticks.
   - Perfect: 5 of 5 won. Her manifest suppressed into her Comedown, five weatherings, no feat. The fight clock, her manifest's and his own all waited.
   - None: 5 missed. Early: 5 early. Both lost, his manifest into his Comedown, hers running on.
   - The window's far edge and a slip: 3 of 5, won. Two: lost.
   - The delegated card with the pad mashed: five results, never early.
5. **Weathering Vasile's Zone.**
   - His hook 14.4, as without his line. A perfect block: 120 ticks off, "ENEMY ZONE 19.0s | weathered 1 (-1.0s) | damage to you halved", his hook after 7.2. A slip: 120 more.
   - Run to its end: weathered 2, stood. The feat event "Stood in the Storm", the ledger `{count 1, day 1, last ioan_vasile}`, "STOOD IN THE STORM - RORY weathered ioan_vasile's transformation without answering".
   - Answered (RORY's SURGE into it): weathered 1, no feat.
   - Knocked down in it and up on the count: no feat.
   - A DualState mid-Zone restored on the same hash, with his side readable; both run to the feat on the same hash; the feat carried through the save.

**Gates moved:**
- The Manifest's data gate: the Clash executed.
- The smoke helper `boss_desperation` spends an authored transformation first, since it would come before his desperation.
- The smoke's generic stages keep an authored line unrun, as the wall gauge is stripped for them.

**Determinism and the drives:**

## C41 — Hybrid research: a Sworn pair of two houses forges a T3 of its own, strongest when relayed between them — PROVEN
**Claim.** NP_08 §6's cross-house hybrid research runs on the sim's ledgers and its relay.

**The requirement.**
- A Sworn pair (economy's Kizuna effect `hybrid`) from two houses.
- Each fighter has a native of his house learned: the house's natives when NATIVE, its SECRET with his `secret:<NAME>` flag, the native his card carries.
- The pair forges once, at a Forge event between duals, paying the reward ledger's Spirits and Beans (40 and 30, a first draft; NP_11 §6 names both currencies' Forge sink).
- Across E07 with Q-NGUYEN-ROY done (`quest:third_bridge`), the pairing forges a level early, at Duo, for half.
- Eight a save.

**The output** is a hissatsu record (NP_11 §9's schema) unique to the pair:
- the library's authored hybrid of its two houses (Two Rivers, Grain & Snow) while no pair has claimed it;
- else one composed from both houses' natives:
  - the family and the damage of the stronger learned native, +0.1, over the T3 floor 2.4;
  - from each house, the first non-damage, non-wall effect the fighter knows, its seconds capped at 2;
  - 2100 ms + 100 a tag;
  - the houses' motifs for the name, the pair's names after a name already forged.
- The composer run on Grain & Snow's parents rebuilds the authored record field for field.

**Either of the pair casts it** as NP_05 §3's T3, "CHARGED or bonded adjacency":
- relayed between them (his partner fought the segment before): the strongest, the partner's afterimage joining at ×1.25;
- CHARGED (the relay opens his T3s);
- from an Awakening's hybrid slot (NP_09 §6), the card's choice of his hybrids.

It answers its family's motion before the pair's Duo T3, a relay T3 and the card's own move, and manager mode casts it first. The registry (the ledger's, written into the save's `hybrid_registry`) counts its casts and dates its debut, its first cast in a dual.

**Mechanism.**
- **The table.** `data/hybrids.v1.json` and the engine copy, a new content table: `forge` (40 Spirits, 30 Beans), `ceiling` 8, `discounts` (E07, `quest:third_bridge`, 50 %, `early_level` 4), `compose` (the T3's cost, element, VFX, camera and counter profile; the damage floor and step; the tag cap; the cinematic; the name rules), `cast` (`relayed_dmg_mult` 1.25, the afterimage), `motifs` (13 houses).
- **The economy.** `economy.v1.json`'s Lv5 gains `"hybrid": true`.
- **The sim** (`core.gd`):
  - The table `HYB` (in the content stamp, out of the snapshot) through `setup`.
  - The requirement: `hybrid_level`, `learned_natives`, `forge_terms`, `forge_why`.
  - The Forge: `forge` (and `"*"`), `_forge_record`, `_authored_hybrid`, `compose_hybrid`, `_whole`.
  - The registry: `rewards["hybrids"]`, ordered by its own `n`; `_hyb_keys`, `hybrids_of`, `hybrid_registry`.
  - The cast: `hybrid_move` (relayed / CHARGED / awakened) and `_hyb_rec` (×1.25 and the afterimage relayed); `castable` passing a hybrid's T3; `cast_move` and `t3_pick` taking it first; `_start_cast`'s event and feed; `_hyb_cast_note` (casts and the debut).
  - The lines: `_hybrid_check` at the segment's load and an Awakening's start; `hybrid_line`, `forge_candidates`, `forge_lines`.
  - The choice and the purse: `choose_hybrid` and the Awakening feed's `_hyb_slot_text`; `seed_purse`.
- **The save** (`sim/save.gd`): the `hybrid_slot` card key, `currencies.beans` from the ledger, `hybrid_registry`.
- **The scene** (`main.gd`):
  - F on the DUAL OVER screen and the FORGE lines;
  - the matchup card's HYBRID line;
  - the FORGED, HYBRID and HYBRID SLOT toasts;
  - the afterimage in both of the pair's elements;
  - `-- purse=`, `-- forge=` and `-- demo hybrid`;
  - `;` as the keyboard's hookercut (the HAYMAKER motion);
  - the help lines and the soak's summary.
- **The drive:** `tools/drive_forge.gd`.

**Test.** `-- smoke` PAC-087 stage: 5 assertions.
1. **The data and the requirement.**
   - The table: the Forge 40/30, the ceiling 8, E07's discount (`quest:third_bridge`, 50 %, Lv4), the composer's 2.4 floor and +0.1, the 2 s cap, 2100 + 100, ×1.25 relayed, 13 motifs; Sworn carrying `hybrid` (Lv5).
   - The library's two authored hybrids.
   - Learned natives: YURI's Grain Elevator and Full Load, MARTA's Premiere Neige and Miroir d'Hiver, a House Iron card's none (its one native its SECRET), YURI's with his SECRET flag + Harvest Moon.
   - Refused, each for its reason: "NOBODY is not in the club", "a hybrid takes two fighters", "RORY carries no house (a hybrid crosses two houses)", "YURI and YURI2 are both House Kowalchuk (a hybrid crosses two houses)", "IRONSIDE knows no native of House Iron", "SAM & YURI are at Lv4 Duo (90) - the Forge opens at Lv5 Sworn", "the Forge costs 40 Spirits and 30 Beans (the ledger holds 39 and 40)", "the Forge is between duals".

   - Its hit 34.125 against 27.3 for the same hybrid from the same state with her partner not before her (CHARGED): ×1.25. Shred 40 in both; her auto-slip state live.
   - R3 SAM: nothing. MARTA out first and YURI second: relayed the other way. MARTA across a slot: nothing.
4. **The Awakening's slot and the choice.**
   - MARTA forged twice: Grain & Snow, and Silence & Snow (ARC; DMG 2.4, counter stance 2 s, auto-slip 2 s). Her slot is her first forged.
   - Refused "MARTA has no hybrid Tide & Snow"; her choice Silence & Snow; refused "between duals only" once the bell has rung; her record's `hybrid_slot`.
   - Out first, none live; CHARGED, her choice.
   - Awakened: the feed "… every drop scored +15 Tension back; the hybrid slot holds Silence & Snow", one slot event, Silence & Snow live at ×2.4, LB + ARC casting it. 40 s on, none.
5. **The manager and the record.**

**Gates moved:** the content-table gate lists 24 tables.

The profile casts Grain & Snow relayed with YURI's afterimage, its debut once. It is identical twice and different from the same log unforged, where the pair's Duo T3 (Coast to Coast - MARTA & YURI) answers the motion.

## C42 — The Exhibition 2v2: two pairs in one ring under one bell and one bar, and the duo sync is both inputs inside 500 ms — PROVEN
**Claim.** NP_01 §8's Exhibition 2v2 runs on the sim: two fighters a side in the ring at once, one segment, festival content, the only simultaneous format. NP_05 §7's duo hissatsu is its showcase: "true simultaneous duo moves (both players input the motion within 500 ms)".

**The model** (the one the build could afford, named):
- The sim is one fighter against one boss in every rule it has (P and B), and C6 keeps one resolution function. A four-body sim would mean re-plumbing every rule in it. So the exhibition runs **two engagements in lockstep**, one per pair, each a whole sim on the `exhibition_2v2` format:
  - pair A: the first card against the boss asked for;
  - pair B: his partner against the enemy's second (the boss list's next).
- Each engagement holds the pair on its card (its own fighter first, the partner beside him), so the bonds read the pair as adjacent: Adjacent Trust lifts both.
- A coordinator owns what the pairs share:
  - **the ledger:** one reward ledger and one Pattern Intel ledger, the same dictionaries in both;
  - **the bars:** NP_05 §1's one meter a team. Each tick the two engagements' changes are summed into the team's bar and written back to both. The enemy's bar and the building's crowd heat work the same way;
  - **the ring clock:** one bell. It runs while either pair fights, so a pair in its own hitstop or count is carried along with it;
  - **the verdict:** once both engagements are over (the bell, or a KO), NP_01 §3.3's ladder runs on the pairs' sums: score, then segment wins, then counter hits. A tie is a DRAW, with no Golden DROP at a festival. It pays once, as a dual: both fighters' closing XP, Spirits and HBP once, the pair's adjacent Kizuna once.
- A pair KO'd is finished and stands scored while the other fights on.
- One pad: the pad plays one pair, and the partner plays on his profile (NP_10 §8). `\` passes the pad between the pairs; a pair finished hands the pad to the one still fighting. In manager mode both pairs play on their profiles.

**The duo sync** (NP_05 §7):
- The library's festival moves (`duo_variant` "sync_500ms") by family:
  - LB + the uppercut flick (RISE) calls **Double Overtime**;
  - LB + block held 400 ms (WALL) calls **Two-Hand Winter**.
- The first motion opens a window of the format's 500 ms. The other fighter's same motion inside it casts the move in **both** engagements.
- The bar pays once, at the caller's price. Each half carries half the price for an interrupt's refund.
- A partner on his profile answers at 250 ms.
- Unanswered, the held motion executes as it would have: the flick as the fighter's own, or his own WALL.
- It is live from the Kizuna level whose effect carries `exhibition_duo`: Lv4 Duo (NP_08 §5.2's "2v2 exhibition duo hissatsu").
- The bar is the pair's call. Both hold it while a window is open, and a fighter on his profile holds it at 90 while the duo can be made.
- In manager mode the profiles call it at 90:
  - Two-Hand Winter when either of the pair is below half his composure;
  - Double Overtime once both are inside the uppercut's reach.
- **Two-Hand Winter** is "the only shared gauge in the game": one wall dictionary behind both fighters. The first half raised sets it, and the other's hands join it. Its clocks are run by the pair that raised it (handed on if that pair is finished).

**Mechanism.**
- **The tables.**
  - `formats.v1.json` (both copies) gains `exhibition_2v2`: `segments` 1, `dressed` 2, `enemy_dressed` 2, `simultaneous`, `sudden_death` false, `sync_ms` 500, `partner_answer_ms` 250, `manager_duo_bar` 90. Its notes say it is no rotation.
  - `economy.v1.json` (both copies): Lv4 Duo gains `"exhibition_duo": true`.
- **The coordinator** (`sim/exhibition.gd`, new):
  - setup and `_mk`: the two sims, the linked ledgers;
  - the tick: `step` / `step_pair`, `_sync_input` (the motions), `_holds`, `_open`, `_window_tick`, `_fire`, `_miss`, `_manager_call`;
  - the shared state: `_reconcile` (the bars and the ring clock), `_link_wall`;
  - the end and the seat: `_check_end` / `_end` (the ladder, the one pay), `restart`, `swap_control`, `set_manager`;
  - the record: `hash_state`, `snapshot` / `restore` (both DualStates and the coordinator's own fields, the ledger and the wall relinked), and `lines` / `duo_line` for the HUD.
- **The sim** (`core.gd`):
  - `exhibit` (the pair's tag) and `bar_hold` (in `can_pay`);
  - `_build_lineup`'s simultaneous branch (the pair on the card);
  - `_end_dual` → `_exhibit_end` (the pair over, the `engagement_end` event, no pay);
  - `_wall_tick` skipping a shared wall another pair owns;
  - the WALL resolve's duo mark (joined or raised);
  - `castable` passing a duo sync's T3, `_meter_pays` refusing it (the team bar pays);
  - `_start_cast`'s event and feed (`duo_sync`, `sync_partner`, `sync_id`);
  - `wall_motion()`, the WALL motion's cast, shared with the coordinator.
- **The scene** (`main.gd`):
  - `-- format=exhibition_2v2`, `-- pair=`;
  - both pairs drawn in their halves of the ring (the lateral compressed), the partner's capsule dimmed when his pair is finished, both halves gilded while the sync casts;
  - the HUD's exhibition lines (the ring clock, the team score and bars, both pairs, the duo sync line and its window) and the shared wall's line;
  - the DUO SYNC / MISSED / TWO-HAND WINTER / PAIR OVER / verdict toasts and the partner's feed lines;
  - `\`, F5 (again), F9 (back to the rotation formats), the refused keys, the help lines, `-- demo`'s aid and the soak's summary.
- **The drive:** `tools/drive_exhibition.gd`.

**Test.** `-- smoke` PAC-088 stage: 5 assertions.
1. **The format and the setup.**
   - The record as NP_01 §8 and NP_05 §7 have it. Lv4 carries `exhibition_duo`.
   - The pad RORY against Yuri, PRIYA on her profile against Moreau.
   - The lineups ["RORY","PRIYA"] / ["PRIYA","RORY"], no reserves, one segment. One ledger (the same dictionaries).
   - At Lv3 Adjacent Trust ×1.05 on both and CHARGED at 20. "DUO SYNC: RORY & PRIYA Lv3 - it opens at Lv4".
   - The mini-dual is still three slots.
2. **One bar, one bell, one ledger.**
   - RORY earns 10 and PRIYA 5 on a team bar of 40: [55, 55, 55]. The enemy's 10 on the enemy's one bar.
   - RORY in hitstop for 10 ticks: the ring clock ran 10 in both.
   - RORY's XP read in PRIYA's engagement.
3. **The duo sync.**
   - RORY's motion opens the window (RISE, Double Overtime): nothing thrown, both bars held.
   - PRIYA's motion 400 ms on: one `duo_cast` (hands, 400 ms). Double Overtime in both engagements, marked PRIYA|RORY, 25 a half. The bar [50, 50, 50]. It lands on both opponents (39.69 and 30.87).
   - At 600 ms: "PRIYA never answered inside 500 ms" at t61. RORY's flick thrown as his own at tick 62 (an uppercut: his move is no RISE). Nothing spent. Her late motion opens her own window, which shuts in turn.
   - The other way: PRIYA calls, and RORY's left uppercut answers at 200 ms.
   - On her profile: answered at 250 ms.
   - At Adjacent Trust (89): the motion is RORY's own at once, and no window opens.
4. **Two-Hand Winter.**
   - The 400 ms hold is the coordinator's (his own wall motion never counts past a tick). The window opens and her profile answers.
   - Both cast it, 50 off the bar once.
   - Resolved: one wall dictionary behind both, owned by pair A, 39 points, linked once.
   - A body hook on PRIYA chips 6.91 off the gauge RORY stands behind, and her composure is untouched.
   - Broken from RORY's side: TRUE OPEN 960 ticks, running 10 in 10 ticks (its owner's clock, not twice), one break. RORY's pair KO'd: PRIYA's pair owns it and runs it on.
5. **The verdict and the pay.**
   - PRIYA's pair KO'd: OVER, one `engagement_end`. RORY fights on; the exhibition is not over.
   - A snapshot of the whole exhibition mid-flight restores on the same hash with the ledger one again, and runs on identically.
   - RORY's pair on the bell: "ASCENDANT WINS THE EXHIBITION" on the score rung. One toast (pair A's), none in B. Kizuna 95 (+5 once), 5 Spirits once, the closing 40 (+10 % houseless) to both.
   - F5: both pairs back at the bell with the ledger carried.
   - `\` hands the pad to PRIYA and RORY goes to his profile. Her pair KO'd hands it back.
   - The manager's seat, with the bar at 95: the profiles call Double Overtime.

**Gates moved:** none.

**Determinism and the drive:**
- `-- determinism` adds the Exhibition 2v2 on the delegated log (the manager's seat), RORY and PRIYA at Duo against Yuri and Moreau, the bar topped up at tick 600.
  - The profiles call Double Overtime and both halves cast it; the exhibition runs to its verdict.
  - Every hash (both engagements and the coordinator) is identical twice and different from the same log at Adjacent Trust, where no duo is live and the profiles spend the bar as their own.
- `tools/drive_exhibition.gd`, 7 / 7:
  - the festival format on the HUD, each pair in its own half;
  - H + O calls Double Overtime, with the window's toast;
  - PRIYA's profile answers at 250 ms, and both halves cast it at RORY's price once, both capsules gilded;
  - it lands on both opponents;
  - `\` hands the pad to PRIYA and the halves swap;
  - the bell on both pairs: EXHIBITION OVER with the ladder's verdict, one REWARD toast and the bond paid once;
  - F5 starts the exhibition again.
**Result.**
- **Headless gates.** GREEN on all 24 bosses (376/376 each, 9,024 assertions); determinism 42/42 (the 41 earlier lines figure for figure as before); geometry 5/5.
- **Compiler and tools.** Margins GREEN; the compiler's selftest 10/10, and the 24 sources recompiled byte-identical to the vault's; sim_test 14/14; the hissatsu audit GREEN.
- **Drives.** The exhibition drive 7/7. Also the enemy-transformation drive 7/7, the Clash drive 5/5, the Awakening, Inheritance, Manifest and Zone drives 6/6 each, the formation drive 12/12, the recruit drive 8/8, the Duo drive 6/6 and the Forge drive 7/7.
- **Soaks.** Two runs each of 16,000-frame soaks in the manager's seat (the scene's seed is the clock; the log's restart starts the next exhibition once one ends), zero script errors:
  - `-- demo format=exhibition_2v2 boss=1 kizuna=RORY+PRIYA:90` (against Yuri and Moreau): two duo syncs a run, Double Overtime and Two-Hand Winter in both, the shared gauge linked in both; two exhibitions to their verdict in one run, one in the other.
  - `-- demo format=exhibition_2v2 boss=7 pair=YURI,MARTA kizuna=YURI+MARTA:150` (a Sworn pair of two houses against Carter and Chiamaka): two duo syncs a run (Double Overtime twice; Double Overtime and Two-Hand Winter); two exhibitions to their verdict in one run, one in the other.
  - `-- demo format=exhibition_2v2 boss=16 kizuna=RORY+PRIYA:50` (Adjacent Trust against Vasile and Itzel): no duo sync (the pair is Lv3); Vasile's Zone in pair A in both runs, twice in one; one exhibition to its verdict in each.
- **No older gate moved.**

## C43 — The club facilities: the Old Gym renovated track by track, each tier paid from the ledger behind the story, and every tier's effect live on the sim — PROVEN
**Claim.** NP_10 §7's gym progression tree runs on the sim's ledgers. There are five tracks, each T1 → T3, and the club starts every one at T0, the Old Gym as found. A tier is a renovation between duals (or before the first bell), paid in HBP and Spirits behind its story gate. It goes up on the Gym Wall as a photograph.

**The tiers.** The price and the gate are one per tier, across the tracks (first drafts against NP_11 §6):
- T1: 150 HBP and 5 Spirits, once the charter is saved (NP_12 §4 beat 2);
- T2: 400 HBP and 15 Spirits, after the funding quest (beat 5);
- T3: 800 HBP and 30 Spirits, after the Nationals (beat 7).

**The effects.** Each track's effects arrive in the order NP_10 §7's table lists them, one a tier. Where the first is a capacity, it grows at every tier after.
- **Bags & Mitts** (NP_11 §7's training):
  - three slots a day;
  - Roadwork from T0 (a squad member along bonds +3), and the harbour's Net Haul with its quest;
  - Mitts and the Heavy Bag at T1, the Slip Bag at T2, Shadow Rounds at T3 (against the ghost of a Scout Book pattern).

  A slot pays its range at the minigame's grade, ×1.25 at T2 and ×1.5 at T3. These are the Beans the Forge spends.
- **The Ring:**
  - the spar's lab flags at T1 (an authored bout keeps its own);
  - the Exhibition 2v2 hosted at home at T2 (C42's format; below it, the festival's boardwalk);
  - the ranked spar board at T3.
- **The Film Room:**
  - the Scout Book's shelf: 6 pages as found, then 12, 24 and 48. A page past it waits, and its PI 3 with it, until a tier makes room;
  - at T2 the grind's rungs come sooner: PI 1 at 2 scraps, PI 2 at 4; 2 spars for PI 2;
  - at T3 the tape reads a decoy seal on sight from a corner that has run one on the club before.
- **The Wall:**
  - two card columns more at T1, four from T2;
  - the honours set at T2: a blessed house's crest and a feat's plaque, once each and zero stats;
  - the Forge at T3 (C41's Forge is now hosted: "a Forge event at the gym").
- **Front of House:**
  - the club's entrance at every dual's bell: a music sting at T1, a banner at T2, the crowd chant at T3, and the Reyes bell with its quest (NP_07A);
  - a hosted dual's crowd starting at 20 from T2;
  - 5 Prestige from a hosted dual at T3.

**Mechanism.**

## C44 — The fighter's growth: the ability board by style, the stat raises on typed Beans, and rarity climbed on Spirits and a trial — PROVEN
**Claim.** NP_11 §3, §4 and §6 run on the sim's ledgers. Between duals a fighter grows three ways, and the reward ledger keeps each (`rewards["growth"]`). At the bell he walks out on his grown stats with his passives on him.

**The board.** One hex layout per style archetype (4), 60 nodes each:
- the spine (8): the stance at the root (every fighter's, free), the style's three stats +2 then +3, and its tail — the Boxer-Puncher's off-style socket (NP_06 §1.2), else the first stat's +5;
- the Body lobe (24): every stat +2 and +3, the style's three +5, and a soft-cap breaker per stat at the far edge;
- the Craft lobe (14) and the Spirit lobe (14): a strip of hexes, the passives at their depths and stat nodes between.

Two nodes are injected per named character, joined to the spine. The eight Millbrook cards' are authored; a recruit's are the rule's (+3 on his two best stats); a generic has none.
- **The price.** A node is taken when it touches one already taken, paid in Board Points (2 a level, NP_11 §2). +2 costs 1, +3 costs 2, +5 costs 3, a breaker 2, a passive 3, the socket 2. A stat node on a stat at 70+ costs double (NP_11 §1) until that stat's breaker is taken.
- **The cap.** A stat node at his rarity's cap is the way through: it can be taken and adds nothing past the cap.
- **Respec** is free at the gym from the Film Room's T2 (C43's tier): every point back, the socket emptied. The raises stay.
- **The passives.** NP_11 §3's nine are live on the sim:
  - Craft: Tight Guard (guard decay −20 % while holding), Snap (jab damage +30 %), Ring Cutter (herding +25 %), Iron Jaw (the wobble 30 % shorter: the composure climbs out of it 1/0.7 as fast), Second Gear (the gassed curve's penalties halved between 15 and 40 stamina);
  - Spirit: Sparkplug (his Tension ×1.15), Cheap Trick (a T0 at 8), Closer (CHARGED at −5), Wallflower (a T2's timed effects +1 s).

  The craft passives split by style: the Swarmer's board carries Ring Cutter, Second Gear and Iron Jaw; the Out-Boxer's Snap, Tight Guard and Second Gear; the Slugger's Iron Jaw, Ring Cutter and Tight Guard; the Boxer-Puncher's Tight Guard and Snap, with the socket for one of the other three. The Spirit passives are on every board.

**The raises.** +1 on a stat for 5 Beans, and +1 Bean for each raise already bought on that stat (NP_11 §6). The Beans are typed (NP_11 §7): a training slot types its Beans by its game's bias (Shadow Rounds' "any" untyped), and a partner along types a quarter of them (rounded) as his best stat. A raise spends the stat's own Beans first, then the untyped; another stat's never pay it. The Forge's spend takes the untyped first, then the typed in the stats' order.

**Rarity.** Club, Circuit, National, Legend (NP_11 §4):
- the cap growth reaches: 70, 80, 90, 99 (a card's own stats stand as dressed);
- up a tier for 10, 25 and 60 Spirits and a trial: a spar won; a transform weathered (C40's Stood in the Storm); the mirror beaten;
- an accessory slot a tier, to the schema's three;
- the Legend's Awakening flag. A story character carries his from the start; a generic earns it at tier 4, and C39's gate reads it.

The mirror is a spar against the fighter's own card, on his grown stats and marked generic, so it fights on strings assembled for it (C24). The spar sends him out, whatever the card order. (Since C47 the strings are his record's where he has one.)

**Mechanism.**
- **The table.** `data/growth.v1.json` and its engine copy, a new content table:
  - the points, the costs, the soft cap, the respec's track and tier, the raises, the Beans' partner share;
  - rarity's names, caps, Spirits, trials, accessory slots and Awakening tier;
  - the nine passives (their numbers, their home styles) of a 40 pool;
  - the four layouts (every node's lobe, kind, cost and links), the named nodes and the rule for the rest.
- **The sim** (`core.gd`):
  - `GROW` (in the content stamp, out of the snapshot) through `setup`'s new last argument;
  - the board: `growth_layout`, `growth_nodes`, `_named_nodes`, `gpoints`, `gpoints_free`, `taken`, `node_cost`, `node_why`, `take_node` (and `"*"`), `node_best` (the aid: a passive in reach, else the first step toward the nearest, else the best stat for its price), `respec_why`, `respec`, `socket_why`, `socket`;
  - the fighter: `grown_stats` (at `_load_segment`, and read by the carried condition, the formation's sums and STAT_PROOF), `passives_of` (on `P["passives"]` at the bell), `_pas` / `_pv` at the nine hooks (`_gassed`, `_land_player_punch` twice, the guard's hold decay, the composure's regen, `_earn_tension`, `hiss_cost`, `_charged_threshold`, `_resolve_cast`);
  - the Beans: `beans_typed`, `beans_untyped`, `_beans_type` (in `train`), `_beans_settle` (after the Forge), `jar_text`, `raise_cost`, `raise_why`, `raise_stat` (and `"*"`), `raise_best`;
  - rarity: `rarity_of`, `rarity_cap`, `accessory_slots`, `awaken_eligible` (in `_awaken_gate`), `ascend_why`, `ascend`, `_trial_mark` (from `_end_bout` and `_boss_tform_end`), `mirror_card`, `start_mirror` (`start_spar`'s `fighter` and `mirror`, read by `_build_lineup`);
  - `growth_line`, `grow_aid` and the OVER input `grow`; `seed_growth`, `seed_beans`.
- **The save** (`sim/save.gd`): each Fighter's `rarity`, `board` (`layout_id`, `nodes_taken`, `points_free`, `socket`), `raises`, `stats_grown`, `passives`, `awakening_flag` and `accessory_slots` — views of the ledger's growth, which a load reads back. A generic card keeps its flag.
- **The scene** (`main.gd`):
  - the DUAL OVER screen's GROWTH lines (the jar by type, each dressed card's line, the focus marked);
  - `[N]` a node, Shift+`[N]` respec, `[Z]` a raise, `[X]` ascend, `[M]` the mirror, `[.]` the next card;
  - the THE BOARD, RESPEC, THE JAR, ASCENDED and TRIAL toasts, the training toast's types, and the matchup card's PASSIVES line;
  - `-- growth=`, `-- beans=`, `-- nodes=`, and `-- demo growth` (between duals the aid takes a node, a raise and an ascension for each dressed card);
  - the help lines and the soak's summary.
- **The drive:** `tools/drive_growth.gd`.

**Test.** `-- smoke` PAC-090 stage: 5 assertions.
1. **The boards.**
   - Four layouts of 60 nodes (spine 8, Body 24, Craft 14, Spirit 14), each reachable from the stance; the passives by board as above; the socket the Boxer-Puncher's alone; nine live of a 40 pool.
   - RORY's board 62 nodes with "Borealis Wind-up (RORY's own)"; a recruit's two by the rule (PWR +3, SPD +3); 20 points at level 10, 2 at level 1.
   - Refused: "+3 PWR does not touch a node RORY has taken"; "+2 STA costs 1 Board Points - JUNE has 0 of 2 free (level 1)"; "the board is worked between duals". Taken: the craft lobe's +2, then Tight Guard (16 of 20 free, two events).
   - RORY's HRT at 99 through the spirit lobe's +2 HRT: 99, at 2 points.
   - The soft cap on MARCUS at Circuit: PWR 70's nodes at double (2, 2, 4, 6), the breaker 2, then single (1); PWR 80.

2. **Each live passive against the same sim without it.** RORY walks out with one node on his board (the socket for the three off his board) and nothing else. Ten sims, one without:
   - Tight Guard: 60 ticks held, the guard 1.0 lost → 0.8;

   Each passive moves its own number and no other.
3. **Typed Beans and the raises.**
   - At Bags & Mitts T3: Mitts pays 8 SPD; the Heavy Bag with PRIYA along 8 (6 PWR, 2 RFX, her best); Shadow Rounds 9 untyped. The jar: "PWR 6, SPD 8, RFX 2, 9 untyped", 25.

4. **Rarity.**

   - In play: a spar won marks `spar_won`; a transform weathered unanswered marks `storm`. The mirror: PRIYA out against "PRIYA'S MIRROR" (generic, her grown SPD 62 on both), won: `spar_won` and `mirror`.
   - National at 25 (the cap 90), refused for Legend on "beat the mirror in a spar", then Legend at 60: 105 left, 99, 3 slots, "RORY is a Legend (tier 4), the top"; three `ascended` events.
   - The Awakening flag: a generic at tier 3 not eligible and C39's gate shut with every flag set; RORY eligible; the generic at tier 4 eligible and the gate open.
5. **The save.** RORY at Circuit with 8 nodes and Second Gear socketed, PRIYA with a raise and a node: each Fighter's `rarity`, `board` (`BOXERPUNCHER`, the 8 nodes, 8 free, `second_gear`), `raises`, `stats_grown` (PWR 52 on the card's 45), `passives`, `awakening_flag` and `accessory_slots`. ## C45 — Techniques and the slots: a kit beside the card's own move, the slots by level, and every source on the ledger — PROVEN
**Claim.** NP_11 §2 and §8 run on the sim's ledgers. A card walks out carrying its own move (its hissatsu, as dressed) and a kit beside it; LB + a motion casts his own move on its family, else the kit's first move of that family he can cast and pay for. What each fighter learned, from where, and what he carries are the reward ledger's (`rewards["techniques"]`).

**The slots.**
- A technique slot at each of levels 5, 15, 25, 35 and 45 (five at most) holds a learned T1 or T2.
- The T0 slot opens at level 5 and holds exactly one T0, the identity move (NP_05 §3). A card whose own move is a T0 has it there, and it is castable from level 5 (NP_12A: Priya's Marginalia unlocks at level 5). Below 5 the motion is its punch.
- His house's natives, when he knows them (C31), take no slot (NP_07 §7: natives ADD).
- The relay's, the duo's, the hybrid's and the transformation's moves are cast by their own rules and never slotted; the boss-only rows are not the club's to learn.

**The sources** read the library's own `source` field:
- **The HBP shop:** three shelves by circuit progress — t1 as found (100 HBP), t2 with the Regionals (400), t3 with the Provincials (800) — selling the `shop:` rows and the generic library.
- **The masters,** each behind a gate, free:
  - Whitlock: Eight Count Answer after the learner's first survived standing-eight (NP_05A §3), Timberfall on the coach questline;
  - the conditioning coach: Roadwork while one is dressed;
  - DEX's Prodigal at a Close Friend's bond (Kizuna Lv2), CARTER's Empty Net on SPD 60, CASSIDY's Freelance in Arc 3 — each while the master is in the stable.
- **Fight tapes:** single use. A quest's tape arrives once with its flag (Portage with the harbour's, Breakup with the rink district's); Black Ice is a found tape.
- **The natives** (C31) and **the Forge's hybrid** (C41), as before.
- **The re-teach:** a learned technique that is not native passed to a stablemate in one of the day's training slots (C43), the teacher keeping it.

A move learned goes straight into his kit when there is room for it; else it waits in what he knows.

**Mechanism.**
- **The table.** `data/techniques.v1.json` and its engine copy, a new content table: the slot levels, the T0's level, the slot tiers; the sources cast by their own rule and the ones not learnable; the shop's shelves (sources, generic tiers, price, flag); the masters (kind, role, list, gates); the tapes (the quests', the found); the re-teach.
- **The sim** (`core.gd`):
  - `TECH` (in the content stamp, out of the snapshot) through `setup`'s new last argument;
  - the kit: `tech_slots`, `t0_open` (read by `castable`), `learned`, `kit_of`, `tech_class`, `known`, `kit_moves` (on `P["kit"]` at `_load_segment`), `kit_why`, `kit_equip`, `kit_unequip`, `kit_best`;
  - the cast: `cast_move`'s tail (his own on its family, else `kit_move`), `wall_move` (his own WALL, else the kit's), `kit_pick` (the profile's);
