- Ring 1's +50 ms counter windows have no boss-side analogue (bosses counter by authored string, not by window); Ring 3's feud Tension is player-side (bosses have no bar). Four package edits have no boss analogue for the same reason: bosses pay no punch stamina, tap no clinch windows, have no back-step input, and land on the act rather than a startup. ALLIED / DEBT / MARRIAGE edges carry no matchup math, per NP_06 §3. Anders Vey's "varies" and Ioan Vasile's "rotating" elements are neutral until their authored mechanics exist. The matchup card is drawn over the opening 3 s of the segment rather than holding the fight for 3 s (a presentation choice; the sim starts the boss idle for 600 ms either way). The four Millbrook cards' rings are editorial pending the content lane (Rory boxer-puncher/FROST/no-name, Priya out-boxer/GALE, Marcus slugger/GRANITE with an off-element Chinook Rush, June swarmer/FROST).
- The Talk is C26: its 24 lines are placeholders for the content lane, and seven of the eight profiles are first drafts from the cards' scenes (Priya's is the doc's); bark sets (NP_11 §9's `personality.bark_set`) are carried empty. Readings taken: every talk offers the same three approaches in the fighter's own words; the bond that reveals is the next fighter's best Kizuna with any dressed teammate, Gymmates ruling one wrong option out and Corner Voices naming the right one; one talk per corner; a right read is bound to the fighter it was said to. The enemy coach (NP_10 §9) has no corner — Hostile Crowd is recorded on the segment and locks nothing. Only the 8 named specials of the launch library of 12 exist; staff passives and levels (C28), the Lv3 coach's third action, the cutman's "get off the ropes" prompt and manager-mode delegation are not in the sim. Patch Job's +30 composure and Second Wind's +40 stamina restore a fresh fighter to nothing beyond the cap; they earn their keep in repeat-slot formats (NP_01 §5, condition carries) and after an overrun, which is what the gate measures. The afterimage and the element trail are rendered by the greybox as a translucent capsule and a tint, soaked through the `seg2` aid, not judged for feel.
- The reward economy (C22) stops at the ledger: no sinks (shops, scouting pulls, stat raises, training, ascension — the overworld's; the Forge since C41; the stat raises and ascension since C44), no Beans until C41 and C43 (the Forge's price reads them since C41; the training slots earn them since C43), no Kizuna from the sources the sim never sees (overworld squads, shared training, bond scenes, the training-partner 65 %), no Sworn (Lv5) effect (the inheritance transform; the Duo's named T3 is C34's), no session Prestige (5–15 per session is the overworld's sum of the scraps' +1–3). The per-event XP amounts, the segment / closing / scrap / spar amounts, the Spirits and HBP by result, the Kizuna points per source and the level thresholds are first-draft design values shaped by the docs (XP continuous, Spirits 3–8, Prestige +1–3, the +50 % reserve rate, +10 % houseless, −50 % for the lab, the +15/day cap that duals bypass); the level curve is the doc's. Readings taken and reversible: the dressed reserves are the fighters dressed but never in the lineup ("undressed" read as "not in the rotation"); a scrap pays only when won and a spar bonds only with a card marked `stablemate` (Ep1's Priya trial card is, in `data/bouts.v1.json`; the roster's bosses are not); a spar or dual lost still pays its floor (the trickle, the 40, the 5 Spirits); Adjacent Trust's +5 % replaces Corner Voices' +2 % rather than stacking; a level's name is an event and a query, not a menu; the toast omits a zero Prestige or HBP line.
- The save (C23) is the ledgers and a fight in flight, no more: the overworld's fields (the calendar as dates and a season, the hybrid registry) are carried as the schema's empty names (the staff are written since C28, the story flags since C29, the string wall since C30, the house codex since C31, the gym wall since C33, the hybrid registry and the Beans since C41); the Fighter record holds only what the sim knows of a card (no nickname, age, ai_profile; the board since C44, the gear since C48; the personality's talk profile since C26, `dressed` since C29); there are no save slots, no migration (an older schema or other content is refused, not converted), no autosave, and no versioning beyond the schema string. Readings taken: the stable is the cards the club owns (since C29: the dressed in the board's order, the rest flagged undressed; before, only the dressed); the dual's `ep_by_segment` is not kept (the sim keeps the cumulative EP and the segment wins); `feud_graph_state` was the graph's edge count, a stamp, until C31 — since then the graph as it stands, its flips from the ledger; the calendar is one day counter the sim's `new_day()` advances. The DualState's exact state is a base64 blob of the sim's variables, engine-native (`var_to_bytes`): readable by this build of the sim under the same content and by nothing else, which the stamp and the signature enforce.
- The assembler (C24) stops at the rules NP_04 §9 names: the 63 pieces are a first draft (labels, actions, motifs, presets, hooks — the launch library's 60 are the content lane's), the dialect sets for Houses without a roster boss (Whitecloud, Delacroix-Cardinal) are invented from NP_07 §8's lines, the tier bands and the stat blocks (40 / 55 / 70) are design values; a generic has no bait hook (§9 names one PUNISH; §5's "≥1 BAIT" is the named-boss rule), no phases, no desperation and no idle lines; fillers are placed by hand (`set_generic_slot`, `-- generic=`) — no club dresses fillers because its roster is short, since every club still dresses from the 24; the mockups' story route (who hands you the card) is the overworld's. Readings taken: a generic card's PI dialect is its named one, else its House's, else its own name, with its tells on the gym's channel when it has no House (Marcus and Priya tick their own tracks); a generic earns no Scout Book page; a card with no seed takes its name's hash; the SPECIAL comes only at tier 3 and is never in the interrupt block; Ep1's cards are tier 1 (the CHALLENGER, MARCUS, JUNE) and tier 2 (PRIYA).
- The neutral game (C25) is a first draft in its numbers: every speed, range, orbit rate, reversal clock, beat window, angle, band, guard rate, wobble multiplier and clock effect in `neutral.v1.json` is a design value read off the roster docs' words, and the guard is a block chance by head and body, not a posture the punch regions test. Readings taken: `charter_stance` as Cal holding the centre and coming no closer than 2.2 m ("the centre is his"); `guard_composed` as a guard that does not halve when he is wobbled, which puts the halving on every other authored guard (NP_03 §3's wobble as "the opponent's cue to swarm"; bosses with no guard line keep the flat 0.35); `herding_bias_ropes` as the ring cut from the centre side; `dead_stance` as the Echo walking at the player's position with no anticipation; `taunt@Ns` on his own steps, fired at his first moment between strings once due, the guard open 500 ms and the next string held 700 ms; `tempo_shift@6s` as two tempos alternating from a slow start; `unmasked` as Remy's slot named at a blind dual's corner. The three flags (`silent`, `footwork_audio_drops_when_arming`, `bell_in_corner`) change nothing in the fight; the greybox has no audio to play them. A generic has no IDLE lines and keeps the shared approach.
- The formation screen (C27) is a board and a projection, not the whiteboard scene: the Gym Wall's card proximities (NP_08 §7) suggested nothing until C33, the scout is one stand-in (Priya's binders on Toronto's two corners; since C53 a scout on the bench reads his home district's clubs too), watching rival duals and story leaks (NP_10 §3's other sources) are not sources, the tactician's Lv3 is earned by use since C28, and his side seals no read of yours. Every number is a first draft: how readable each archetype is (Standard 40, Wall-First 35, Ambush 30, Relay Ladder 25), Priya's 30, PI's 5 a level, the cap 95, silhouettes under 35, the decoy rates (Vey's Academy 0.5, the Hollow Circuit 0.4), the ace and anchor stat sums, the ring notes; Ep1's 70 % is Standard 40 + Priya 30 under those numbers (with no PI levels and no tactician). Readings taken: the projection is honest by construction and a decoy shifts every name one slot; the decoy lives in your read of his order, never in the order he fights; a board of 3 or fewer closes on its ace; the Relay Ladder chains by Kizuna points; a scrap or spar still sends the first dressed card; his Relay Ladder still orders as Standard (PAC-060). The greybox's R1 is still the boss you asked for, so a projection can show someone else there.
- The staff (C28) stop at the corps and the corner: recruiting staff (NP_08's channels), the cornerman-in-training stable role (since C53 built: a trainee is a staffer's use at a dual's end) and the head coach's library growth are not built (the club facilities, NP_10 §7, are C43's); a staffer's level is his uses and nothing else (no staff XP from dressing, no level beyond 3, Lv2 unlocks nothing), and his active's charges do not grow with it. The ten staffers are NP_10 §6's own list with placeholder handles, not names; three of the five second passives (the cutman's +10 composure, the conditioning coach's 90 % overrun, the Professor's bout PI) and the level thresholds are first drafts, as are the crowd's six sources, the 30 heat Raise the Roof converts and the heat itself (one meter for your side, reset each dual, fed by your spectacle only). Readings taken: two of a role both count, two carrying one active pool its charges and are credited in dressing order; the cutman works a fighter whose condition is carried (a fresh one has nothing to restore) and before an overrun's clamp; Raise the Roof needs the heat it converts; the rival corner has no staff and no crowd.
- Recruitment (C29) stops at the grammar and the stable (the String Wall's maps are C30's): the scouting machines and their leads, open-mat days (§2.4), the story channels (§2.5), the roster UI are not built (since C52 the machines and the open mat are; since C54 the roster's locker room) (blessings arriving with natives or UNSANCTIONED, §3, are C31's); nothing in the sim sets a story flag (QUEST, REP, FEUD_RESOLVED, BLESSING, ITEM are the overworld's, seeded here with `-- flag=`), (since C55 REP is the sim's: the district rep ledger) so ten of the thirteen conditions (nine of twelve before C30 opened Jean-Guy's) can only be met with a seed; there is no roster cap enforced (100), no release, no Prestige cost. Since C52 the open mat enforces the cap and the machine costs Prestige; since C54 a recruit is refused at the cap and the locker room releases a card. Readings taken: Yuri's and Sam's "recruited post-dual" as BEAT_IN(dual[segment]); Dex's whole condition and his surfacing with Moira; "beating a club" as winning a dual against the squad he was in, the flagged being that squad's recruitables in its order up to four; BOND(X, n) as X's best Kizuna with anyone in the stable; WITNESS as a dual won after the approach, the greybox having one venue (the Old Gym); a scrap against an NPC card counts against that card's name. The eight new cards (the boss's rings, a flat 70 block, his desperation move; Vane's drafted Pressure Cooker) have no talk profile yet, so The Talk cannot read them.
- The String Wall (C30) is the maps and their strings, not the corkboard scene: no scouting machines or gacha leads (NP_08 §2.4 — the wall's leads are the questlines' people, not pulls), no open-mat days, no story channels (§2.5); until C31 nothing in the sim granted a blessing — `blessing:<house>` was the overworld's flag alone and the houses' NP_07A lines were shown, not evaluated; C31 reads them, grants, and sets arrivals NATIVE or UNSANCTIONED; a lead joins nothing (no staff recruitment into the corps; no card for Vera Iron or for the students, cousins, pairs and recruits the questlines' rewards promise unnamed); until C31 feud-graph mutations were not applied and a chain read the launch graph; since C31 it reads the graph as it stands. Readings taken: "recruiting a node" as a recruit through the ledger — a card the club begins with (the sanctioned eight's SAM, DEX, YURI, MARTA) brings no strings; a fighter a string turns up is approached (his condition surfaces) rather than merely approachable; the Kowalchuk cousins surface with the house's blessing (Q-KOWALCHUK's reward), not with Yuri; Sam → the Choir as a master's string (the quiet corner); Anders and Baró strung to House Petrov (the heir taken; the system Petrov descends from); Iron's crest opening with Vera because no blessing exists; the chain crossing ALLIED, MARRIAGE and DEBT, never a FEUD; `recruit("*")` signing a string's already-met recruit in the same call; Jean-Guy recruitable in Arc 2 on Q-MOREAU's blessing line, Rémy's Q-MOREAU completion as `QUEST(cold_storage)`. The six maps' grouping, the unnamed leads' labels and roles are first drafts; the 43 nodes meet NP_08 §9's six maps and §2.3's "40+ nodes" from the docs' own people and are the content lane's to replace.
- Blessings and the live graph (C31) stop at the ledgers: no blessing scene, no gift ceremony, no SECRET gate event (the SECRETs stay locked however blessed a house is), no cadet house or disowned heir, no marriage-line or adopted status (NATIVE and UNSANCTIONED only, NP_11 §9 names four more), no house trait executed until C32 (nine run since; the eleven unauthored of NP_07 §3's pool of 20 and the three variants do not), no technique library until C45 (a card equips his own house's natives as its move, or his signature back, between duals; the kit beside the move is C45's); the bosses' own UNSANCTIONED META (Efua's) stays presentational — her home bonus as a boss is unchanged and her stumble is her PBN's; a graph flip writes no same-day Note to the graph data (NP_07A §1) and commentary does not speak it; Q-SEALED's self-blessing and the restored house node are not built, E01 flips only on a flag the Arc 2 spine would set. Readings taken: a house's line with the house as its subject — BEAT_IN against any fighter of that house (a generic of its dialect counts), WITNESS as a dual won while its crest is up on the String Wall (the greybox has one venue), Tagaq's "without your crowd-heat actives" as no crowd heat converted by your corner that dual, Petrov's "petrov-taught moves only" as every hissatsu your fighter cast in a won segment, scrap or spar being a House Petrov move (at least one) against a fighter Ascendant A or B corners; a met line blessing at once, the flag blessing too; a card the club begins with NATIVE (the story signed him before the ledger); natives as the library's non-SECRET moves of the house; a FEUD flipping on its own FEUD_RESOLVED flag (either order) and FEUD_RESOLVED reading the edge for a pair on the graph, the flag for a feud off it; E02 needing both final beats and its own flag; La Campana's breach voiding the Reyes blessing for good, before or after it lands; Efua's early route (`BEAT_IN(dual[segment])`) a first draft.
- House traits (C32) stop at the nine NP_07 §3 authors: the launch pool's other eleven and the three §8 variants are not run, Iron's own trait is lost (its doubling has nothing to double), the sealed house's comes online only at the unsealing, the disowned heir's reconciliation and the cadet house are not built, and NP_09's inheritance transformation (a source's trait borrowed) is not built. Boss-side analogues are absent where the sim gives a boss no mechanism: Deep Roots (no boss guard regen), First Frost (bosses counter by string), Open Door (no boss bonds). Readings taken: "exchange win" as a clean scoring blow, the trigger of the centre exchange bonus, Harvest's +1 landing anywhere; Load-Bearing's "composure pool +20 %" as composure damage ÷1.2 while he stands on the ropes or in a corner (the same fraction of a pool 20 % deeper; regen unchanged); "trailing on DROPS" as his side having scored fewer drops in the dual; "segment-opening 15 s" as 15 s of sim time from the segment's load; Never Twice's "once per dual" per fighter, a referee's awarded drop not a knockdown; Debt of Honour against the live graph (a flipped feud ends it) with stats clamped to 99; Open Door as the larger of it and ALLIED's ×1.25; UNSANCTIONED keeping the trait; the lineage qualifiers (root line and origin line blood; taken, extracted and a teaching root system not); "doubled" as every magnitude's delta and use count ×2.
- The Gym Wall (C33) is the bond board as data, not the scene: no photo mode, no wall in cutscenes, no bond scenes behind the polaroids (a polaroid records a level crossed), The Wall facility's tiers are C43's (NP_10 §7: the base wall widened, the honours set), no unpinning (a card leaves the locker only when pinned to a free cell), and no Sworn effect (NP_08 §5.2 Lv5 — the gilding is the line's look; the Duo's T3 is C34's). Readings taken: proximity as the straight-line distance between card cells and the suggestion as the starters' order with the shortest walk (the nearest cards in adjacent slots), a tie broken by the first permutation in card order, an unpinned card 100 cells from everything; the suggestion orders the board's starters (the sealed board's, else the dressed not in reserve) and never chooses who is dressed; a wall-sealed board is an order like any other, so pins after the seal move nothing; the letter as the wall's first decoration and the club's cards from the cell beside it in the stable's order, a recruit at the nearest free cell to the stablemate whose string brought him; a poster per dual won, a stub on the first dual against a club won or lost (the coach table's club, an unaffiliated squad its lead's), a polaroid per Kizuna level crossed, the wraps per recruit; a line for any Kizuna between pinned cards, its fill toward the next level, gilded at Sworn. The 8 × 4 grid, the band, the bounds and the unpinned distance are first drafts.
- The Duo (C34) is the relay's named T3 and nothing more: the Exhibition 2v2's duo hissatsu (the festival moves' `sync_500ms`, NP_01 §8) is C42's, Sworn's Inheritance (NP_09 §5, a T4) and cross-house hybrid research (NP_08 §6) are not built (Inheritance since C38, hybrid research since C41), duo callouts and barks (the GDD's §8.2) are absent, and the unique VFX are ids the greybox answers with one gilded capsule. Found building it, closed by C35: NP_05 §6's CHARGED unlock opened T3s only to a card that carried one, and no Millbrook card did. Readings taken: the unlock by the bond, CHARGED or not (the GDD's "relay state or bonded adjacency"), with NP_05 §6's first-cast half price kept; "adjacent" as the relay's order — the fighter who fought the segment immediately before, after swaps; the pair's base as the one that names it, else the relay T3 of the element both carry, else the ANY-element one; the variant hitting as its base (the docs give it a name and VFX, not numbers), `effect_add` adding only the afterimage state where the base lacks it; its family's motion answered by the T3 while the bar can pay it and by the card's own move below; manager mode taking it first; Lv5 keeping it; the six names and the generated "{base} - {A} & {B}" (pair order) placeholders for the content lane.
- The relay's own T3 (C35) opens the library's relay moves and nothing more: the enemy's relay T3s are not built (bosses cast by composure, C14), a CHARGED fighter's choice among open T3s is the motion alone (no menu), the "multi-teammate shot-chain" VFX is the existing element trail, and Borrowed Blood's steal is the only way to close the relay. Readings taken: "T3 moves unlocked for their segment" as every relay-sourced T3 in the library, whatever the card carries; North Star's "FULL RELAY exclusive" binding a card that carries it too; each T3 answering its own family's motion, the Duo's variant in its base's place, the T3 before the card's own move while the bar can pay it (NP_05 §4's fall-through, downward); the afterimage the previous fighter's; manager mode's pick by the damage in the move (its DMG multiplier, the chain's links, the same-element spike), the Duo's first.
- In the Zone (C36) is the first of NP_09's transformations and its shared rules, no more: Spirit Manifestation (the manifest meter, the echo, the Manifest Clash — since C37 all but the Clash run), Inheritance (since C38) and Awakening (since C39) are named and not run; enemy transforms are authored per boss (NP_09 §1) and none is built (built since C40, the roster's one line Vasile's); the Zone's presentation is the greybox's (the fighter lit in his element, a HUD line) — no desaturation, breath audio or heartbeat crowd; the Zone's unlock reads the story's flag and the XP ledger's level, and no overworld event sets them (seeded with `-- flag=` and `-- level=`); "counterplay" beyond the Comedown window (NP_09 §7's weathering option) is not built (since C40 it is, against the enemy's transformations). Readings taken: the whole bar as NP_09 §1 prices it — 100, the FULL RELAY's 75 — with no CHARGED half and no element edge (those are the hissatsu's), emptied whatever it held; the cinematic as a camera hold the world waits out (2.5 s, NP_05 §5's hold at the top of its range, a first draft) — "uninterruptible" by construction; "time perception ×0.75" as the flash window's slow-time (his timers), stacked with it; "the first incoming punch every 3 s" as the first in each 3 s window from the Zone's start, any punch, body shots too; "stamina frozen (no drain)" as neither drain nor regen, from the value at the SURGE; "punches +15 % speed" as startup and recovery ÷ 1.15; the Comedown's 85 % on every stat, the stamina resuming at its value; both ending with the segment (the fighter leaves the ring); the SURGE as the manager's call through delegation and never the card's own; the flag's name `story:provincials_semifinal`.
- Spirit Manifestation (C37) is the tier's mechanics on §1's rules, no more: the Manifest Clash is named and not run (both fighters must manifest, and enemy transforms are authored per boss — none is built; since C40 it runs, and no roster boss authors a Manifest), the named characters' figures are the content lane's (the tier runs under "<NAME>'s manifest" until one is authored; content review's "says something true about its fighter" is theirs), the element-generic manifests are placeholder names with no figure, the presentation is the greybox's (a translucent capsule behind the fighter, a pulse on each echo, a HUD line — no camera orbit break, no arena reaction, no authored commentary), NP_09 §7's weathering is not built (since C40 it is), and nothing in the sim sets `story:manifest:<NAME>` or `mastery:zone:<NAME>` (seeded with `-- flag=`; how a stablemate masters the Zone is not defined). No generic stablemate exists in the club; the gate builds one (a card marked `generic`). Readings taken: the SURGE casting the highest tier unlocked with no choice of a lower (NP_09 names none), and "one transformation per fighter per dual" across tiers; the named path the story's flag alone (no level) and the generic path closed to named characters, and the reverse; "all damage ×1.4" as every composure hit the fighter deals — punches clean or chipped, hissatsu packets — and not the damage he takes; the meter paying each move's own price with the CHARGED half and the element edge, SAVE THE BAR not holding it, the motion throwing its punch when the meter is short (no fall-back to the team bar: "instead of"), half of a broken cast back to the meter, the remainder leaving with the manifest; the echo following a punch that lands clean (not a block, a whiff or a foul; not a hissatsu — "the fighter's punches"), 0.3 s of fight time later (hitstop does not count), for 30 % of that punch's damage as it stood before his composure took it, landing whatever he does in between, cancelled by a drop or the segment's end, stopped by his timed wall, taken by his gauge at the punch's own source factor, earning no Tension and scoring nothing; "zone displacement effects" as the DISPLACE packets (NP_05 §5's "zone displacement (knock to ropes/corner)"), metres ×2; §4 naming no Comedown of its own, the shared one; the manager spending the meter as an opening, since it leaves with the manifest; the flags' names `story:manifest:<NAME>` and `mastery:zone:<NAME>`.
- Inheritance (C38) is the tier's mechanics on §1's rules, no more: no legacy source is authored (the departed coach's slot waits for the content lane: whose Inheritance, which house) and no card carries a second house (NP_04 calls MARTA dual-house without naming her second line; NP_07A gives Tam two lineages; both are the content lane's to write — the gates build one); the source's stance and animation set, his standing at the apron mirroring the stance and the legacy's film grain are the greybox's capsules; enemy transforms and the weathering are not built (Awakening is C39's; both are C40's); no technique-slot model (NP_07 §5's hybrid tax) and no hybrid research (NP_08 §6; C41's). Readings taken: the sources in the order the story, then the bond, then the blood — a legacy with its flag set and its house written, then the Sworn stablemate with the most Kizuna points (the stable's order on a tie), dressed or not, then his own second house — a source carrying a house (the tier is a house style) other than his own; "between the pair" both ways; Inheritance over the Manifest and the Zone, so a fighter with a source can no longer SURGE into a lower tier (NP_09 names no choice; §6's "chosen prior tier" suggests one for Awakening); the trait beside his own, read as the source would carry it (lineage, disowned, a last-of-line's doubling), the larger where both carry the same, a stat trait scaling his stats (clamped to 99) while he wears it with the trait's own house's feuds; his house rings (the house matchup, the feuds' Tension, ALLIED's bonds) staying his — the house worn is the style, not the membership; the natives as a stablemate's unlocked set (an UNSANCTIONED source lends none), a legacy's or a second house's the house's set, SECRETs excepted, the move the source carries first, each answering its motion after an open T3 and before the card's own move while the meter can pay it; "cast from the private 50-pt meter" as the natives only — his own move and the relay's T3s pay the team bar — SAVE THE BAR not holding the meter, half a broken cast back to it, the rest leaving with the Inheritance; "enemies literally misread you" as the enemy's read at startup (C6/C7: the roll and its odds unchanged) always a misread — no dodge planned, no guard — the sim having no enemy PI on the player; a feint drawing nothing from a misread; "stance/animation set" as presentation, his style package staying his; the Comedown's "own dialect scrambled 10 s" as his feints drawing nothing for 10 s, beside §1's 85 %; the second house at 75 read as "the whole bar" at 75, emptied, with no bond needed; the manager spending the meter on the strongest native (the DMG in it).
- Awakening (C39) is the summit's mechanics on §1's rules, no more: the SECRET gate events and the Arc 3 trials are flags the story sets (seeded with `-- flag=`); the sealed house's unsealing itself (NP_07 §7), the hybrid techniques that would fill its slot (NP_08 §6's research, built since C41), the element-storm arena takeover, the house crest and the leitmotif (the greybox's lit ring) and a menu for the two choices (the sim's `choose_surge`, seeded with `-- surge=` and `-- form=`) are not built, nor NP_11's `zone_unlocked`, `manifest_id` and `awakening_flag` Fighter fields (the save writes the two choices). Readings taken: the gate as the flags `secret:<NAME>` and `story:trial:<NAME>` (whether a houseless fighter has a SECRET is the story's call), the protagonist as RORY (NP_05's "protagonist signature line") with `story:sealed_house_resolved` besides, and a prior tier unlocked (it stacks one); "a prior tier's effect at 75 % strength" by effect kind — a multiplier's reach from ×1 (×0.75 → ×0.8125, ×1.4 → ×1.3, ×2 → ×1.75), an amount (+200 ms → +150, a 50-point meter → 37.5, +30 % → +22.5), a period's frequency (every 3 s → every 4 s), a state's fraction (stamina 75 % frozen: its moves at a quarter; three reads in four misread; the inherited trait's magnitudes at 75 %, a use count kept whole) — a timing and a route kept (the echo's 0.3 s; what the meter pays), the tier's own identity kept whole (the manifest's figure, the Inheritance's source); "chosen" as the card's, between duals, his highest prior tier by default, one he has unlocked; the SURGE's choice of a lower tier an addition answering C38's flag (NP_09 names no choice); "+1 hybrid technique slot active" as a slot opened on nothing (since C41 holding one of his hybrids); the caps ×1.75 for his side only, on every advantage the sim caps (the hissatsu's ring product, the house traits' multipliers), the opponent's staying ×1.5; the refund as 15 flat to the team bar per drop he scores (a flash drop and an echo's drop too), outside the earning modifiers, to 100; the Comedown as the standard, without the stacked tier's riders.
- Hissatsu geometry uses the family's punch row for the dodge check; a move's own reach/arc is not modelled beyond `range_plus`. Duo variants execute since C34 (their VFX and camera ids are carried; the greybox gilds the afterimage); hybrid parents are read by the Forge since C41 (an authored hybrid's two houses); camera scripts and VFX sets are carried as data and not executed. `all_stats_r4r5_plus5` is a no-op in the 3-segment mini-dual (R4/R5 do not exist there). `scout_book_note_on_hit` and `copies_last_player_hissatsu_vfx` are events only (meta-progression and presentation).

- The enemy's transformations (C40) are the mechanics on §1's rules and one authored line, no more.
  - **What is not built.**
    - Vasile's is the roster's only line: which of the other 23 bosses transform, into what and when is the content lane's. The Manifest runs on a test's line and the greybox aid's.
    - No boss has a named manifest figure; the tier runs as "<boss>'s manifest".
    - No boss casts Inheritance or Awakening (NP_09 names no enemy use).
    - The presentation is the greybox's: the gathering's swell, the boss lit in his element, a capsule behind him, HUD lines and toasts. There is no arena reaction, no camera break and no authored commentary.
    - Stood in the Storm is a ledger entry: no achievements screen and no commentary legend.
    - The Clash is timing on the ordinary keys: no QTE art and no scripted camera.
  - **Readings taken.**
    - Vasile's "T4 usage at the 50% mark" (NP_04B §24) read in three parts:
      - "T4" as NP_05's transformation class;
      - the tier as the Zone, NP_09's universal tier, since he has no house and no named manifest (the Manifest is a one-line edit of his pattern);
      - the mark as 50 % composure. The alternative is a clock reading (the 45 s mark).
      The "fight's hidden achievement" for weathering it is read as Stood in the Storm.
    - "The player must feel it coming" as three things: a tell at the ARMORED floor, run on fight time behind his guard, with his line said; a gathering nothing interrupts (NP_05 §9's Enemy T4, "not interruptible"); and his hooks, desperation and survival grab held while it runs.
    - The trigger checked between his actions, never mid-string.
    - His Zone as the player's mirror: his timers ×1/0.75 outside his tells, his acting +15 %, the auto-slip on the player's punches, his stamina frozen. There is no counter-window analogue, since bosses counter by authored string (C13).
    - His Manifest without a meter, since bosses cast by composure (C14): damage ×1.4 on punches and hissatsu, the echo on his clean punches, DISPLACE ×2.
    - Both tiers at the table's durations, his bar emptied, once per boss per dual, and the shared Comedown on every stat.
    - Weathering as the player's alone (§7 honours "the player who saved nothing"):
      - 1 s per perfect-defense action: a perfect block; a slip, whether his own dodge or his Zone's auto-slip; or a Clash exchange won;
      - his damage halved from the first weathering for the rest of it;
      - both a first draft of §7's "reduce incoming transform damage 50% and shave 1 s off its duration each" (the alternative: a halving compounding per action).
    - Stood in the Storm as at least one weathering, no transformation of the player's while it ran, and never knocked down (a Clash's suppression is an answer).
    - The Clash's window as 180 ms at ×0.5 time, 360 ms either side of the cue. An early press is lost; the enemy strikes first; the clocks wait. The delegated card presses on the cue six times in ten and ignores the pad.
    - The enemy side's strings saying "the enemy", not "he".

- Hybrid research (C41) is the Forge's rules on the sim's ledgers and relay, no more.
  - **What is not built.**
    - The Forge event is its feed line and event. Its scene, the hybrid's own VFX and camera (ids carried; the greybox tints the afterimage in both elements) and the name generator's full motif sets are not built. The 13 motif words are a first draft for the content lane.
    - Nothing in the sim earned Beans before C43 (the training slots): the greybox seeds the purse with `-- purse=`. Since C43 the Forge is hosted at The Wall's T3.
    - No roster card of House Delacroix-Cardinal exists, so the E07 gate builds a stand-in.
    - The Toronto academy's industrialized hybrids (NP_07 §10) are C46's syntheses.
    - The technique-slot model (NP_07 §5's hybrid tax) is not built. (The 2v2 exhibition's simultaneous duo moves, NP_01 §8, are C42's.)
    - The Delacroix-Cardinal elder's hook ("after your first dual using Two Rivers") is dated in the registry; its questline is the overworld's.
  - **Readings taken.**
    - The requirement:
      - Sworn by the economy's Kizuna effect.
      - Two primary houses; a sealed house forges nothing until it opens.
      - "A native technique learned" as his house's natives when NATIVE, its SECRET with his `secret:<NAME>` flag, or the house native his card carries (an UNSANCTIONED recruit knows what he carries).
    - "A Forge event (Beans + Spirits cost + a scene)" as between duals (or before the first bell), paying 40 Spirits and 30 Beans from the ledger: a first draft against NP_11 §6's 3–8 Spirits a dual and 3–10 Beans a training slot.
    - One hybrid per pair ("unique to that pair").
    - Q-NGUYEN-ROY's "arms the Two Rivers Forge pairing early" and "Forge discount for E07-pair hybrids" as a pair across E07, with `quest:third_bridge` set, forging at Duo for half. The flag's name is editorial.
    - The output:
      - The library's authored hybrid of the pair's two houses, while unclaimed, is that pairing's hybrid.
      - "Name generated from both house motifs" as the motifs joined in the houses' order, with the pair's names after a name already forged (the Duo's rule).
      - "Effect composed from both native families' effect tags" as the composer's rules, derived to rebuild Grain & Snow exactly: the stronger learned native's family and damage +0.1 over the T3 floor, a tag from each house, seconds capped at 2, 2100 ms + 100 a tag.
    - "Both fighters can cast it" as either of the pair, under NP_05 §3's T3 rule (CHARGED or bonded adjacency).
    - "Strongest when relayed between them" as the partner fighting the segment before, either order: ×1.25 damage and the partner's afterimage. The ×1.25 is a first draft.
    - The hybrid answers its motion before the pair's Duo T3, which it shadows on a shared family.
    - The Awakening's "+1 hybrid slot" as one of his hybrids live for the Awakening, the card's choice between duals, his first forged by default.
    - A DUAL element takes no same-element spike and no home bonus, as the library's two authored hybrids have read since C10.
    - The registry kept on the reward ledger and ordered by forge number, written to NP_11 §9's `hybrid_registry`.

- The Exhibition 2v2 (C42) is NP_01 §8's format and NP_05 §7's duo sync on two engagements, no more.
  - **What is not built.**
    - A four-body ring. The two pairs never touch: no fighter can reach the other pair's opponent, there are no tag-ins and no cross-pair collisions, and each pair fights on the ring's own geometry (ropes, corners, zones). The greybox draws them in halves, with the lateral compressed; that is presentation only.
    - Co-op. There is one pad. The "both players" of NP_05 §7 are the pad and a partner on his profile; a second pair of hands is `step_pair`'s other input, which the tests drive.
    - The festival's calendar and venue (NP_02 §1's summer boardwalk). The format runs whenever it is asked for; since C43, hosting it at home is The Ring's T2.
    - An enemy duo. The enemy pair never syncs, as bosses cast by composure. There are no corners in its one segment.
    - A mid-exhibition save. The SaveGame is one DualState; the exhibition's snapshot is the coordinator's own and is not written to a file (Shift+F5 is refused).
    - The duo moves' own VFX and camera. The ids are carried; the greybox gilds both capsules. There are no duo callouts or barks.
  - **Readings taken.**
    - **The model (the effort named).** Two engagements in lockstep under a coordinator, rather than a four-body sim, which would re-plumb every P/B rule in the core. C6's one resolution function stands.
    - **The pairs.**
      - A is the first card against the boss asked for; B is his partner against the enemy's second, the boss list's next.
      - Each engagement holds the pair on its card, so "adjacent" is side by side in the ring and Adjacent Trust is live in both.
    - **The shared state.**
      - One bar: each engagement's change is summed into the team's bar, capped at 100. The enemy's one bar and the building's one crowd heat work the same way.
      - The ring clock is the lowest of the pairs still fighting. A pair's own hitstop or count never stops the bell.
      - A pair KO'd is finished and stands scored; the other fights on to the bell. The pad passes to the pair still fighting.
    - **The verdict and the pay.**
      - NP_01 §3.3's ladder on the pairs' sums (score, segment wins, counter hits). A tie is a DRAW, with no Golden DROP at a festival (`sudden_death` false).
      - It pays once, as a dual (NP_11 §6): both fighters' closing XP, the pair's adjacent Kizuna once, Spirits and HBP once. The recruitment flags and the Gym Wall's poster come from pair A's club.
    - **The duo sync.**
      - "Input the motion": the family's motion. LB + the uppercut flick calls RISE, Double Overtime; LB + block held 400 ms calls WALL, Two-Hand Winter. These are the library's two `sync_500ms` moves, found by the format's `sync_ms`.
      - "Within 500 ms": the first opens a window of that length, and the other's same motion inside it casts the move in both engagements.
      - A partner on his profile answers at 250 ms, a first draft.
      - Unanswered, the held motion executes as it would have: the flick as his own, next tick; the WALL as his own at once.
      - The motions a duo takes are throws, not arm feints.
    - **The duo's price and hold.**
      - The bar pays the caller's price once, NP_06 §2.1's element edge included. Each half carries half of it for an interrupt's refund. It is live at Lv4 Duo (`exhibition_duo`, NP_08 §5.2's "2v2 exhibition duo hissatsu").
      - The bar is the pair's call. Both hold it while a window is open, and a profile holds it at 90 while the duo can be made (its own hissatsu waits), so an AI partner leaves the call to the pad.
      - In manager mode the profiles call it at 90: Two-Hand Winter when either of the pair is below half his composure, else Double Overtime once both are inside the uppercut's reach (+0.55 m for the cast's reach and the windup's lunge).
    - **Two-Hand Winter's one gauge.**
      - The first half resolved raises it: its points are that fighter's (his rings), and pair A's on a same-tick resolve. The other's hands join it.
      - Its TRUE OPEN runs on its owner's clock, handed to the other pair if the owner is finished.
    - The festival moves carry no afterimage: the partner is in the ring.

- The club facilities (C43) are NP_10 §7's tiers and their effects on the sim's ledgers, no more.
  - **What is not built.**
    - The renovations' scenes (the visible Old Gym coming back to life) and the Arc 3 televised dual. The greybox names the tier and hangs its photograph.
    - The training minigames themselves. A slot takes the minigame's grade (the aid's 0.75); Mitts, the Heavy Bag and the rest are the overworld's games. (Typed Beans, a partner's flavour and the stat raises they buy are C44's.)
    - The ranked spar board's ranking. The Ring's T3 opens the venue; the board's order and rewards are not in the docs.
    - (The mirror spar as a lab flag is C47's: the boss agent runs the tracker's record as a pattern.)
    - (Respec at the Film Room is C44's.)
    - The tactics the facilities teach (NP_10 §5 names them without a track).
    - The entrance's presentation beyond an event and a toast.
    - The honours set's pieces beyond the crest and the feat.
  - **Readings taken.**
    - **The tiers.**
      - "T1 → T3 effects" as the effects in the table's listed order, one a tier; a capacity (the Scout Book's pages, the wall's columns, the entrance, the Beans' yield) grows at every tier after.
      - The club starts every track at T0, the Old Gym as found (NP_12A's condemned gym).
      - A renovation is between duals or before the first bell.
    - **The price and the gate.**
      - One price and one story gate a tier across the tracks: 150/5, 400/15 and 800/30 (first drafts against NP_11 §6's 50–150 HBP and 3–8 Spirits a session).
      - The gates are the charter saved, the funding quest after the failed inspection, and the Nationals (NP_12 §4 beats 2, 5 and 7). The flag names are editorial.
    - **Bags & Mitts.**
      - Roadwork (The Hill) and Net Haul (the harbour) need no gym. Mitts and the Heavy Bag are T1; NP_11 §7's T2 and T3 labels are the Slip Bag and Shadow Rounds.
      - "+25/50%" is T2 and T3's yield on every slot.
      - A slot pays lo + the grade's share of (hi − lo). Roadwork with a squad member along bonds at the spar's repeatable rate (+3, under the day's cap).
      - Shadow Rounds ghosts a Scout Book pattern, the first on the shelf unless named.
    - **The Ring.**
      - The lab flags you choose are The Ring's; an authored bout's (the Priya trial) are the story's.
      - "2v2 exhibition hosting" is the Exhibition 2v2 at home rather than the festival.
      - The ranked spar is the CRBF Annex's (NP_02 §2.2).
    - **The Film Room.**
      - "Scout Book capacity" is the shelf, and the shelf is the page in hand: a page past it waits, and PI 3 with it (NP_02 §3's "their pattern page"). A ledger from before the shelf shelves its pages in the ledger's order.
      - "PI grind rate +" is the scrap and spar rungs sooner; a dual segment counts as before.
      - "Decoy detection assist" is the tape: a corner that has run a decoy seal on the club is read on sight. The tactician's Lv3 read stands beside it.
    - **The Wall.**
      - "Bond board slots" are card columns added to the Gym Wall table's base wall.
      - "Decoration sets" is the honours set: a blessed house's crest and a feat's plaque, hung from the tier on (the ones earned before it go up with it).
      - "Forge event hosting" means the Forge needs The Wall at T3 ("a Forge event at the gym", NP_08 §6).
    - **Front of House.**
      - The entrance is the club's walk-out at every dual (NP_01 §9): the sting, the banner, the chant, and the Reyes bell as its quest's piece (NP_07A: "the bell rings at your entrances (Front of House upgrade)"). The exhibition's pair A carries it.
      - "Crowd heat base +" and "Prestige from hosted duals" apply to a hosted dual only, at 20 and 5 (first drafts). Prestige is paid won or lost.
      - A dual is away unless hosted (`-- host=home`); the greybox's duals were all away before.
    - **The tape** is kept from any tier (the footage exists); only T3 reads it.

- The fighter's growth (C44) is NP_11 §3, §4 and §6 on the sim's ledgers, no more.
  - **What is not built.**
    - The boards' presentation: the hex screen and the node art. A node is its name, its effect, its price and its links.
    - The other 31 passives of the 40-passive launch pool: the content lane's. The table carries NP_11 §3's nine, live, and the pool's size.
    - (NP_11 §2's technique slots and the T0 signature at 5 are C45's; NP_11 §5's gear in the accessory slots is C48's; NP_11 §10's obsolescence audit as a gate is C49's.)
    - The enemy's boards: the passives are the player's side. A boss's card is authored, and the mirror carries its fighter's grown stats but not his passives.
    - (NP_02 §2.2's mirror lab flag is C47's. Since C47 the trial's mirror is the same copy: the card on his record's strings.)
    - The minigames themselves (C43); a slot's type is its game's bias.
  - **Readings taken.**
    - **The board.**
      - One layout per style: the spine's stance and the style's three stats, the Body lobe's every stat and the style's +5s, a breaker per stat at the far edge; the Craft and Spirit lobes as strips of hexes with the passives at their depths. The layouts and every price are first drafts.
      - The craft passives split by style (above); the Spirit passives on every board; the Boxer-Puncher's two craft passives and the socket for one of the other three. The socket changes freely between duals once its node is taken.
      - "70+ costs double board points" is a stat node's price when the stat stands at 70+ as grown; a stat's "soft-cap breaker" lifts the doubling for that stat. The raises' Beans do not double.
      - A stat node at the rarity's cap is passable, adding nothing past it, so a card whose own stat is over the cap (RORY's HRT 99) is not walled out of a lobe.
      - Board Points are 2 × the level from level 1. Respec refunds every point and empties the socket; the Beans a raise spent stay spent.
      - The named nodes: two for each of the eight Millbrook cards, named from their cards (first drafts); a recruit's two are the rule's (+3 on his two best stats); a generic has none.
      - The aid (`"*"`, `-- demo growth`) takes a passive in reach, else the first step toward the nearest one, else the stat node that gives most for its price.
    - **The raises and the Beans.**
      - "+1 = 5 Beans, scaling" is +1 Bean for each raise already bought on that stat.
      - The Beans' types are the stats. A slot's Beans take its game's bias ("any" is untyped); a partner along types a quarter of them (rounded) as his best grown stat, ties in the stats' order.
      - The untyped Beans (seeded, a quest's drip, Shadow Rounds) pay any raise; another stat's typed Beans never do. The Forge spends the untyped first, then the typed in the stats' order.
    - **Rarity.**
      - The caps bound growth only: a card's own stats stand as dressed. The Club's cap is 70, NP_11 §1's soft cap. (Since C50 a card under his tier's total is lifted to it, NP_11 §1's stat totals by rarity.)
      - The trials: NP_11 §4 names two (a mirror match, weathering a transform) for three ascensions. Circuit's is a spar won (editorial), National's a transform weathered (C40's Stood in the Storm, by the fighter in the ring), the Legend's the mirror beaten. A trial is marked on the ledger when done, whatever tier he stands at.
      - "+1 equip slot for accessories" as a slot a tier to the schema's three (1 / 2 / 3 / 3).
      - The Awakening flag: a card not marked `generic` is a story character, pre-flagged; a generic's is the Legend's. C39's story gate still applies to both.
    - **At the bell.** The grown stats replace the card's at `_load_segment`, and the carried condition, the formation's sums and STAT_PROOF read them. Nothing grows during a dual.

- Techniques and the slots (C45) are NP_11 §2 and §8 on the sim's ledgers, no more.
  - **What is not built.**
    - The shop's counter and the tapes' finds: a found tape and a festival prize are seeded (`-- tapes=`); only the quests' tapes arrive with their flags. No festival prize table.
    - The gift ceremony (NP_07 §4: a house gifts a native to an outsider, once a save): natives stay membership-only.
    - The marriage-line's tax (NP_07 §5: both native sets at −1 slot each): no dual-house card exists.
    - A T0 identity move for the cards without one — RORY, MARCUS, the recruits. The library's 13 T0s are six natives, three shop rows, Priya's, a generic (June's) and two taught; the content lane's. Their T0 slot takes a learned T0 meanwhile.
    - NP_05 §3's defensive boards (the `board:anchor` T2 walls), the worlds bosses' and the syndicate's rows: not the club's to learn yet. (Since C50 the board's walls are, behind the Anchor Wall; the worlds bosses' and the syndicate's rows are still not.)
    - Masters beyond the list: befriending a fighter the club has not signed, and the other staff roles.
    - An in-fight choice between two kit moves of one family: the first in the kit answers the motion.
  - **Readings taken.**
    - **The slots.** "5 slots max + natives ADD": the card's own move stands outside the five and the natives take none. The T0 slot opens at level 5 and holds one T0 (NP_05 §3: "every fighter gets exactly one"). A card's own T0 is it and castable from level 5 (NP_12A's Marginalia); another T0 (the shop's, a tape's, Roadwork) fills the slot when his own move is not a T0.
    - **The sources by the library's `source`.**
      - `shop:*` and the generic rows go to the shop (a generic T0 on t1, a generic T1 on t2).
      - `taught:*` goes to Whitlock and the conditioning coach; `taught` (Black Ice) is a found tape.
      - `personal:*` is the recruit's own list, Priya's identity T0 and Anders' excepted. `quest:*` is the quest's tape.
    - **The shop.** "Tiers by circuit progress": t1 as found, t2 the Regionals (`story:regionals`), t3 the Provincials (`story:provincials_semifinal`), at 100 / 400 / 800 HBP (first drafts inside NP_11 §6's 100–800). A move bought is one fighter's. Since C57 t2 and t3 also open by the Downtown Strip's rep (10 / 25).
    - **The masters.** "Recruit/befriend → they teach their list": a card in the stable, the coach always, a staff role while one is dressed; free, the gate the price. Whitlock's "the player's first survived standing-eight" is the learner's own. The gates on DEX, CARTER and CASSIDY are first drafts.
    - **The re-teach** is one of the day's three training slots (C43's), no Beans, and passes what the teacher learned — never his own move, his natives or a hybrid.
    - A move learned goes straight into the kit when there is room. The profile casts his own move when it can, else the kit's first. The Forge's hybrid (C41) is known and cast by its own rule, never slotted.

- The academy's syntheses (C46) are NP_07 §10's thesis as a squad and a rule on the sim, no more.
  - **What is not built.**
    - The thesis fight itself: the Worlds, Team Canada and the final dual's staging (NP_12 §6). `-- academy` fields the squad against whatever the club dresses. NP_12 §6's strongest version — the academy absorbed into Team Canada, Vey in the player's corner — is not built: the academy fights on the enemy side only.
    - The prodigies' names and cards: ASCENDANT 1–5 are placeholder handles for the content lane. Vasile's Meridian (NP_04B: Vey's thesis continent-wide) stays his own pattern boss; the squad does not borrow him. Vey recruiting the club's beaten rivals between arcs (NP_12's "the anti-you") is not built: the squad is the table's five. Since C56 it is: the rivals the academy signs fight ahead of the prodigies.
    - The synthesis's own VFX and camera: the ids are carried (`vfx_synth`, the family's T3 camera); the greybox toasts it and draws no afterimage.
    - "Spiritually hollow" beyond the numbers: nothing the crowd, the commentary or the corner says of a synthesis.
    - Tuning. The prodigies are tier-3 generics: the academy soaks stood 2–12 against the default four and 7–18 against the forged pair's five when they ended. The balance is the content lane's.
  - **Readings taken.**
    - "Industrialized synthesis without bonds" as the Forge's own composer run on two houses' natives from the library, with no pair and no native learned, so a synthesis and a forged hybrid of the same houses share family and tags and differ by the price-down.
    - "Slightly weaker per move" as ×0.85 on the damage; "overwhelming in breadth" as three a prodigy, fifteen distinct over the twelve houses with natives against the Forge's ceiling of eight. The ×0.85, the three, the house pairs and the reach +0.3 are first drafts.
    - The cast as the enemy's own: bosses cast by composure (C14), and a prodigy has no bond to relay by, so his bar pays at his decision point, within reach, the next in his rotation. An interrupt refunds the enemy side nothing (the half refund is the player's, as before C46).
    - The prodigies as C24's generic cards on a house's dialect with the house left out (drilled in its dialect, not of it: no trait, no home bonus). Pattern Intel ticks against the dialect as for any generic.
    - The club's answer ("duo VFX and feud/alliance history") as C41's relay: ×1.25 with the partner's afterimage. The THESIS line sets the numbers side by side.
    - Director Vey's corner (`ascendant_a`, C13's record) coaches the squad as authored.

- The mirror spar (C47) is NP_02 §2.2's lab flag as the tracker's record fought as a pattern, no more.
  - **What is not built.**
    - A copy of how he moves: the record is the tracker's tokens (rows and feints). His footwork, his guard, his body-or-head choice, his power and his timing are not in it. The copy walks and guards as the Style's generic does, and a feint is a pause.
    - A copy of his defense beyond C7's read: the copy dodges by the sim's own rule (the correct dodge for the punch), not his slip-or-duck habit.
    - His tells: the copy's are the dialect's, on the gym's channel for a card without a house.
    - Presentation: the copy wears the card; no mirror visuals.
  - **Readings taken.**
    - "A copy of your own fighter" as C44's mirror card on his record. "The adaptation tracker turned into content" as the tracker's tokens and reads kept: what he throws is the copy's offense, and what the tracker reads is its defense.
    - The record is the card's, whoever plays him: the delegated profile's punches count. In manager mode the copy is the profile's habits on that card.
    - Ranking by thrown × length, the tracker's count (3) as the floor, the tier's count as the cap, the three reads seeded, the counter's minimum (2) and weight (×1.3), and the 200 ms pause are first drafts.
    - His counter as his most-thrown punch into his own windows, at his rate. It is fixed for the bout: the copy does not adapt in the fight (D3).
    - A mirror books no Pattern Intel (NP_02 §3's PI is an opponent's dialect's). Any mirror beaten meets the Legend's trial (NP_11 §4: "beat a mirror match").
    - C24's "named strings never appear" is the pieces' rule. A habit is his own sequence and may match a named boss's actions.
    - The record grows the save: the determinism log's save at tick 12,000 reads 239 KB (was 218).

- Accessories and gear (C48) are NP_11 §5 on the sim's ledgers, no more.
  - **What is not built.**
    - The gear's art: the greybox's figure wears coloured shapes; the looks are a line of text each.
    - Trading: nothing is sold back, swapped between clubs or given away. "Untradeable" is read as bound to its owner.
    - NP_11 §5's "small passive slots" beyond one passive a piece. No piece carries two effects or a set bonus.
    - Gear on the enemy's side: the named bosses' gear is their tells' (the visual-gear channel, NP_04 §3), not the locker's. The mirror (C47) wears none.
    - The rest of the circuit's prizes and the festival's calendar: three prizes on the story's flags, three for the boardwalk in turn.
  - **Readings taken.**
    - "≤3% effects; gear never outweighs boards" as one passive a piece, its kind's, on the board passive's own hook. One piece of each kind means at most the cap on any key, against 15–30 % on the board.
    - The kinds' keys (gloves the jab, wraps the guard held, a mouthguard the wobble, a robe the walk-out's Tension), the percentages, the names, looks and prices are first drafts for the content lane.
    - "Signature gear … untradeable identity" as in the locker while its owner is in the club, worn only by him, never sold. It waits there to be put on (the aid puts it on its owner first); `signature_worn_from_start` in the table reverses that.
    - "House gifts" as a blessed house's colours, once, the piece by the house's style bias. They are named by rule, never after a cultural object (NP_07 §9).
    - "Circuit prizes" as the story's flags; "festival events" as an Exhibition 2v2 won on the boardwalk (C42's festival venue).
    - The story's pieces are derived from the ledgers, not stored, so a load, the sim it came from and every new dual agree. What is bought or won at the festival is the ledger's.
    - The club keeps one locker; a piece bought twice is two copies.

- The obsolescence audit (C49) is NP_11 §10's gate on the sim, no more.
  - **What is not built.**
    - The fixes it calls for: its verdict on this build is RED (techniques for all three, growth for PRIYA), and the fixes are PAC-096's. (C50 makes them; the verdict is GREEN since.)
    - Playing the trials: the audit marks them through their own code paths (the bout's end, the transform's end) with the outcome given; whether the fighter could win them is the fights' question.
    - The economy's pacing: the budget is a first draft. Whether a story-pace player has 95 Spirits, 100 Beans and 3,000 HBP for one fighter by Arc 3 is NP_11 §6's faucet audit, not this one. (C51 runs it: on this build the faucet pays the Spirits and the Beans for the three, not the HBP, and a story-pace protagonist stands at level 21, not 35.)
    - A best build: the builder is one reasonable build (the aid's nodes, the lowest stat raised, the answering kit), not an optimiser. A fighter it fails may pass another build; the audit says which system the build ran out on.
    - The milestone's calendar: the audit runs when asked (`-- audit`), not per milestone automatically.
  - **Readings taken.**
    - "The three earliest-recruitable fighters" as NP_12 §4's order of recruitment beats: Priya (Ep1), Dex (Building five), Yuri (the Regionals pool, first named).
    - "The current endgame" as the arc-3 patterns at NP_11 §2's Arc 3 level.
    - "Viability (tier 4, competitive board, coherent technique kit)" as:
      - tier 4;
      - the board spent with passives on it;
      - every slot the level opens holding a move that answers a motion (a slot no move can use is a hollow slot);
      - plus holding the endgame: half the segments won against its bosses.
    - "The failing system gets fixed" as named, with the numbers: rarity, the board, techniques, growth, or the fight itself when the stats stand level with the reference's.
    - The late recruits as the reference: an early recruit is obsolete if the systems cannot bring him to where the latest arrivals stand. Their cards are the recruit table's tier-3 block (C29's first draft), so the reference moves with it.
    - The endgame XP applies the card's own XP curve (the no-name's +10 %), so a houseless fighter gets his hidden levels.

- The audit's fixes (C50) are the two named systems' repairs, no more.
  - **What is not built.**
    - Level 45's fifth slot. Five slots against the four families his own move leaves free (three stick families and WALL) leaves one slot hollow again. That is the postgame (NP_11 §2: 50 is postgame polish); an audit at level 45 would name it.
    - Anchor-class as a card's own class: the formation reads it from a squad (C27), so a card's kit would move with who else is dressed. The defensive board is every card's route to the wall.
    - A player's choice of where the rarity's lift goes: it follows the card's own shape.
    - The wall's element: the three walls are open to every card. The audit's builder takes the first by name, the aid's lesson the first in the list.
    - The economy's pacing (NP_11 §6's faucet audit, C51) and the fight's tuning (PAC-052's lane).
  - **Readings taken.**
    - "Stat totals by rarity keep early recruits viable" as the least total at each tier, the card's own stats scaled in proportion. National's 490 is the late recruits' card (the recruit table's 70 a stat), so the catch-up ends where they begin and never moves them. The Legend adds the 99 cap and the Awakening, not a total.
    - "Anchor-class fighters + defensive boards" as a node on every style's board. Taking the Anchor Wall makes a board defensive, and a T2 in a slot needs it (NP_05 §3's unlock). NP_05 §10's "Anchor board" source is the board that teaches the walls.
    - The aid's reason to take the node: a technique slot only a WALL can fill.

- The faucet/sink audit (C51) is NP_11 §6's gate on the sim, no more.
  - **What is not built.**
    - The fixes it calls for: its verdict on this build is RED (all five currencies), and the fixes are PAC-098's. (C52 makes them; the verdict is GREEN since.)
    - Playing the playthrough: every fight is paid with its result given. A segment's own XP is the one measured quantity, from the obsolescence audit's builds against its endgame, so an Arc 1 segment is paid as an endgame one.
    - A calendar: sessions and days are counted, not lived. The Kizuna the duals pay is carried and not audited.
    - The optional duals (NP_02 §5's 2–4 between story duals), quests, items, the festival and the scouting machine: none pays or charges on the sim, so none is in the faucet or the sinks.
    - Spending as it goes: the faucet spends nothing, so a renovation's higher Beans yield is read off the phase's gym, not bought.
  - **Readings taken.**
    - "Story-pace" as the critical path: NP_12 §8's episodes, NP_02 §5's cadence and session shape at their midpoints. Arc 2 is at Arc 1's length (editorial).
    - NP_11 §6's "Earn (typical/session)" as per 45-minute session (NP_02 §5).
    - "Desired sinks" as the sanctioned dual's eight built as the obsolescence audit builds, every facility track to T3 and a Forge. The 70 % target has a ±15 band either side (editorial): under it the player is starved of choices, over it the sinks stop being choices.
    - The captain fights the overworld, so the protagonist's level is the most a story-pace fighter reaches.

- The faucet audit's fixes (C52) are the five named currencies' repairs, no more.
  - **What is not built.**
    - The overworld that calls `story_mark`: there are no quests, districts or calendar on the greybox scene. The playthrough and the tests call the verb, and a seeded flag stays unpaid.
    - District rep milestones, circuit placements and a scout's passive Prestige (NP_08 s1, s2.4): Prestige is still paid by scraps and hosted duals alone. Since C53 a scout's is paid (his district's, when the day turns); rep and placements are not. Since C55 the rep milestones are paid; circuit placements are not.
    - The pull's base rare rate (only NP_08's pity is canon, so only the pity makes a lead rare). Leads to people with no card beyond Vera Iron's condition. A choice between open-mat generics beyond the first.
    - The generics' stable roles (training partner, scout, cornerman-in-training, NP_08 s1): a signed generic dresses and fights like any card. Since C53 any card on the bench takes a role.
    - Level 45's fifth slot, and NP_08 s9's ship-size content (60 named, 240 generics): the machine's wanted Prestige reads the recruit table as it stands (13 named and Vera).
  - **Readings taken.**
    - The XP amounts as first drafts under a canon curve, so one pace calibrates them.
    - NP_11 s6's "Spirits 3–8" as a session's pay.
    - NP_02 s5's "recruitment step" as a quest step, and NP_02 s4's quests as HBP's.
    - NP_08 s2.4's rare-tier leads as the named recruits (and a String Wall lead the docs give a condition), the commons as the open mat's generics. NP_11 s6's open-mat priority as signing off an open-mat day.
    - The obsolescence budget as the faucet's share of a fighter at the 70 % target.

- The stable roles (C53) are NP_08 §1's three jobs on the sim's ledgers, no more.
  - **What is not built.**
    - The locker room's browse by slot-role (NP_08 §4): the scene's key sets the aid's next role, not a card and a role of the player's choosing. The sim takes any (`assign_role`). Since C54 the locker room browses by slot-role; setting a role is still the aid's key.
    - A trainee becoming a staffer: the corps is its table's ten (NP_08 §1's cap), and a trainee feeds their levels only.
    - A scout beyond NP_08 §1's Prestige and NP_10 §3's projection: no district rep, no leads from a scout, no rival duals watched.
    - The bench's own training: the aid trains the club's first card. An unnamed partner along on Roadwork bonds as a named one does.
  - **Readings taken.**
    - "Beans bonus keyed to their stats" as the partner's lead over the trainee at the game's stat: a partner teaches what the trainee lacks.
      - The effect is named here because it bears on the audit. The club phase's tier-1 generics (40 at every stat) lead a story-pace captain nowhere, so the faucet's Beans a slot stay at C52's 9.6.
      - NP_11 §6's 3–10 leaves 0.4 a slot of headroom at story pace. A partner paid on his own stats alone would break that band.
    - "Passive Prestige from their home district" as each covered district paying once a day.
    - "Feeds the staff system" as staff uses (NP_10 §6: level via use). NP_08 §1 cites "NP_10 §7" for the staff system; the staff system is §6, since §7 is the facilities.
    - A House card's home as the House's root (NP_07 §8) on the formation's five circuit districts, a houseless card's as Millbrook Secondary, a generic's as the machine's draw.
    - The story-pace bench as three open-mat generics from the circuit on, one to each role (the faucet's `pace.bench`).

- The locker room (C54) is NP_08 §4 on the sim's ledgers, no more.
  - **What is not built.**
    - The diegetic locker room and its crest wall: the browse is a DUAL OVER line. The comparison card is one card at a time, not two side by side.
    - Where a returned recruit is met: "return to their district" is the approach becoming possible again (the overworld's districts are not built), and the district is named on the lines.
    - A released story card: the story's four are never released (editorial).
    - Anything the released card does while away (fighting for a rival, joining another club). Since C56 a released recruit can be signed by the academy at an arc's turn and fight for it.
  - **Readings taken.**
    - "Rare" as the design's expectation, not a limit: the grudge is the cost.
    - "Reversible for 3 in-game days" as undoable until the third day turns (released on day 1, settled when day 4 begins), the undo restoring everything and leaving no memory.
    - "Remember (a small grudge/warmth flag)" as warmth with a bond left behind (Kizuna Lv1 to a stablemate who stays), else a grudge, each changing only the road back.
    - "Remain re-recruitable" as a recruit's approach and condition (waived by warmth, a spar added by a grudge), and a generic's open mat (free any day, or the priority always).
    - "Frees cap space" as NP_08 s1's 100 enforced on the recruit's answer too.

- District rep (C55) is NP_02 §2.1's and NP_08 §2.2 / §2.4's rep on the sim's ledger, no more.
  - **What is not built.**
    - The overworld's districts: a scrap's district is given (the scene's `-- district=`, the audit's phases). No walk puts a scrap anywhere.
    - NP_02 §4's vendor tiers: the shop's shelves still open by the story's flags (C45). Since C57 they open by the Downtown Strip's rep as well.
    - Rep from anything but scraps (duals at a venue, district events, quests).
    - A scout's rep: PAC-099's scout pays the district's Prestige and reads no rep.
  - **Readings taken.**
    - "Mid-scrap" as after the first exchange (the first tick of the fight), and "small district rep (−1)" as a point, never under 0.
    - A scrap won as +1 in its district (the docs name the flee's cost and not the gain).
    - "District rep milestones" as three thresholds, each paid once, their Prestige sized to NP_11 §6's shared "5–15 (scraps, rep)".
    - A scrap placed nowhere as moving no rep, so the older scrap gates stand.
    - The story-pace scraps' districts by phase as Ep1's (NP_12A), the circuit's questlines' (the rink, the harbour) and Arc 2's (the Hill, downtown), in turn.

- The anti-you (C56) is NP_12 §2's line on the sim's recruit ledger and the academy's squad, no more.
  - **What is not built.**
    - Arc 2's end as the story: `story:hollow_circuit` is a flag the overworld would set, not a beat the greybox plays.
    - Vey's recruiting beyond the recruit table: the String Wall's leads and the open mat's generics are not "beaten rivals".
    - Scenes, the defection's storyline (NP_12 §7: Ascendant's "the defector storyline"), and a rival's own kit carried to the academy (it takes a prodigy's).
    - Team Canada (NP_12 §6): the academy beaten gives its rivals back approachable; the absorption, and Vey in the club's corner, are not built.
  - **Readings taken.**
    - "Between arcs" as the arc's closing story flag landing through `story_mark` (the Nationals; the Hollow Circuit's end).
    - "YOUR beaten rivals" as the recruit table's fighters the club flagged and never signed, releases that stood included, in the order met, three a turn.
    - The academy's hold as ending when it is beaten in a dual, its rivals coming over (NP_12 §6's "absorbed into Team Canada", as far as the ledger goes).
    - A signed rival fielded with the displaced prodigy's syntheses (NP_07 §10's synthesis without bonds); a signed recruit with no pattern held, not fielded.

- Vendor tiers (C57) are NP_02 §4's line on the sim's two shops, no more.
  - **What is not built.**
    - Vendors beyond the two shops (the scouting machines, the arcade, a district's own stall), and stock that exists only by rep: every shelf holds what C45 and C48 put there.
    - Prices by rep (a regular's discount).
    - The walk to the Strip: the shops are menus between bouts.
  - **Readings taken.**
    - "Gates … vendor tiers" as a second key to a shelf the story already opens: the rep opens it early and never shuts one the story opened.
    - The shops on the Downtown Strip (NP_02 §1.1's "Shops (HBP spending)").
    - The rep as it stands, not its best: a flee below the line shuts the shelf until a win brings the rep back.
    - The thresholds on the rep milestones (10, 25), and a seeded rep flag counting as REP() counts it.

## Reproduction

```ps1
godot --headless --path engine/greybox -- smoke boss=5       # Alfie: referee ladder
godot --headless --path engine/greybox -- determinism
godot --headless --path engine/greybox -- geometry
godot --headless --path engine/greybox -- smoke boss=N      # N = 0..23
godot --headless --path engine/greybox --quit-after 3600 -- demo
godot --headless --path engine/greybox --quit-after 3600 -- demo seg2 boss=0   # CHARGED R2 on a FULL RELAY (since C35 the chain seeded to 3), full bar: the relay's North Star and the afterimage
godot --headless --path engine/greybox --quit-after 3600 -- demo ep1=scrap_2    # Ep1 by name; also scrap=SCR-06 boss=1, spar flags=tell_highlight facility=ring:1 (the lab is The Ring's T1 since C43)
godot --headless --path engine/greybox --quit-after 3600 -- demo boss=1 pi=kowalchuk:2   # a seeded recon ledger: his tells +10%, the commentary, the page when he is beaten
godot --headless --path engine/greybox --quit-after 3600 -- demo boss=1 remix=yuri_kowalchuk.rematch   # the rematch remix: Freight feints, Coupling quicker, burned after three
godot --headless --path engine/greybox --quit-after 3600 -- demo boss=16   # Vasile: the clock kits turn at 30s and 60s with the seam; the finale under 10%
godot --headless --path engine/greybox --quit-after 3600 -- demo boss=6 pi=tremblay:2,okafor:2   # Vey: the copied houses' PI at half against each kit
godot --headless --path engine/greybox --quit-after 3600 -- demo boss=4 ep1=scrap_1   # the scrap pays: "SCRAP: 17 XP, +2 Prestige", the ledger printed at the soak's end
godot --headless --path engine/greybox --quit-after 3600 -- demo boss=1 kizuna=RORY+PRIYA:50   # a seeded bond: Adjacent Trust, RORY at +5% and CHARGED at 20
godot --headless --path engine/greybox --quit-after 16000 -- demo boss=1 saveload   # the save: written at tick 3,000, loaded back at 3,600 on the same hash (SAVE/LOAD line)
godot --headless --path engine/greybox --quit-after 3600 -- demo boss=1 load=user://tnb_save.json   # a save resumed at the bell (the file the line above wrote)
godot --headless --path engine/greybox --quit-after 3600 -- demo boss=1 generic=SLUGGER:kowalchuk:2   # an assembled filler in R1: the feed names his pieces at the bell, Kowalchuk ticks, no page
godot --headless --path engine/greybox --quit-after 3600 -- demo boss=1 spar generic=OUTBOXER:tremblay:3   # a spar against a tier-3 generic of the Tremblay dialect
godot --headless --path engine/greybox --quit-after 3600 -- demo boss=7    # Carter's neutral game: the fast circle, the taunt every 10s (the HUD's HIS NEUTRAL line)
godot --headless --path engine/greybox --quit-after 3600 -- demo boss=22   # Tam: the angled walk, the low guard, the tempo shifting every 6s
godot --headless --path engine/greybox --quit-after 16000 -- demo boss=1 format=sanctioned talk kizuna=PRIYA+RORY:25   # The Talk in the corners, a Corner Voices bond naming Priya's read
godot --headless --path engine/greybox --quit-after 16000 -- demo formation board=ep1   # Ep1's whiteboard: the five picks, the Ep1 board, Ascendant B at 70%, the overlay; the reveal checked at the end
godot --headless --path engine/greybox --quit-after 16000 -- demo formation boss=6 format=sanctioned order=RELAY_LADDER staff=the_reveal:3,patch_job,raise_the_roof kizuna=RORY+SAM:50,SAM+MARCUS:50   # the ladder vs Vey's corner, the tactician's read
godot --headless --path engine/greybox -s tools/drive_formation.gd -- formation   # the whiteboard's keys driven on the real scene: 2 / 5, the Gym Wall's suggestion and 6, 4 before the bell, ENTER, TAB, 1 after it; the Duo relays each order holds (12 checks)
godot --headless --path engine/greybox --quit-after 16000 -- demo boss=1 staff=janitor:3,ring_announcer,professor   # a staffed corner: 10 Tension at the bell, the crowd heating, Raise the Roof converting, the Professor's PI
godot --headless --path engine/greybox --quit-after 16000 -- demo boss=0 format=iron staff=sportsmed:3,physio,janitor   # the cutman at Lv3 working RORY's carried condition in an Iron dual
godot --headless --path engine/greybox -s tools/drive_recruit.gd -- boss=1   # the recruits' keys on the real scene: the traits on the matchup card, a dual won on segments, P / L / P / L, the String Wall and the HOUSES lines after the join (8 checks)
godot --headless --path engine/greybox --quit-after 16000 -- demo boss=7 format=sanctioned   # the TRAITS line under each matchup card: Carter's Harvest at the bell (SAM's Never Twice waits in R5)
godot --headless --path engine/greybox --quit-after 16000 -- demo boss=17 format=sanctioned board=YURI,RORY,PRIYA,MARCUS,SAM   # traits on both corners in R1: YURI's Load-Bearing against Itzel's Debt of Honour, +25 % across E11
godot --headless --path engine/greybox --quit-after 60000 -- demo recruit boss=19 flag=quest:cold_storage   # two duals against Moira's squad (the scene's seed is the clock: a run may lose both); a win flags four, the approach surfaces them, Remy joins (quest:q_moreau until C30)
godot --headless --path engine/greybox --quit-after 60000 -- demo recruit boss=19 flag=quest:cold_storage,feud_resolved:moreau+tremblay   # E05 closed: since C31 E05 flips and the Moreau blessing lands before the bell, bringing JEAN-GUY and REMY, both signed NATIVE on the first win (Remy's string shows on quest:cold_storage alone)
godot --headless --path engine/greybox --quit-after 16000 -- demo recruit boss=2 flag=blessing:moreau   # a house's blessing: both Moreaus approached, House Iron (E08) and VERA IRON up, House Tremblay face down across E05
godot --headless --path engine/greybox --quit-after 60000 -- demo recruit boss=1   # two duals against Yuri's squad if the profile wins both: YURI and SAM arrive UNSANCTIONED, the second win is the home dual the Kowalchuk patriarch attends
godot --headless --path engine/greybox --quit-after 16000 -- demo recruit boss=17 flag=blessing:reyes,feud_resolved:kowalchuk+reyes,quest:okafor_table,quest:open_ledger   # the live graph: E04 repaid, E13 added, E11 refused, House Reyes blessed by its flag
godot --headless --path engine/greybox --quit-after 16000 -- demo formation boss=1 wall=MARCUS:1:1,PRIYA:1:2 order=GYM_WALL   # the Gym Wall: MARCUS and PRIYA pinned down the wall, its suggestion R1 RORY, R2 MARCUS, R3 PRIYA sealed; the wall's rows, lines and decorations at DUAL OVER
godot --headless --path engine/greybox --quit-after 16000 -- demo formation boss=7 format=sanctioned wall=SAM:1:1 order=GYM_WALL   # the sanctioned five with SAM pinned under RORY: MARCUS-PRIYA-JUNE-RORY-SAM
godot --headless --path engine/greybox -s tools/drive_duo.gd -- kizuna=RORY+PRIYA:90   # the Duo on the real scene: the matchup card's DUO line in R2, Whiteout armed with RORY's afterimage gilded and the DUO toast, none in R1 or R3; R3 out CHARGED with the relay's RELAY line (6 checks)
godot --headless --path engine/greybox --quit-after 16000 -- demo duo boss=1 kizuna=RORY+PRIYA:90   # the demo aid: R2 PRIYA with a full bar, the matchup card's DUO line, the profile casting Whiteout, RORY's afterimage gilded
godot --headless --path engine/greybox --quit-after 16000 -- demo formation boss=6 format=sanctioned order=RELAY_LADDER kizuna=RORY+SAM:90,SAM+MARCUS:90   # the Relay Ladder's two Duo relays: RORY -> SAM (Aurora Chain - RORY & SAM), SAM -> MARCUS (Coast to Coast - MARCUS & SAM)
python3 tools/hissatsu_audit.py   # the library's gate, the Duo variants among it
godot --headless --path engine/greybox -s tools/drive_zone.gd -- level=RORY:20 flag=story:provincials_semifinal   # the Zone on the real scene: H + Y refused with an empty bar, the SURGE with a full one, the Zone's line, the Comedown's (6 checks)
godot --headless --path engine/greybox --quit-after 16000 -- demo zone boss=1 level=RORY:20,PRIYA:20,MARCUS:20,JUNE:20 flag=story:provincials_semifinal   # the aid SURGEs whoever is out with the Zone unlocked, once each: the Zone, the Comedown
godot --headless --path engine/greybox -s tools/drive_manifest.gd -- level=RORY:20 flag=story:provincials_semifinal,story:manifest:RORY   # the Manifest on the real scene: the SURGE's toast with the manifest and its meter, the MANIFEST line, the figure behind him, a straight's echo, the Comedown (6 checks)
godot --headless --path engine/greybox --quit-after 16000 -- demo manifest boss=1 level=RORY:20,PRIYA:20,MARCUS:20,JUNE:20 flag=story:provincials_semifinal,story:manifest:RORY,story:manifest:PRIYA   # the aid SURGEs whoever can: RORY and PRIYA manifest, the others Zone; the soak line lists the transforms and the echoes
```
